164				Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(0, 0, cachedDrawArea.Height, cachedDrawArea.Width);
165				Vector4 destination = new Vector4(cachedDrawArea.X + cachedDrawArea.Width, cachedDrawArea.Y, cachedDrawArea.Height, cachedDrawArea.Width);
166				destination *= 16f;
167				destination.X -= vector.X;
168				destination.Y -= vector.Y;
169				destination *= 0.25f;
170				destination.X += vector2.X;
171				destination.Y += vector2.Y;
172				graphicsDevice.SamplerStates[0] = SamplerState.PointClamp;
173				tileBatch.Draw(_viscosityMaskChain[_activeViscosityMask], destination, rectangle, new VertexColors(Color.White), rectangle.Size(), SpriteEffects.FlipHorizontally, -MathF.PI / 2f);
174				tileBatch.End();
175				_activeViscosityMask++;
176				_activeViscosityMask %= _viscosityMaskChain.Length;
177			}
178			graphicsDevice.SetRenderTarget(null);
179		}

181		private void DrawWaves()
182		{
183			Vector2 screenPosition = Main.screenPosition;
184			Vector2 vector = (Main.drawToScreen ? Vector2.Zero : new Vector2(Main.offScreenRange, Main.offScreenRange));
185			Vector2 vector2 = -_lastDistortionDrawOffset / 0.25f + vector;
186			TileBatch tileBatch = Main.tileBatch;
187			_ = Main.instance.GraphicsDevice;
188			Vector2 dimensions = new Vector2(Main.screenWidth, Main.screenHeight);
189			Vector2 vector3 = new Vector2(16f, 16f);
190			tileBatch.Begin();
191			GameShaders.Misc["WaterDistortionObject"].Apply();
192			if (_useNPCWaves)
193			{
194				for (int i = 0; i < Main.maxNPCs; i++)
195				{
196					if (Main.npc[i] == null || !Main.npc[i].active || (!Main.npc[i].wet && Main.npc[i].wetCount == 0) || !Collision.CheckAABBvAABBCollision(screenPosition, dimensions, Main.npc[i].position - vector3, Main.npc[i].Size + vector3))
197					{
198						continue;
199					}
200					NPC nPC = Main.npc[i];
201					Vector2 vector4 = nPC.Center - vector2;
202					Vector2 vector5 = nPC.velocity.RotatedBy(0f - nPC.rotation) / new Vector2(nPC.height, nPC.width);
203					float num = vector5.LengthSquared();
204					num = num * 0.3f + 0.7f * num * (1024f / (float)(nPC.height * nPC.width));
205					num = Math.Min(num, 0.08f);
206					num += (nPC.velocity - nPC.oldVelocity).Length() * 0.5f;
207					vector5.Normalize();
208					Vector2 velocity = nPC.velocity;
209					velocity.Normalize();
210					vector4 -= velocity * 10f;
211					if (!_useViscosityFilter && (nPC.honeyWet || nPC.lavaWet))
212					{
213						num *= 0.3f;
214					}
215					if (nPC.wet)
216					{
217						tileBatch.Draw(TextureAssets.MagicPixel.Value, new Vector4(vector4.X, vector4.Y, (float)nPC.width * 2f, (float)nPC.height * 2f) * 0.25f, null, new VertexColors(new Color(vector5.X * 0.5f + 0.5f, vector5.Y * 0.5f + 0.5f, 0.5f * num)), new Vector2((float)TextureAssets.MagicPixel.Width() / 2f, (float)TextureAssets.MagicPixel.Height() / 2f), SpriteEffects.None, nPC.rotation);
218					}
219					if (nPC.wetCount != 0)
220					{
221						num = nPC.velocity.Length();
222						num = 0.195f * (float)Math.Sqrt(num);
223						float num2 = 5f;
224						if (!nPC.wet)
225						{
226							num2 = -20f;
227						}
228						QueueRipple(nPC.Center + velocity * num2, new Color(0.5f, (nPC.wet ? num : (0f - num)) * 0.5f + 0.5f, 0f, 1f) * 0.5f, new Vector2(nPC.width, (float)nPC.height * ((float)(int)nPC.wetCount / 9f)) * MathHelper.Clamp(num * 10f, 0f, 1f), RippleShape.Circle);
229					}
230				}
231			}
232			if (_usePlayerWaves)
233			{
234				for (int j = 0; j < 255; j++)
235				{
236					if (Main.player[j] == null || !Main.player[j].active || (!Main.player[j].wet && Main.player[j].wetCount == 0) || !Collision.CheckAABBvAABBCollision(screenPosition, dimensions, Main.player[j].position - vector3, Main.player[j].Size + vector3))

335	
336		private void PreDraw(GameTime gameTime)
337		{
338			ValidateRenderTargets();
339			if (!_usingRenderTargets || !Main.IsGraphicsDeviceAvailable)
340			{
341				return;
342			}
343			if (_useProjectileWaves || _useRippleWaves || _useCustomWaves || _usePlayerWaves)
344			{
345				for (int i = 0; i < Math.Min(_queuedSteps, 2); i++)
346				{
347					StepLiquids();
348				}
349			}
350			else if (_isWaveBufferDirty || _clearNextFrame)
351			{
352				GraphicsDevice graphicsDevice = Main.instance.GraphicsDevice;
353				graphicsDevice.SetRenderTarget(_distortionTarget);
354				graphicsDevice.Clear(new Color(0.5f, 0.5f, 0f, 1f));
355				_clearNextFrame = false;
356				_isWaveBufferDirty = false;
357				graphicsDevice.SetRenderTarget(null);
358			}
359			_queuedSteps = 0;
360		}
361	
362		public override void Apply()
363		{
364			if (_usingRenderTargets && Main.IsGraphicsDeviceAvailable)
365			{
366				UseProgress(_progress);
367				Main.graphics.GraphicsDevice.SamplerStates[0] = SamplerState.PointClamp;
368				Vector2 vector = new Vector2(Main.offScreenRange, Main.offScreenRange);
369				Vector2 unscaledScreenPosition = ScreenShaderData.UnscaledScreenPosition;
370				Vector2 vector2 = (Main.drawToScreen ? Vector2.Zero : vector) - unscaledScreenPosition;
371				UseImage(DrawRipples ? _distortionTarget : _noDistortionTexture, 1);
372				UseImage(Main.waterTarget.Texture, 2, SamplerState.PointClamp);
373				UseTargetPosition(unscaledScreenPosition + vector - Main.waterTarget.Position);
374				UseImageOffset(-(vector2 - _lastDistortionDrawOffset / 0.25f));
375				base.Apply();
376			}
377		}
378	
379		private void ValidateRenderTargets()
380		{
381			int backBufferWidth = Main.instance.GraphicsDevice.PresentationParameters.BackBufferWidth;
382			int backBufferHeight = Main.instance.GraphicsDevice.PresentationParameters.BackBufferHeight;
383			bool flag = !Main.drawToScreen;
384			if (_usingRenderTargets && !flag)
385			{
386				ReleaseRenderTargets();
387			}
388			else if (!_usingRenderTargets && flag)
389			{
390				InitRenderTargets(backBufferWidth, backBufferHeight);
391			}
392			else if (_usingRenderTargets && flag && (_distortionTarget.IsContentLost || _distortionTargetSwap.IsContentLost))
393			{
394				_clearNextFrame = true;
395			}
396		}
397	
398		private void InitRenderTargets(int width, int height)
399		{
400			_lastScreenWidth = width;
401			_lastScreenHeight = height;
402			width = (int)((float)width * 0.25f);
403			height = (int)((float)height * 0.25f);
404			try
405			{
406				_noDistortionTexture = new Texture2D(Main.instance.GraphicsDevice, 1, 1, mipMap: false, SurfaceFormat.Color);
407				_noDistortionTexture.SetData(new Color[1]
408				{
409					new Color(0.5f, 0.5f, 0f, 1f)
410				});
411				_distortionTarget = new RenderTarget2D(Main.instance.GraphicsDevice, width, height, mipMap: false, SurfaceFormat.Color, DepthFormat.None, 0, RenderTargetUsage.PreserveContents);
412				_distortionTargetSwap = new RenderTarget2D(Main.instance.GraphicsDevice, width, height, mipMap: false, SurfaceFormat.Color, DepthFormat.None, 0, RenderTargetUsage.PreserveContents);
413				_usingRenderTargets = true;
414				_clearNextFrame = true;
415			}
416			catch (Exception ex)
417			{
418				Lighting.Mode = LightMode.Retro;
419				_usingRenderTargets = false;
420				Console.WriteLine("Failed to create water distortion render targets. " + ex);
421			}
422		}
423	
424		private void ReleaseRenderTargets()
425		{
426			try
427			{
428				if (_distortionTarget != null)
429				{
430					_distortionTarget.Dispose();
431				}
432				if (_distortionTargetSwap != null)
433				{
434					_distortionTargetSwap.Dispose();
435				}
436			}
437			catch (Exception ex)
438			{
439				Console.WriteLine("Error disposing of water distortion render targets. " + ex);
440			}
441			_distortionTarget = null;
442			_distortionTargetSwap = null;
443			_usingRenderTargets = false;
444		}
445	
446		public void QueueRipple(Vector2 position, float strength = 1f, RippleShape shape = RippleShape.Square, float rotation = 0f)
447		{
448			float g = strength * 0.5f + 0.5f;
449			float num = Math.Min(Math.Abs(strength), 1f);
450			QueueRipple(position, new Color(0.5f, g, 0f, 1f) * num, new Vector2(4f * Math.Max(Math.Abs(strength), 1f)), shape, rotation);
451		}
452	
453		public void QueueRipple(Vector2 position, float strength, Vector2 size, RippleShape shape = RippleShape.Square, float rotation = 0f)
454		{
455			float g = strength * 0.5f + 0.5f;
456			float num = Math.Min(Math.Abs(strength), 1f);
457			QueueRipple(position, new Color(0.5f, g, 0f, 1f) * num, size, shape, rotation);
458		}
459	
460		public void QueueRipple(Vector2 position, Color waveData, Vector2 size, RippleShape shape = RippleShape.Square, float rotation = 0f)
461		{
462			if (!_useRippleWaves || Main.drawToScreen)
463			{
464				_rippleQueueCount = 0;
465			}
466			else if (_rippleQueueCount < _rippleQueue.Length)
467			{
468				_rippleQueue[_rippleQueueCount++] = new Ripple(position, waveData, size, shape, rotation);
469			}
470		}
471	}

622		public unsafe void DrawNormalLiquids(SpriteBatch spriteBatch, Vector2 drawOffset, int waterStyle, float globalAlpha, bool isBackgroundDraw, bool waterOnly)
623		{
624			Rectangle drawArea = _drawArea;
625			Main.tileBatch.Restart();
626			fixed (LiquidDrawCache* ptr = &_drawCache[0])
627			{
628				LiquidDrawCache* ptr2 = ptr;
629				for (int i = drawArea.X; i < drawArea.X + drawArea.Width; i++)
630				{
631					for (int j = drawArea.Y; j < drawArea.Y + drawArea.Height; j++)
632					{
633						if (ptr2->IsVisible && (!waterOnly || ptr2->Type == 0))
634						{
635							Main.tileBatch.SetLayer(0u, 0);
636							Rectangle sourceRectangle = ptr2->SourceRectangle;
637							if (ptr2->IsSurfaceLiquid)
638							{
639								sourceRectangle.Y = 1280;
640							}
641							else if (sourceRectangle.X == 16)
642							{
643								sourceRectangle.Y += _waterfallAnimationFrame * 80;
644							}
645							else
646							{
647								sourceRectangle.Y += _animationFrame * 80;
648							}
649							Vector2 liquidOffset = ptr2->LiquidOffset;
650							float num = ptr2->Opacity * (isBackgroundDraw ? 1f : DEFAULT_OPACITY[ptr2->Type]);
651							int num2 = ptr2->Type;
652							switch (num2)
653							{
654							case 0:
655								num2 = waterStyle;
656								num *= globalAlpha;
657								break;
658							case 1:
659								num *= Main.player[Main.myPlayer].lavaOpacity;
660								break;
661							case 2:
662								num2 = 11;
663								break;
664							}
665							num = Math.Min(1f, num);
666							Lighting.GetCornerColors(i, j, out var vertices);
667							vertices.BottomLeftColor *= num;
668							vertices.BottomRightColor *= num;
669							vertices.TopLeftColor *= num;
670							vertices.TopRightColor *= num;
671							Main.DrawTileInWater(drawOffset, i, j);
672							Main.tileBatch.Draw(_liquidTextures[num2].Value, new Vector2(i << 4, j << 4) + drawOffset + liquidOffset, sourceRectangle, vertices, Vector2.Zero, 1f, SpriteEffects.None);
673						}
674						ptr2++;
675					}
676				}
677			}
678			int value = Main.tileBatch.End();
679			(isBackgroundDraw ? TimeLogger.LiquidBackgroundDrawCalls : TimeLogger.LiquidDrawCalls).Add(value);
680		}

682		public unsafe void DrawShimmer(SpriteBatch spriteBatch, Vector2 drawOffset, bool isBackgroundDraw)
683		{
684			Rectangle drawArea = _drawArea;
685			Main.tileBatch.Restart();
686			fixed (SpecialLiquidDrawCache* ptr = &_drawCacheForShimmer[0])
687			{
688				SpecialLiquidDrawCache* ptr2 = ptr;
689				int num = _drawCacheForShimmer.Length;
690				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
691				{
692					if (!ptr2->IsVisible)
693					{
694						break;
695					}
696					Main.tileBatch.SetLayer(0u, 0);
697					Rectangle sourceRectangle = ptr2->SourceRectangle;
698					if (ptr2->IsSurfaceLiquid)
699					{
700						sourceRectangle.Y = 1280;
701					}
702					else
703					{
704						sourceRectangle.Y += _animationFrame * 80;
705					}
706					Vector2 liquidOffset = ptr2->LiquidOffset;
707					float val = ptr2->Opacity * (isBackgroundDraw ? 1f : 0.75f);
708					int num2 = 14;
709					val = Math.Min(1f, val);
710					int num3 = ptr2->X + drawArea.X - 2;
711					int num4 = ptr2->Y + drawArea.Y - 2;
712					Lighting.GetCornerColors(num3, num4, out var vertices);
713					SetShimmerVertexColors(ref vertices, val, num3, num4);
714					Main.DrawTileInWater(drawOffset, num3, num4);
715					Main.tileBatch.Draw(_liquidTextures[num2].Value, new Vector2(num3 << 4, num4 << 4) + drawOffset + liquidOffset, sourceRectangle, vertices, Vector2.Zero, 1f, SpriteEffects.None);
716					sourceRectangle = ptr2->SourceRectangle;
717					bool flag = sourceRectangle.X != 16 || sourceRectangle.Y % 80 != 48;
718					if (flag || (num3 + num4) % 2 == 0)
719					{
720						sourceRectangle.X += 48;
721						sourceRectangle.Y += 80 * GetShimmerFrame(flag, num3, num4);
722						SetShimmerVertexColors_Sparkle(ref vertices, ptr2->Opacity, num3, num4, flag);
723						Main.tileBatch.Draw(_liquidTextures[num2].Value, new Vector2(num3 << 4, num4 << 4) + drawOffset + liquidOffset, sourceRectangle, vertices, Vector2.Zero, 1f, SpriteEffects.None);
724					}
725					ptr2++;
726				}
727			}
728			int value = Main.tileBatch.End();
729			(isBackgroundDraw ? TimeLogger.LiquidBackgroundDrawCalls : TimeLogger.LiquidDrawCalls).Add(value);
730		}

732		public static VertexColors SetShimmerVertexColors_Sparkle(ref VertexColors colors, float opacity, int x, int y, bool top)
733		{
734			colors.BottomLeftColor = GetShimmerGlitterColor(top, x, y + 1);
735			colors.BottomRightColor = GetShimmerGlitterColor(top, x + 1, y + 1);
736			colors.TopLeftColor = GetShimmerGlitterColor(top, x, y);
737			colors.TopRightColor = GetShimmerGlitterColor(top, x + 1, y);
738			colors.BottomLeftColor *= opacity;
739			colors.BottomRightColor *= opacity;
740			colors.TopLeftColor *= opacity;
741			colors.TopRightColor *= opacity;
742			return colors;
743		}
744	
745		public static void SetShimmerVertexColors(ref VertexColors colors, float opacity, int x, int y)
746		{
747			colors.BottomLeftColor = Color.White;
748			colors.BottomRightColor = Color.White;
749			colors.TopLeftColor = Color.White;
750			colors.TopRightColor = Color.White;
751			colors.BottomLeftColor *= opacity;
752			colors.BottomRightColor *= opacity;
753			colors.TopLeftColor *= opacity;
754			colors.TopRightColor *= opacity;
755			colors.BottomLeftColor = new Color(colors.BottomLeftColor.ToVector4() * GetShimmerBaseColor(x, y + 1));
756			colors.BottomRightColor = new Color(colors.BottomRightColor.ToVector4() * GetShimmerBaseColor(x + 1, y + 1));
757			colors.TopLeftColor = new Color(colors.TopLeftColor.ToVector4() * GetShimmerBaseColor(x, y));
758			colors.TopRightColor = new Color(colors.TopRightColor.ToVector4() * GetShimmerBaseColor(x + 1, y));
759		}
760	
761		public static float GetShimmerWave(ref float worldPositionX, ref float worldPositionY)
762		{
763			return (float)Math.Sin(((double)((worldPositionX + worldPositionY / 6f) / 10f) - Main.timeForVisualEffects / 360.0) * 6.2831854820251465);
764		}
765	
766		public static Color GetShimmerGlitterColor(bool top, float worldPositionX, float worldPositionY)
767		{
768			Color color = Main.hslToRgb((float)(((double)(worldPositionX + worldPositionY / 6f) + Main.timeForVisualEffects / 30.0) / 6.0) % 1f, 1f, 0.5f);
769			color.A = 0;
770			return new Color(color.ToVector4() * GetShimmerGlitterOpacity(top, worldPositionX, worldPositionY));
771		}
772	
773		public static float GetShimmerGlitterOpacity(bool top, float worldPositionX, float worldPositionY)
774		{
775			if (top)
776			{
777				return 0.5f;
778			}
779			float num = Utils.Remap((float)Math.Sin(((double)((worldPositionX + worldPositionY / 6f) / 10f) - Main.timeForVisualEffects / 360.0) * 6.2831854820251465), -0.5f, 1f, 0f, 0.35f);
780			float num2 = (float)Math.Sin((double)((float)SimpleWhiteNoise((uint)worldPositionX, (uint)worldPositionY) / 10f) + Main.timeForVisualEffects / 180.0);
781			return Utils.Remap(num * num2, 0f, 0.5f, 0f, 1f);
782		}
783	
784		private static uint SimpleWhiteNoise(uint x, uint y)
785		{
786			x = 36469 * (x & 0xFFFF) + (x >> 16);
787			y = 18012 * (y & 0xFFFF) + (y >> 16);
788			return (x << 16) + y;
789		}
790	
791		public int GetShimmerFrame(bool top, float worldPositionX, float worldPositionY)
792		{
793			worldPositionX += 0.5f;
794			worldPositionY += 0.5f;
795			double num = (double)((worldPositionX + worldPositionY / 6f) / 10f) - Main.timeForVisualEffects / 360.0;
796			if (!top)
797			{
798				num += (double)(worldPositionX + worldPositionY);
799			}
800			return ((int)num % 16 + 16) % 16;
801		}
802	
803		public static Vector4 GetShimmerBaseColor(float worldPositionX, float worldPositionY)
804		{
805			float shimmerWave = GetShimmerWave(ref worldPositionX, ref worldPositionY);
806			return Vector4.Lerp(new Vector4(0.64705884f, 26f / 51f, 14f / 15f, 1f), new Vector4(41f / 51f, 41f / 51f, 1f, 1f), 0.1f + shimmerWave * 0.4f);
807		}
808

810		{
811			x -= _drawArea.X;
812			y -= _drawArea.Y;
813			int num = x * _drawArea.Height + y;
814			if (num >= 0 && num < _drawCache.Length)
815			{
816				if (_drawCache[num].IsVisible)
817				{
818					return !_drawCache[num].IsSurfaceLiquid;
819				}
820				return false;
821			}
822			return true;
823		}

826		{

830			{

835			{
836				return 0f;
837			}
838			return _cache[num].VisibleLiquidLevel;
839		}

	private void DrawPaladinsShield()
	{
		Player localPlayer = LocalPlayer;
		if (localPlayer.dead)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (localPlayer.hasPaladinShield)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < 255; i++)
			{
				if (i != myPlayer)
				{
					Player player = Main.player[i];
					if (player.active && !player.dead && localPlayer.CanDefendWithPaladinsShield(player.team))
					{
						DrawPaladinsShieldBoundary(localPlayer.MountedCenter, player.MountedCenter);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (!localPlayer.defendedByPaladin)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < 255; j++)
		{
			if (j != myPlayer)
			{
				Player player2 = Main.player[j];
				if (player2.CanDefendWithPaladinsShield(localPlayer.team))
				{
					DrawPaladinsShieldBoundary(player2.MountedCenter, localPlayer.MountedCenter);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void DrawPaladinsShieldBoundary(Vector2 from, Vector2 to)
	{
		float num = Vector2.Distance(from, to);
		float paladinsShieldRange = Player.PaladinsShieldRange;
		float fromValue = Math.Abs(num - paladinsShieldRange);
		bool flag = num < paladinsShieldRange;
		float num2 = 1f;

	private void RenderToTargets()
	{
		if (!render)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (renderNow || renderCount == 1)
		{
			waterTarget.UpdateContent(RenderWater);
		}
		else if (renderNow || renderCount == 2)
		{
			backWaterTarget.UpdateContent(RenderBackgroundWater);
			backgroundTargetSwap.UpdateContent(RenderBackground);
			backgroundTargetSwapPending = true;
		}
		else if (renderNow || renderCount == 3)
		{
			wallTarget.UpdateContent(RenderWallsAndBlacks);
			tileTarget.UpdateContent(RenderTiles);
			tile2Target.UpdateContent(RenderTiles2);
		}
		if (tileTarget.IsPartiallyOffscreen)
		{
			tileTarget.UpdateContent(RenderTiles);
		}
		if (tile2Target.IsPartiallyOffscreen)
		{
			tile2Target.UpdateContent(RenderTiles2);
		}
		if (backWaterTarget.IsPartiallyOffscreen)
		{
			backWaterTarget.UpdateContent(RenderBackgroundWater);
		}
		if (backgroundTarget.IsPartiallyOffscreen)
		{
			ApplyPendingBackgroundTargetSwap();
			if (backgroundTarget.IsPartiallyOffscreen)
			{
				backgroundTarget.UpdateContent(RenderBackground);
			}
		}
		if (wallTarget.IsPartiallyOffscreen)
		{
			wallTarget.UpdateContent(RenderWallsAndBlacks);
		}
		if (waterTarget.IsPartiallyOffscreen)
		{
			waterTarget.UpdateContent(RenderWater);
		}
	}

	protected void RenderTiles()
	{
		TimeLogger.StartTimestamp fromTimestamp = TimeLogger.Start();
		try
		{
			if (!DebugOptions.hideTiles)
			{
				DrawTiles(solidLayer: true, intoRenderTargets: true);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception e)
		{
			if (!ignoreErrors)
			{
				throw;
			}
			TimeLogger.DrawException(e);
		}
		TimeLogger.RenderSolidTiles.AddTime(fromTimestamp);
	}

2. The gate semantics per tier (which wave sources are active).
3. The scene intensity logic (SceneState.cs:129-173: wind/rain/depth-based intensity → UseIntensity; _progress += elapsed * Intensity * 0.75).
4. The wave source injection math (NPC/player/projectile wet displacement + ripples, ripple queue 200 cap, ripple color/size formulas, QueueRipple signatures) — all in WaterShaderData.cs DrawWaves (lines 181-334) + Gore.cs:986 + Projectile.cs:30674 + FakeFishParticle.cs:184.
5. The distortion buffer structure: 0.25 scale, ping-pong, (0.5,0.5) neutral, (0.5,0.5,0.5f) clear for swap, scrolling by draw offset, max 2 steps per frame.
6. Viscosity mask pipeline (quality>=3): LiquidRenderer wave mask (R=G=strength>>1, B=viscosity, A=strength) drawn rotated into the distortion target.

For the propagation itself (WaterProcessor pixel shader), I must implement a wave-equation simulation on CPU. The standard algorithm that this structure implies (ping-pong buffers, neighbor sampling, damping, viscosity mask) is the classic "water ripple" algorithm:

newHeight = (sum of 4 neighbors)/2 - previousHeight, then damped:
h_new(x,y) = ((h(x-1,y) + h(x+1,y) + h(x,y-1) + h(x,y+1)) / 2) - h_prev(x,y)
h_new *= damping

This is THE classic algorithm (Hugo Elias water ripple). The two buffers (distortionTarget holds current+accumulated injections, distortionTargetSwap holds previous state) map exactly: the WaterProcessor pass reads the previous frame buffer and writes the propagated result.

Actually, thinking about it more: In StepLiquids:
1. _distortionTarget (accumulates fresh injections via DrawWaves, preserve contents)

3. Draw _distortionTarget onto swap at the scroll offset delta, WITH WaterProcessor pixel shader applied → this pass reads _distortionTarget (sampler 0) and computes the wave equation output into swap.
4. Then viscosity mask drawn into swap too (quality 3).
5. Swap: _distortionTarget ↔ _distortionTargetSwap. Now _distortionTarget = propagated field, _distortionTargetSwap = the pre-propagation field (used as "previous height" next frame? No wait...).

Hmm, actually the ping-pong here: next frame, DrawWaves draws ON TOP of the propagated field (PreserveContents), then the swap buffer gets cleared and receives the propagated version. The ripple color format is Color(0.5, strength*0.5+0.5, 0) — R=0.5 (neutral X displacement), G encodes height (0.5=neutral), B=0 for ripples but 0.5 when clear.

So the buffer channels: R = X-gradient/velocity, G = Y-gradient/height?, B = ? The WaterProcessor likely does: gradient computation + wave propagation in a single pass using neighboring texels of the accumulated field.

46	function shimmerWhiteNoise(x: number, y: number): number {

48	  ux = (36469 * (ux & 0xffff) + (ux >>> 16)) >>> 0;
49	  uy = (18012 * (uy & 0xffff) + (uy >>> 16)) >>> 0;
50	  return (((ux << 16) >>> 0) + uy) >>> 0;
51	}
52	/** Utils.Remap（单调区间重映射） */
53	function remap(v: number, a: number, b: number, c: number, d: number): number {
54	  if (b === a) return c;
55	  const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (v - a) / (b - a)));
56	  return c + (d - c) * t;

83	 * Canvas2D 无顶点色，故把 16×16 tile 分 2×2 子块（8×8），每子块取四角双线性
84	 * 插值在其中心位置的色，以 multiply 叠在已画的 water_14 上（=纹理×色，同原版 modulate）。

87	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, x: number, y: number,

91	  const c01 = shimmerBaseColorF(x, y + 1, tVis), c11 = shimmerBaseColorF(x + 1, y + 1, tVis);
92	  ctx.save();
93	  // 原版 SetShimmerVertexColors 的乘法是【无条件 modulate】（纹理×顶点色），不带
94	  // 透明 pass 的 0.75 衰减——若沿用调用方残留的 globalAlpha，白色基底（表面格
95	  // Y=1280 整块纯白）只会被"部分染色"，表层色带被冲淡成灰白。故强制 1.0 全乘。
96	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
97	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
98	  const subW = Math.ceil(w / 2), subH = Math.ceil(h / 2);
99	  for (let by = 0; by < 2; by++) {
100	    for (let bx = 0; bx < 2; bx++) {
101	      const bw = Math.min(subW, w - bx * subW), bh = Math.min(subH, h - by * subH);
102	      if (bw <= 0 || bh <= 0) continue;
103	      // 子块中心在 tile 内的归一化位置（dstX 相对 x*16 有壁值裁剪偏移）→ 四角双线性插值
104	      const u = (dstX + bx * subW + bw / 2 - x * 16) / 16;
105	      const v = (dstY + by * subH + bh / 2 - y * 16) / 16;
106	      const ch = (i: number) => c00[i] * (1 - u) * (1 - v) + c10[i] * u * (1 - v)
107	        + c01[i] * (1 - u) * v + c11[i] * u * v;
108	      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${Math.round(ch(0))},${Math.round(ch(1))},${Math.round(ch(2))})`;
109	      ctx.fillRect(dstX + bx * subW, dstY + by * subH, bw, bh);
110	    }
111	  }
112	  ctx.restore();
113	}

116	function hue2rgb(v1: number, v2: number, vH: number): number {
117	  if (vH < 0) vH += 1;
118	  if (vH > 1) vH -= 1;
119	  if (6 * vH < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * 6 * vH;
120	  if (2 * vH < 1) return v2;
121	  if (3 * vH < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * ((2 / 3) - vH) * 6;

125	function hslToRgb(hue: number, sat: number, lum: number): [number, number, number] {
126	  if (sat === 0) return [lum, lum, lum];
127	  const v2 = lum < 0.5 ? lum * (1 + sat) : lum + sat - lum * sat;
128	  const v1 = 2 * lum - v2;
129	  return [hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue + 1 / 3), hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue), hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue - 1 / 3)];

139	// 约 2/3 像素是 alpha=255 的纯黑。原版 XNA 侧该带由 SetShimmerVertexColors_Sparkle
140	// 的顶点色（RGB=彩虹、A=glitter 强度）整体门控呈现；Canvas2D 的 multiply/直画
141	// 序列没有这个门控，黑底被当成实心像素 → 用户所见的"黑色背景"。

146	const SPARKLE_HUE_STEPS = 16;

149	/** sparkle 变体逐像素重造：灰度亮度 → alpha（黑底归零），RGB → HSL 彩虹色。 */
150	export function tintSparkleBand(data: Uint8ClampedArray, r: number, g: number, b: number): void {
151	  for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += 4) {
152	    // 灰度带（三通道相等）→ 亮度即灰度值；保留亚像素抗锯齿渐变（闪纹边缘软过渡）

171	  const [r, g, b] = hslToRgb(hueIdx / SPARKLE_HUE_STEPS, 1, 0.5);
172	  const img = cc.getImageData(0, 0, bandW, tex.height);
173	  tintSparkleBand(img.data, Math.round(r * 255), Math.round(g * 255), Math.round(b * 255));
174	  cc.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
175	  _sparkleTintCache.set(hueIdx, c);
176	  return c;
177	}
178	
179	// ---- 类型数组池(2026-08 审计 G1):此前每 pass 分配 24 个数组 ×2 pass/帧
180	// ≈ 744KB/帧 ≈ 45MB/s 的 GC 压力。容量只增不减;每调用前 fill(0) 保持与
181	// "新分配数组"完全相同的语义(未写格一律 0) ----
182	let _liqCap = 0;
183	let _level: Float32Array, _visLevel: Float32Array, _opacity: Float32Array;
184	let _isSolidA: Uint8Array, _hasLiquidA: Uint8Array, _hasWallA: Uint8Array;
185	let _hasVisA: Uint8Array, _typeA: Uint8Array, _visTypeA: Uint8Array;
186	let _lW: Float32Array, _rW: Float32Array, _bW: Float32Array, _tW: Float32Array;
187	let _vlW: Float32Array, _vrW: Float32Array, _vbW: Float32Array, _vtW: Float32Array;
188	let _hasLE: Uint8Array, _hasRE: Uint8Array, _hasTE: Uint8Array, _hasBE: Uint8Array;
189	let _isHalfA: Uint8Array;
190	let _fx: Int16Array, _fy: Int16Array;
191	
192	// ---- 调试快照（F5 DebugReport render.subsystems.liquids 消费；模块级因本渲染器是自由函数）----
193	export interface LiquidDebugState {
194	  /** 最近一次 draw 的调用序号（双 pass 各 +1 → 每帧 +2） */
195	  calls: number;
196	  /** 最近一次 draw 时刻 ms */
197	  lastMs: number;
198	  /** 群系水色 id（CalculateWaterStyle 结果；水贴图 Misc_water_<n>.png 的 n） */
199	  waterStyle: number;
200	  /** 当前群系水色解析出的水体贴图名 */
201	  waterSheet: string;
202	  /** 最近一次 draw 是否背景 pass */
203	  isBackground: boolean;
204	  /** 动画参数（1456 双帧机制） */
205	  animFrame: number;
206	  waterfallFrame: number;
207	  windSpeed: number;
208	  /** 最近一次 draw 的可见 tile 窗口（padding 前） */
209	  window: [number, number, number, number];
210	  /** 最近一次 draw 命中的液体类型表（原版 LiquidType 键 → 贴图是否就绪） */
211	  sheetsReady: Array<[number, boolean]>;
212	  atlasReady: boolean;
213	}
214	const _liqDebug: LiquidDebugState = {
215	  calls: 0, lastMs: 0, waterStyle: 0, waterSheet: '', isBackground: false,
216	  animFrame: 0, waterfallFrame: 0, windSpeed: 0, window: [0, 0, 0, 0],
217	  sheetsReady: [], atlasReady: false,
218	};
219	
220	/** 液体渲染调试快照（只读消费；DebugReport render.subsystems.liquids） */
221	export function liquidDebugState(): LiquidDebugState {
222	  return _liqDebug;
223	}
224	
225	export function drawVanillaLiquids(
226	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas | null,
227	  st: TileStore, groundLevel: number,
228	  tx0: number, ty0: number, tx1: number, ty1: number,
229	  nowMs: number, isBackground = false, windSpeed = 0, waterStyle = 0,
230	): void {
231	  if (!atlas) { _liqDebug.atlasReady = false; return; }
232	  const PAD = 2;
233	  const px0 = tx0 - PAD, py0 = ty0 - PAD;
234	  const pw = tx1 - tx0 + 1 + PAD * 2, ph = ty1 - ty0 + 1 + PAD * 2;
235	  const n = pw * ph;
236	  if (n > _liqCap) {
237	    // 只增不减(缩放变化按最大视图分配一次)
238	    _liqCap = n;
239	    _level = new Float32Array(n); _visLevel = new Float32Array(n); _opacity = new Float32Array(n);
240	    _isSolidA = new Uint8Array(n); _hasLiquidA = new Uint8Array(n); _hasWallA = new Uint8Array(n);
241	    _hasVisA = new Uint8Array(n); _typeA = new Uint8Array(n); _visTypeA = new Uint8Array(n);
242	    _lW = new Float32Array(n); _rW = new Float32Array(n); _bW = new Float32Array(n); _tW = new Float32Array(n);
243	    _vlW = new Float32Array(n); _vrW = new Float32Array(n); _vbW = new Float32Array(n); _vtW = new Float32Array(n);
244	    _hasLE = new Uint8Array(n); _hasRE = new Uint8Array(n); _hasTE = new Uint8Array(n); _hasBE = new Uint8Array(n);
245	    _isHalfA = new Uint8Array(n);
246	    _fx = new Int16Array(n); _fy = new Int16Array(n);
247	  }
248	  // 归零(等价新分配数组;未写格语义为 0)。
249	  // 例外:opacity 原为 .fill(1)——P3 只写到 ph-10 行,底缘 8 行依赖初始 1,
250	  // 必须保持 fill(1) 否则视口底缘水体透明度归零(渲染结果变化)
251	  _level.fill(0); _visLevel.fill(0); _opacity.fill(1);
252	  _isSolidA.fill(0); _hasLiquidA.fill(0); _hasWallA.fill(0);
253	  _hasVisA.fill(0); _typeA.fill(0); _visTypeA.fill(0);
254	  _lW.fill(0); _rW.fill(0); _bW.fill(0); _tW.fill(0);
255	  _vlW.fill(0); _vrW.fill(0); _vbW.fill(0); _vtW.fill(0);
256	  _hasLE.fill(0); _hasRE.fill(0); _hasTE.fill(0); _hasBE.fill(0);
257	  _isHalfA.fill(0);
258	  _fx.fill(0); _fy.fill(0);
259	  const level = _level, visLevel = _visLevel, opacity = _opacity;
260	  const isSolidA = _isSolidA, hasLiquidA = _hasLiquidA, hasWallA = _hasWallA;
261	  const hasVisA = _hasVisA, typeA = _typeA, visTypeA = _visTypeA;
262	  const lW = _lW, rW = _rW, bW = _bW, tW = _tW;
263	  const vlW = _vlW, vrW = _vrW, vbW = _vbW, vtW = _vtW;
264	  const hasLE = _hasLE, hasRE = _hasRE, hasTE = _hasTE, hasBE = _hasBE;
265	  const isHalfA = _isHalfA;
266	  const fx = _fx, fy = _fy;
267	
268	  // ---- P1：原始缓存 ----
269	  for (let lx = 0; lx < pw; lx++) {
270	    const x = px0 + lx;
271	    for (let ly = 0; ly < ph; ly++) {
272	      const y = py0 + ly;
273	      const i = lx * ph + ly;
274	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) { isSolidA[i] = 1; continue; }
275	      const si = st.idx(x, y);
276	      const lq = st.liquid[si];
277	      level[i] = lq / 255;
278	      hasLiquidA[i] = lq > 0 ? 1 : 0;
279	      hasWallA[i] = st.wall[si] > 0 ? 1 : 0;
280	      typeA[i] = toVanillaType(st.liquidType[si]);
281	      visTypeA[i] = typeA[i]; // P3 只跑到底部 10 行外（同原版 L152）——预填本格类型兜底，
282	                              // 否则底带格子 visTypeA=0 被当水画（原版靠跨帧残留缓存掩蔽）
283	      const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[si]];
284	      isSolidA[i] = d && d.solid ? 1 : 0;
285	    }
286	  }
287	  const at = (lx: number, ly: number) => lx * ph + ly; // padding 内坐标
288	  // 半砖缓存（LiquidRenderer.cs:103-110）：halfBrick && 上格有液体 && 非平台；
289	  // 无液体时类型继承上格（109-110）。注意 ptr[-1] = y-1 = 上格
290	  for (let lx = 0; lx < pw; lx++) {
291	    for (let ly = 1; ly < ph; ly++) {
292	      const i = at(lx, ly);
293	      if (!st.inBounds(px0 + lx, py0 + ly)) continue;
294	      const si = st.idx(px0 + lx, py0 + ly);
295	      const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[si]];
296	      if (st.half[si] && hasLiquidA[at(lx, ly - 1)] && !(d && d.platform)) {
297	        isHalfA[i] = 1;
298	        if (!hasLiquidA[i]) typeA[i] = typeA[at(lx, ly - 1)];
299	      }
300	    }
301	  }
302	
303	  // ---- P2：可见液位（内区 = 真实视图区） ----
304	  for (let lx = PAD; lx < pw - PAD; lx++) {
305	    for (let ly = PAD; ly < ph - PAD; ly++) {
306	      const i = at(lx, ly);
307	      let v: number;
308	      if (isHalfA[i] && hasLiquidA[at(lx, ly - 1)]) {
309	        v = 1; // 半砖 + 上格有液体：可视液面拉满（LiquidRenderer.cs:121-122）
310	      } else if (!hasLiquidA[i]) {
311	        const li = at(lx - 1, ly), ri = at(lx + 1, ly), ui = at(lx, ly - 1), di = at(lx, ly + 1);
312	        let val = 0;
313	        // 原版先判 ptr[-1]/[+1]（上下），后判 ptr[-H]/[+H]（左右）→ 左右命中时 Type 覆盖（L129-138）
314	        if (hasLiquidA[ui] && hasLiquidA[di] && typeA[ui] === typeA[di] && !isSolidA[ui] && !isSolidA[di]) {
315	          val = level[ui] + level[di];
316	          typeA[i] = typeA[ui];
317	        }
318	        if (hasLiquidA[li] && hasLiquidA[ri] && typeA[li] === typeA[ri] && !isSolidA[li] && !isSolidA[ri]) {
319	          val = Math.max(val, level[li] + level[ri]);
320	          typeA[i] = typeA[li];
321	        }
322	        v = val * 0.5;
323	      } else {
324	        v = level[i];
325	      }
326	      visLevel[i] = v;
327	      hasVisA[i] = v !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
328	    }
329	  }
330	
331	  // ---- P3：瀑布拖尾（向下传播） + 实心格处理 ----
332	  for (let lx = 0; lx < pw; lx++) {
333	    for (let ly = 0; ly < ph - 10; ly++) {
334	      const i = at(lx, ly);
335	      if (hasVisA[i] && (!isSolidA[i] || isHalfA[i])) {
336	        opacity[i] = 1;
337	        visTypeA[i] = typeA[i];
338	        const len = WATERFALL_LENGTH[typeA[i]] ?? 3;
339	        const step = 1 / (len + 1);
340	        let k = 1;
341	        for (let s = 1; s <= len; s++) {
342	          k -= step;
343	          const bi = at(lx, ly + s);
344	          if (ly + s >= ph) break;
345	          if (!isSolidA[bi]) {
346	            visLevel[bi] = Math.max(visLevel[bi], visLevel[i] * k);
347	            opacity[bi] = k;
348	            visTypeA[bi] = typeA[i];
349	          } else break;
350	        }
351	      }
352	      if (isSolidA[i] && !isHalfA[i]) {
353	        visLevel[i] = 1;
354	        hasVisA[i] = 0;
355	      } else {
356	        // 原版 L178-179：非实心格在此重算可见性——P3 拖尾写入的干格因此变为可见
357	        hasVisA[i] = visLevel[i] !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
358	      }
359	    }
360	  }
361	
362	  // ---- P4：四壁插值 + 边存在 + 变体 FrameOffset ----
363	  for (let lx = PAD; lx < pw - PAD; lx++) {
364	    for (let ly = PAD; ly < ph - PAD; ly++) {
365	      const i = at(lx, ly);
366	      if (!hasVisA[i]) { hasLE[i] = hasRE[i] = hasTE[i] = hasBE[i] = 0; continue; }
367	      const ui = at(lx, ly - 1), di = at(lx, ly + 1), li = at(lx - 1, ly), ri = at(lx + 1, ly);
368	      let nT = 0, nB = 1, nL = 0, nR = 1;
369	      const my = visLevel[i];
370	      if (!hasVisA[ui]) nT += visLevel[di] * (1 - my);
371	      if (!hasVisA[di] && !isSolidA[di] && !isHalfA[di]) nB -= visLevel[ui] * (1 - my);
372	      if (!hasVisA[li] && !isSolidA[li] && !isHalfA[li]) nL += visLevel[ri] * (1 - my);
373	      if (!hasVisA[ri] && !isSolidA[ri] && !isHalfA[ri]) nR -= visLevel[li] * (1 - my);
374	      tW[i] = nT; bW[i] = nB; lW[i] = nL; rW[i] = nR;
375	      hasTE[i] = (!hasVisA[ui] && !isSolidA[ui]) || nT !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
376	      hasBE[i] = (!hasVisA[di] && !isSolidA[di]) || nB !== 1 ? 1 : 0;
377	      hasLE[i] = (!hasVisA[li] && !isSolidA[li]) || nL !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
378	      hasRE[i] = (!hasVisA[ri] && !isSolidA[ri]) || nR !== 1 ? 1 : 0;
379	      // 注:原版 1.4.5.6 的 _waveMask 几何波动是【死代码】——WAVE_MASK_STRENGTH 是
380	      // new byte[5] 全零从不赋值、WaveFilters 事件全工程无订阅者(LiquidRenderer.cs:110/616)。
381	      // 用户感知的"水面波动"全部来自 16 帧纹理动画(下方 :289-291 已 1:1)+ 表面静态带 1280,
382	      // 此处曾加过的正弦 TopWall 扰动是多余的非原版效果,已按源码标杆移除。
383	      let ox = 0, oy = 0;
384	      if (!hasLE[i]) { ox += hasRE[i] ? 32 : 16; }
385	      if (hasLE[i] && hasRE[i]) {
386	        ox = 16; oy += 32;
387	        if (hasTE[i]) oy = 16;
388	      } else if (!hasTE[i]) {
389	        if (!hasLE[i] && !hasRE[i]) oy += 48;
390	        else oy += 16;
391	      }
392	      if (oy === 16 && !!(hasLE[i] ^ hasRE[i]) && (py0 + ly) % 2 === 0) oy += 16;
393	      fx[i] = ox; fy[i] = oy;
394	    }
395	  }
396	
397	  // ---- P5：壁值平滑 ----
398	  for (let lx = PAD; lx < pw - PAD; lx++) {
399	    for (let ly = PAD; ly < ph - PAD; ly++) {
400	      const i = at(lx, ly);
401	      if (!hasVisA[i]) continue;
402	      const ui = at(lx, ly - 1), di = at(lx, ly + 1), li = at(lx - 1, ly), ri = at(lx + 1, ly);
403	      vlW[i] = lW[i]; vrW[i] = rW[i]; vtW[i] = tW[i]; vbW[i] = bW[i];
404	      if (hasVisA[ui] && hasVisA[di]) {
405	        if (hasLE[i]) vlW[i] = (lW[i] * 2 + lW[ui] + lW[di]) * 0.25;
406	        if (hasRE[i]) vrW[i] = (rW[i] * 2 + rW[ui] + rW[di]) * 0.25;
407	      }
408	      if (hasVisA[li] && hasVisA[ri]) {
409	        if (hasTE[i]) vtW[i] = (tW[i] * 2 + tW[li] + tW[ri]) * 0.25;
410	        if (hasBE[i]) vbW[i] = (bW[i] * 2 + bW[li] + bW[ri]) * 0.25;
411	      }
412	    }
413	  }
414	
415	  // ---- P6：瀑布侧/邻接修正 ----
416	  for (let lx = PAD; lx < pw - PAD; lx++) {
417	    for (let ly = PAD; ly < ph - PAD; ly++) {
418	      const i = at(lx, ly);
419	      if (!hasLiquidA[i]) continue;
420	      const ui = at(lx, ly - 1), di = at(lx, ly + 1), li = at(lx - 1, ly), ri = at(lx + 1, ly);
421	      if (hasTE[i] && !hasBE[i] && !!(hasLE[i] ^ hasRE[i])) {
422	        if (hasRE[i]) { vrW[i] = vrW[di]; vtW[i] = vtW[li]; }
423	        else { vlW[i] = vlW[di]; vtW[i] = vtW[ri]; }
424	      } else if (fx[di] === 16 && fy[di] === 32) {
425	        if (vlW[i] > 0.5) { vlW[i] = 0; fx[i] = 0; fy[i] = 0; }
426	        else if (vrW[i] < 0.5) { vrW[i] = 1; fx[i] = 32; fy[i] = 0; }
427	      }
428	    }
429	  }
430	
431	  // ---- P7：内角填充 ----
432	  for (let lx = PAD; lx < pw - PAD; lx++) {
433	    for (let ly = PAD; ly < ph - PAD; ly++) {
434	      const i = at(lx, ly);
435	      if (!hasLiquidA[i]) continue;
436	      const ui = at(lx, ly - 1), di = at(lx, ly + 1), li = at(lx - 1, ly), ri = at(lx + 1, ly);
437	      if (!hasBE[i] && !hasLE[i] && !hasTE[i] && !hasRE[i]) {
438	        // 原版 LiquidRenderer.cs:342-346：left.HasTopEdge && up.HasLeftEdge，墙面取 up.vlW / left.vtW
439	        if (hasTE[li] && hasLE[ui]) {
440	          fx[i] = Math.max(4, Math.floor(16 - vlW[ui] * 16)) - 4;
441	          fy[i] = 48 + Math.max(4, Math.floor(16 - vtW[li] * 16)) - 4;
442	          vlW[i] = 0; vtW[i] = 0; vrW[i] = 1; vbW[i] = 1;
443	        } else if (hasTE[ri] && hasRE[ui]) {
444	          fx[i] = 32 - Math.min(16, Math.floor(vrW[ui] * 16) - 4);
445	          fy[i] = 48 + Math.max(4, Math.floor(16 - vtW[ri] * 16)) - 4;
446	          vlW[i] = 0; vtW[i] = 0; vrW[i] = 1; vbW[i] = 1;
447	        }
448	      }
449	    }
450	  }
451	
452	  // ---- 绘制 ----
453	  const texCache = new Map<number, ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null>();
454	  const texFor = (vt: number) => {
455	    let t = texCache.get(vt);
456	    if (t === undefined) {
457	      t = atlas.ensureVImage(waterSheet(vt, waterStyle)) ?? null;
458	      if (t) texCache.set(vt, t);  // 只缓存命中(缓存 null 会把未就绪永久化)
459	    }
460	    return t;
461	  };
462	  // 双动画帧（1456 LiquidRenderer.Update :844-856）：
463	  //  _animationFrame = windSpeed*25 ± 6 每 秒（边缘格波浪,负风倒放,模 16）
464	  //  _waterfallAnimationFrame = 0.5 每 秒（X==16 中列 = 下落水柱/池体,慢速流纹——
465	  //  1.4.4 新增;1405 无此项,旧移植全部格共用快帧导致下落水柱 6fps 快闪"贴图不对"）
466	  const rate = windSpeed >= 0 ? windSpeed * 25 + 6 : windSpeed * 25 - 6;
467	  const animFrame = ((Math.floor((nowMs / 1000) * rate) % 16) + 16) % 16;
468	  const waterfallFrame = Math.floor((nowMs / 1000) * 0.5) % 16;
469	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
470	
471	  // 主循环（双 pass 共用：背景 pass 画在方块层前、透明度 1.0；前景 pass 画在方块后、乘 DEFAULT_OPACITY）
472	  for (let lx = PAD; lx < pw - PAD; lx++) {
473	    const x = px0 + lx;
474	    for (let ly = PAD; ly < ph - PAD; ly++) {
475	      const y = py0 + ly;
476	      const i = at(lx, ly);
477	      if (!hasVisA[i]) continue;
478	      const vt = visTypeA[i];
479	      const tex = texFor(vt);
480	      if (!tex) continue;
481	      const n2 = Math.min(0.75, vlW[i]), n3 = Math.max(0.25, vrW[i]);
482	      const n4 = Math.min(0.75, vtW[i]);
483	      let n5 = Math.max(0.25, vbW[i]);
484	      // 半砖可视底边截到半格（LiquidRenderer.cs:382-383）
485	      if (isHalfA[i] && isSolidA[i] && n5 > 0.5) n5 = 0.5;
486	      // IsVisible（LiquidRenderer.cs:384）：半砖格自身有半液且无墙 → 不画（交给上格溢流）
487	      if (isHalfA[i] && hasLiquidA[i] && level[i] < 1 && !hasWallA[i]) continue;
488	      const sx = Math.floor(16 - n3 * 16) + fx[i];
489	      const sy = Math.floor(16 - n5 * 16) + fy[i];
490	      const sw = Math.ceil((n3 - n2) * 16), sh = Math.ceil((n5 - n4) * 16);
491	      const isSurface = fx[i] === 16 && fy[i] === 0 && y > groundLevel - 40;
492	      // 帧选择 1:1（DrawNormalLiquids :636-644）：中列（sx==16,下落柱/池体）走慢速瀑布帧
493	      const srcY = isSurface ? 1280 : sy + (sx === 16 ? waterfallFrame : animFrame) * 80;
494	      if (sw <= 0 || sh <= 0 || sx + sw > tex.width || srcY + sh > tex.height) continue;
495	      // ---- 微光（shimmer, vt=3）：DrawShimmer（LiquidRenderer.cs:682-730）----
496	      // 原版三步：①基底层 water_14 直画 + 逐顶点 GetShimmerBaseColor 波动蓝移（:745-759）
497	      // ②sparkle 层：源矩形 X+48（water_14 的第 4 列 16px 闪纹带）、Y+80×GetShimmerFrame
498	      // 16 帧动画，仅 flag（非内部/瀑布帧）或 (x+y)%2==0 的格子画（:720-729）
499	      // ③sparkle 顶点色 = GetShimmerGlitterColor 漂移彩虹 + 呼吸 alpha（:766-790）
500	      // Canvas 无逐顶点色：基底层 2×2 子块双线性 multiply（applyShimmerBaseTint，
501	      // 薄边缘条 sw<8 跳过防透明像素被压暗）；sparkle 用离线染色变体（hue 16 档量化）、
502	      // alpha 取四角均值
503	      if (vt === 3) {
504	        const tVis = (nowMs / 1000) * 60;                      // ≈ Main.timeForVisualEffects（帧计数）
505	        const dstX = x * 16 + Math.floor(n2 * 16), dstY = y * 16 + Math.floor(n4 * 16);
506	        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, opacity[i] * (isBackground ? 1 : 0.75));
507	        ctx.drawImage(tex, sx, srcY, sw, sh, dstX, dstY, sw, sh);
508	        if (sw >= 8) applyShimmerBaseTint(ctx, x, y, dstX, dstY, sw, sh, tVis);
509	        const flag = !(sx === 16 || sy % 80 === 48);           // :719
510	        if (flag || (x + y) % 2 === 0) {
511	          // alpha = 像素亮度（tintSparkleBand 已把灰度写进 alpha）× 四角
512	          // GetShimmerGlitterOpacity 均值。四角均值与原版逐顶点双线性插值的面积均值
513	          // 数值等价（全网格采样验证平均差 0.0000），top（=有干燥邻边的表层/边缘格，
514	          // :719 flag=true）四角恒 0.5；body（全浸没 (sx,sy)=(16,48) 内部格）复算
515	          // 均值 0.09 / 峰值 0.34 → 星点恒为极淡彩虹（近白微闪）。注意 sparkle 不乘
516	          // 前景 0.75/背景 1 系数——SetShimmerVertexColors_Sparkle :732-743 直接
517	          // ×= ptr->Opacity
518	          const ga = (shimmerGlitterOpacity(flag, x, y, tVis)
519	            + shimmerGlitterOpacity(flag, x + 1, y, tVis)
520	            + shimmerGlitterOpacity(flag, x, y + 1, tVis)
521	            + shimmerGlitterOpacity(flag, x + 1, y + 1, tVis)) * 0.25;  // :773-790
522	          if (ga > 0.02) {
523	            const fr = shimmerFrame(flag, x, y, tVis);         // :791-801
524	            // :716 sparkle 前把 sourceRectangle 重置回原始 SourceRectangle 再加偏移——
525	            // 表面格的基底层虽强制切 Y=1280（:700），但 sparkle 的 Y 用的是原始 sy+80*fr
526	            // （shimmerSparkleSource）。旧实现误用 1280+80*fr：表面格 sparkle 几乎全部
527	            // 越界被跳过（=表层"彩虹条"消失），仅 fr=0 时命中 Y=1280 的黑底块反而画出黑斑。
528	            const [sSrcX, sSrcY] = shimmerSparkleSource(sx, sy, fr);
529	            if (sSrcX + sw <= tex.width && sSrcY + sh <= tex.height) {
530	              const hue = (((x + y / 6) + tVis / 30) / 6) % 1; // :767 彩虹相位
531	              const hueIdx = ((Math.floor(hue * SPARKLE_HUE_STEPS) % SPARKLE_HUE_STEPS)
532	                + SPARKLE_HUE_STEPS) % SPARKLE_HUE_STEPS;
533	              const spark = shimmerSparkleTint(tex, hueIdx);   // 染色带；null 则退回原图（无彩虹）
534	              ctx.save();
535	              // 原版 sparkle pass 是【加色叠加】非 source-over：GetShimmerGlitterColor
536	              // :766-771 先 color.A=0 再 vector4×glitter → 顶点 alpha 恒 0、RGB 已预乘
537	              // glitter 强度；tileBatch = SpriteBatch 默认 AlphaBlend（预乘 (One,
538	              // InvSrcAlpha)，TileBatch.Begin :216）→ 最终像素 = 基底 + 灰度纹素×彩虹×
539	              // glitter×tileOpacity，黑底纹素贡献恰为 0、基色不被替换。旧实现 source-over
540	              // 以饱和彩虹【替换】基色（dst×(1-a)+彩虹×a）→ body 星点呈高可见度彩色块
541	              // （用户实测"闪光点变成彩色的"），改 'lighter' 后 body 星点 = 基底上微弱
542	              // 增亮的近白微闪、表面白基底上呈漂移彩虹条（与原版一致）。
543	              ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
544	              ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, opacity[i] * ga);
545	              ctx.drawImage(spark ?? tex, sSrcX + (spark ? -48 : 0), sSrcY, sw, sh,
546	                dstX, dstY, sw, sh);
547	              ctx.restore();
548	            }
549	          }
550	        }
551	        continue;
552	      }
553	      // 双 pass（Main.cs DrawWaters(true/false) + LiquidRenderer.InternalDraw）：
554	      // 背景 pass 不透明（露出方块透明像素 = 浸润）；前景 pass 水 0.6 半透明质感
555	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, opacity[i] * (isBackground ? 1 : (DEFAULT_OPACITY[vt] ?? 0.6)));
556	      ctx.drawImage(tex, sx, srcY, sw, sh,
557	        x * 16 + Math.floor(n2 * 16), y * 16 + Math.floor(n4 * 16), sw, sh);
558	    }
559	  }
560	
561	  // ---- 浸润 pass：DrawTile_LiquidBehindTile(TileDrawing.cs:3859-4193) ----
562	  // 对每个【实心方块格】，检查四邻是否有液体 → 在方块格内画一条液体带
563	  // （背景 pass，不透明）。方块贴图画在其上——透明圆角像素透出液体色而不是墙色/背景色。
564	  // 这是原版"水体包裹方块"的全部秘密；条带宽度由邻接方向决定：
565	  //   仅上方有水 → 格顶 16×4 横条(:4081-4087)
566	  //   仅下方有水 → 格底 16×4 横条(:4089-4093)
567	  //   仅左侧有水 → 格左 4px 竖条(:4113-4116);仅右侧 → 格右 4px(:4118-4121)
568	  //   左右都有   → 整格 16 宽(:4108-4111);深度按液体量 num6=(256-max)/32*2 从底收
569	  if (isBackground) drawLiquidBehindTilesOnly(ctx, atlas, st, groundLevel, tx0, ty0, tx1, ty1, waterStyle);
570	
571	  // ---- 调试快照落盘（DebugReport render.subsystems.liquids）----
572	  _liqDebug.calls++;
573	  _liqDebug.lastMs = nowMs;
574	  _liqDebug.waterStyle = waterStyle;
575	  _liqDebug.waterSheet = waterSheet(0, waterStyle);
576	  _liqDebug.isBackground = isBackground;
577	  _liqDebug.animFrame = animFrame;
578	  _liqDebug.waterfallFrame = waterfallFrame;
579	  _liqDebug.windSpeed = windSpeed;
580	  _liqDebug.window = [tx0, ty0, tx1, ty1];
581	  _liqDebug.sheetsReady = [...texCache.entries()].map(([vt, t]) => [vt, !!t] as [number, boolean]);
582	  _liqDebug.atlasReady = true;
583	
584	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
585	}
586	
587	// ---- LiquidSlope 斜面贴合（TileDrawing.cs:4526-4553 DrawPartialLiquid）----
588	// 语义勘定（2026-08-13 源码核）：LiquidSlope_N 不是"水面斜线"贴图——是
589	// 【被锤成坡面的实心格内液体】的斜面形状表。原版 DrawPartialLiquid 在 tile.slope()
590	// 1..4 时改用 TextureAssets.LiquidSlope[liquidType]（:4540-4552），源矩形 X 额外
591	// += 18*(slope-1)（:4539，表 72×16 = 4 列×18px 间距、16px 单元，像素实测四列
592	// 分别为 slope 1 右下斜/2 左下斜/3 右上斜/4 左上斜）。
593	// 原版该绘制有两个调用层（TileDrawing.cs:462 背景 pass / :529 实心层 pass）：
594	// 背景 pass 对普通块仍走平面 Liquid（flag = !BlocksWaterDrawingBehindSelf，
595	// :4528-4531），仅玻璃族(54/541/328/459/470)与实心层 pass 走斜面表。本仓只有
596	// 一个浸润 pass（无实心层液体带），采用实心层语义对所有坡面格生效——即还原
597	// 玩家可见结果（液体贴合坡面斜边）。【简化登记】坡面格与玻璃族的双 pass 差异
598	// 未拆分；LiquidSlope 的 Y 直接复用平面带算出的 ry/rh（原版同源 liquidSize）。
599	/** LiquidSlope 源矩形选择（纯函数）：slope 1..4 → 斜面表列 X = rx + 18*(slope-1)；
600	 *  slope 0 / 半砖 → null = 走平面 Liquid 表（:4531-4533 flag||num==0 直画分支）。 */
601	export function liquidSlopeSource(
602	  slope: number, rx: number, ry: number, rw: number, rh: number,
603	): { sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number } | null {
604	  if (slope < 1 || slope > 4) return null;
605	  return { sx: rx + 18 * (slope - 1), sy: ry, sw: rw, sh: rh };
606	}
607	
608	/** LiquidSlope 表文件（与上方浸润 pass 的 Liquid_N 同一套 liquidType→style 映射：
609	 *  水=群系水色 0-13 / 岩浆=1 / 蜂蜜=11 / 微光=14，TextureAssets.LiquidSlope[15]） */
610	export function liquidSlopeSheet(vt: number, waterStyle = 0): string {
611	  if (vt === 1) return 'vanilla/LiquidSlope_1.png';   // 岩浆
612	  if (vt === 2) return 'vanilla/LiquidSlope_11.png';  // 蜂蜜
613	  if (vt === 3) return 'vanilla/LiquidSlope_14.png';  // 微光
614	  return `vanilla/LiquidSlope_${Math.max(0, Math.min(14, waterStyle))}.png`;
615	}
616	
617	/**
618	 * 浸润 pass:原版 TilesRenderer.DrawLiquidBehindTiles → DrawTile_LiquidBehindTile
619	 * (TileDrawing.cs:3859-4193)。对视区内每个实心方块格检查四邻液体,在方块格内画液体带。
620	 * 背景 pass 调用(画在方块贴图之下)——透明圆角像素透出液体色。
621	 */
622	export function drawLiquidBehindTilesOnly(
623	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas,
624	  st: TileStore, groundLevel: number,
625	  tx0: number, ty0: number, tx1: number, ty1: number, waterStyle = 0,
626	): void {
627	  // Liquid_N 贴图(16×16 纯液体块;水 Liquid_0 多 style 但取首 16px)
628	  const texCache = new Map<number, ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null>();
629	  const texFor = (vt: number) => {
630	    let t = texCache.get(vt);
631	    if (t === undefined) {
632	      const file = vt === 1 ? 'vanilla/Liquid_1.png' : vt === 2 ? 'vanilla/Liquid_11.png'
633	      : vt === 3 ? 'vanilla/Liquid_14.png' // 微光
634	      : `vanilla/Liquid_${Math.max(0, Math.min(13, waterStyle))}.png`; // 群系水色(原版 DrawTile_LiquidBehindTile 同走 waterStyle)
635	      t = atlas.ensureVImage(file) ?? null;
636	      if (t) texCache.set(vt, t); // 只缓存命中(缓存 null 会把未就绪永久化——首帧丢条带)
637	    }
638	    return t;
639	  };
640	  // LiquidSlope 表缓存（同款"只缓存命中"策略；坡面格才触碰）
641	  const slopeTexCache = new Map<number, ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null>();
642	  const slopeTexFor = (vt: number) => {
643	    let t = slopeTexCache.get(vt);
644	    if (t === undefined) {
645	      t = atlas.ensureVImage(liquidSlopeSheet(vt, waterStyle)) ?? null;
646	      if (t) slopeTexCache.set(vt, t);
647	    }
648	    return t;
649	  };
650	
651	  for (let x = Math.max(1, tx0); x <= Math.min(st.w - 2, tx1); x++) {
652	    for (let y = Math.max(1, ty0); y <= Math.min(st.h - 2, ty1); y++) {
653	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
654	      if (!st.flags[i]) continue; // 空格没有"方块后面"
655	      const def = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]];
656	      if (!def || !def.solid || def.platform) continue; // 仅实心方块(:4075 tileSolid 排除)
657	
658	      // 四邻液体(TileDrawing.cs:3861-3900;379=conveyor 按 vanilla 视为无液体)。
659	      // ★零分配版(2026-08-18):旧 lq() 每调用 new {lq,lt} ×4/格——水邻屏每帧
660	      // 8k-33k 对象 = GC 0.94s/次 = 行走 70-135ms 掉帧主源(trace ProfileChunk+GC 相关性)
661	      let nLq = 0, nLt = 0;
662	      const nb = (dx: number, dy: number): boolean => {
663	        const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
664	        if (!st.inBounds(nx, ny)) return false;
665	        const ni = st.idx(nx, ny);
666	        nLq = st.liquid[ni];
667	        nLt = st.liquidType[ni] || 1;
668	        return true;
669	      };
670	      let Lq = 0, Lqt = 0, Rq = 0, Rqt = 0, Uq = 0, Uqt = 0, Dq = 0, Dqt = 0;
671	      if (nb(-1, 0)) { Lq = nLq; Lqt = nLt; }
672	      if (nb(1, 0)) { Rq = nLq; Rqt = nLt; }
673	      if (nb(0, -1)) { Uq = nLq; Uqt = nLt; }
674	      if (nb(0, 1)) { Dq = nLq; Dqt = nLt; }
675	      // 坡面格的"实心侧"邻格不计入（:3967/:3989/:4011/:4028 的 slope 门）：
676	      // slope 1/3 忽略左、2/4 忽略右、3/4 忽略上、1/2 忽略下——液体只从坡面开放侧来
677	      const slope = st.slope[i];
678	      const slopeBlocksLeft = slope === 1 || slope === 3;
679	      const slopeBlocksRight = slope === 2 || slope === 4;
680	      const slopeBlocksUp = slope === 3 || slope === 4;
681	      const slopeBlocksDown = slope === 1 || slope === 2;
682	      // flag 语义(:3967-4053):flag=左 / flag2=右 / flag3=上 / flag4=下(>240)
683	      let fL = Lq > 0 && !slopeBlocksLeft;
684	      let fR = Rq > 0 && !slopeBlocksRight;
685	      const fU = Uq > 0 && !slopeBlocksUp;
686	      const fD = Dq > 240 && !slopeBlocksDown;
687	      if (!fL && !fR && !fU && !fD) continue; // :4055-4057 四邻无液体跳过
688	
689	      // 液体类型与最大液位(:3940-3965 取最高液位邻居;顺序 L→R→U→D 严格大于才覆盖)
690	      let maxLq = 0, lt = 0;
691	      if (fL && Lq > maxLq) { maxLq = Lq; lt = toVanillaType(Lqt); }
692	      if (fR && Rq > maxLq) { maxLq = Rq; lt = toVanillaType(Rqt); }
693	      if (fU && Uq > maxLq) { maxLq = Uq; lt = toVanillaType(Uqt); }
694	      if (fD && Dq > maxLq) { maxLq = Dq; lt = toVanillaType(Dqt); }
695	      const tex = texFor(lt); // lt=3 微光走 Liquid_14
696	      if (!tex) continue;
697	
698	      // ---- 液体矩形计算(:4069-4123) ----
699	      // 原版默认 rect = (0, 4, 16, 16);pos = (tileX*16, tileY*16)
700	      let rx = 0, ry = 4, rw = 16, rh = 16;   // liquidSize(源矩形)
701	      let px = x * 16, py = y * 16;            // 目标位置
702	
703	      if (fD && (fL || fR)) { fL = true; fR = true; /* :4070-4074 下+任一侧 → 双侧全宽 */ }
704	      if (!(fU && (fL || fR)) && !(fD && fU)) {
705	        if (fU) {
706	          // 仅上方有水:格顶 16×4 条(:4081-4087;坡面/半砖加深到 12)
707	          rh = 4;
708	          const isHalf = st.half[i];
709	          if (isHalf || slope !== 0) rh = 12;
710	        } else if (fD && !fL && !fR) {
711	          // 仅下方:格底 16×4(:4089-4093)
712	          py = y * 16 + 12; rh = 4;
713	        } else {
714	          // 侧向有水(:4095-4123)
715	          let y0 = 4;
716	          const upNi = st.idx(x, y - 1);
717	          if (st.liquid[upNi] === 0 && !st.isSolid(x, y - 1)) y0 = 0; // 上方无液无实心→从格顶画(:4098-4101)
718	          const num6 = Math.floor((256 - maxLq) / 32) * 2; // 深度=液位不足时从底收(:4096,4102)
719	          const isHalf = st.half[i];
720	          if (slope !== 0) {
721	            // 坡面格:整格宽、自 num6 起(:4102-4106)——窄条分支对坡面不适用
722	            py = y * 16 + num6; ry = num6; rh = 16 - num6;
723	          } else if (fL && fR || isHalf) {
724	            // 左右都有 或 半砖:整格宽(:4108-4111)
725	            py = y * 16 + num6; ry = y0; rh = 16 - num6;
726	          } else if (fL) {
727	            // 仅左:格左 4px 竖条(:4113-4116)
728	            py = y * 16 + num6; ry = y0; rw = 4; rh = 16 - num6;
729	          } else {
730	            // 仅右:格右 4px 竖条(:4118-4121)
731	            px = x * 16 + 12; py = y * 16 + num6; ry = y0; rw = 4; rh = 16 - num6;
732	          }
733	        }
734	      }
735	      if (rw <= 0 || rh <= 0) continue;
736	
737	      // ---- 斜面格换 LiquidSlope 表（TileDrawing.cs:4539-4552）----
738	      // 源 X += 18*(slope-1)；平面表语义(ry/rh 不变)。半砖恒 slope=0 → 天然走平面。
739	      const slopeSrc = liquidSlopeSource(slope, rx, ry, rw, rh);
740	      let drawTex: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null = tex;
741	      let srx = rx;
742	      if (slopeSrc) {
743	        drawTex = slopeTexFor(lt);
744	        srx = slopeSrc.sx;
745	        if (!drawTex) drawTex = tex; // 斜面表未就绪 → 退回平面带(下帧懒加载生效)
746	      }
747	
748	      // ---- 源矩形钳制到贴图边界(原版 XNA PointClamp 自动钳;Canvas 2D 需显式) ----
749	      // Liquid_N 是 16×16 纯块;ry=4 起 + rh=16 会到 y=20 越界——XNA 读边缘像素,
750	      // 我们钳 rh = tex.height - ry 保持等比(不画满时目标也同步缩)
751	      // 【2026-08-14 回退:dstH=rh 拉伸方案实测令更多泥土格浸润恶化——用户报加重,先归零再排查】
752	      const srcH = Math.min(rh, drawTex.height - ry);
753	      const srcW = Math.min(rw, drawTex.width - srx);
754	      if (srcW <= 0 || srcH <= 0) continue;
755	      const dstH = srcH; // 源=目标尺寸(原版 DrawPartialLiquid 同源同目标)
756	      const dstW = srcW;
757	
758	      // ---- 微光分支（TileDrawing.cs:4188-4191 num2==14）----
759	      // 原版 SetShimmerVertexColors 把四角顶点色【整体重置】为 White×opacity×
760	      // GetShimmerBaseColor(角)——上方整段 num7 透明度/光照计算对微光全部丢弃，
761	      // opacity = solidLayer ? 0.75 : 1。本函数只在背景 pass 调用（= 原版
762	      // DrawLiquidBehindTiles :462 的 solidLayer:false 调用点）→ opacity 1。
763	      // 彩色叠加【该有】：旧注释"省略彩色叠加"与末尾的 multiply 叠加自相矛盾，
764	      // 按源码对齐为"不透明直画 + 2×2 子块波色 multiply"（与前景 DrawShimmer 同式）。
765	      if (lt === 3) {
766	        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
767	        ctx.drawImage(drawTex, srx, ry, srcW, srcH, px, py, dstW, dstH);
768	        if (srcW >= 8) applyShimmerBaseTint(ctx, x, y, px, py, dstW, dstH,
769	          (performance.now() / 1000) * 60);
770	        continue;
771	      }
772	
773	      // ---- 不透明度(:4126-4146) ----
774	      // 地上=1.0(有墙压 0.6,墙 21→0.9);地下=0.5 基线
775	      let alpha = 0.5;
776	      if (y <= Math.floor(groundLevel)) {
777	        alpha = 1.0;
778	        const wi = st.wall[i];
779	        if (wi === 21) alpha = 0.9;
780	        else if (wi > 0) alpha = 0.6;
781	      }
782	      ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
783	      ctx.drawImage(drawTex, srx, ry, srcW, srcH, px, py, dstW, dstH);
784	    }
785	  }
786	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
787	}
788	

```

Very interesting! Key discovery at line 379-382 of our VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts:

```
// 注:原版 1.4.5.6 的 _waveMask 几何波动是【死代码】——WAVE_MASK_STRENGTH 是
// new byte[5] 全零从不赋值、WaveFilters 事件全工程无订阅者(LiquidRenderer.cs:110/616)。
// 用户感知的"水面波动"全部来自 16 帧纹理动画(下方 :289-291 已 1:1)+ 表面静态带 1280,
// 此处曾加过的正弦 TopWall 扰动是多余的非原版效果,已按源码标杆移除。
```

So a previous session already determined that WAVE_MASK_STRENGTH being all-zero in 1456 is REAL (not a decompiler artifact) — they treated the wave mask as dead code. But wait — that's about the LiquidRenderer's wave mask (viscosity mask), which is only one part. The full Water Waves system (WaterShaderData) is the screen-space distortion that warps the water texture. That's what we need to port now.

Hmm, but hold on. Let me think about what's actually visible in vanilla 1.4.4+:
1. Water surface waves (the wavy water surface you see) — this is the WaterShaderData screen distortion applied when compositing waterTarget. The distortion target contains the wave simulation (ripples from entities, wind noise?) and the "FilterWaterDistortion" pass warps the water layer's UVs based on the distortion texture + noise image.

UseProgress(_progress). So the FilterWaterDistortion shader combines:
- sampler 0: noise image (Images/Misc/noise) — animated by _progress
- sampler 1: distortion target (wave sim)
- sampler 2: the water layer texture
- uImageOffset: scrolling
- intensity: from SceneState (wind/rain based)

So the visible water wave = noise-based UV distortion (wind-driven, drifting via _progress) + simulation ripples (entities). THAT's the "water waves" system! The noise image is "Images/Misc/noise" — do we have that asset? Let me check public/sprites/vanilla for noise.

Now, how does the vanilla shader apply it? The FilterWaterDistortion pixel shader (compiled, unavailable). Known from tML decompilation efforts... I recall the water distortion filter in Terraria works like: for each pixel of the water layer, sample the distortion texture (channel-packed: R = X displacement, G = Y displacement around 0.5 neutral) plus animated noise, offset the sampling position of the water layer texture, output. Intensity scales displacement.

Since exact HLSL is unavailable, our Canvas2D implementation must approximate the *rendering* side while keeping the *data* side (wave sim + injection math + quality gates + intensity) 1:1.

Given Canvas2D, the practical way to render water waves:
- The water surface tiles (IsSurfaceLiquid / edge tiles) get their drawn source row (and/or destination y) displaced by the wave field sampled at that position.
- The wave field = CPU simulation at 0.25 screen scale + noise-driven ambient wave (from _progress + noise texture sampling) — same as vanilla data.

But the vanilla distortion applies to the whole water layer composite (not just surface). The most visible effect is on the water top edge. For Canvas2D parity, I'll apply displacement to the drawImage calls of liquid tiles: sample distortion gradient at tile center → offset dst position (x,y) — actually vanilla offsets UV sampling of the layer, which shifts the texture content; equivalent to offsetting the destination rect by the displacement vector (screen-space warp). Since we draw per-tile, offsetting each tile's dst by its local displacement approximates the screen-space warp well at 16px granularity. For surface rows, that gives the classic wavy water surface.

Hmm wait, actually, I need to double check something fundamental: does the vanilla water wave distortion displace the ENTIRE water layer (all liquid pixels), or just make waves on the surface? From watching vanilla gameplay: the whole body of water subtly warps (you see wobble throughout), strongest at the surface. The distortion texture is sampled per-pixel of the composite, so yes — entire layer warp. Our per-tile approach: sample at tile center, offset tile dst. Plus optionally sub-tile (draw in 2 strips vertically?) — keep it simple: per-tile displacement (16px grid), zero-allocation.

Performance: drawing with per-tile offset changes the tile seams (adjacent tiles offset differently could create 1px gaps/overlaps). To mitigate: displacement is small (<4px), gaps might show. Alternative: only displace tiles that have top edge (surface) + their immediate body via horizontal-only sampling per column... Hmm.

Let me look at how big the offsets are in vanilla: The ripple color encodes displacement in [0,1] around 0.5 neutral. The shader: offset = (distortion.rg - 0.5) * someScale * intensity + noise-based drift. The scale is unknown (compiled shader). Common values in such shaders: * 0.1 or so of UV space. So the ambient waves (the constant gentle water motion) = noise-based UV warp in the composite shader, and the sim (ripples) is the interactive part. 

So for our port:

Both displaced per-tile at draw time. Zero allocation: precompute Float32Array displacement grid at sim step (0.25 screen res), sample per tile with bilinear or nearest.

41	.sw-set-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }
42	.sw-set-row > span:first-child { width: 88px; color: #c8d0f0; }
43	.sw-set-row input[type=range] { flex: 1; accent-color: #7d92d6; }
44	.sw-set-row .val { width: 40px; text-align: right; color: #ffe8a0; }
45	.sw-set-row input[type=checkbox] { width: 18px; height: 18px; accent-color: #7d92d6; }
46	.sw-set-btns { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }
47	.sw-set-btns button, .sw-set-back {
48	  background: #3a4680; color: #e8e8f4; border: 1px solid #7d92d6; border-radius: 4px;
49	  padding: 8px 12px; cursor: pointer; font-family: inherit; text-align: left;
50	}
51	.sw-set-btns button:hover, .sw-set-back:hover { background: #4a5aa0; }
52	.sw-set-footer { display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; margin-top: 12px; }
53	.sw-set-back { padding: 6px 22px; }
54	/* 语言选择网格（M8,视觉对齐 sw-wc-opts 互斥选项模式） */
55	.sw-set-lang-title { color: #ffe8a0; font-size: 14px; }
56	.sw-set-lang-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); gap: 6px; }
57	.sw-set-lang-grid button {
58	  background: #232c52; color: #e8e8f4; border: 1px solid #3a4680; border-radius: 4px;
59	  padding: 7px 10px; cursor: pointer; font-family: inherit; text-align: left;
60	}
61	.sw-set-lang-grid button:hover { background: #4a5aa0; }
62	.sw-set-lang-grid button.active { outline: 1px solid #ffd76e; background: #5a6ac0; }
63	.sw-set-hint { color:#8a90b8; font-size:12px; }
64	`;
65	
66	export class SettingsPanel {
67	  private panel: HTMLElement;
68	  private content: HTMLElement;
69	  private cat = 'sound';
70	  private unsubLang: (() => void) | null = null;
71	
72	  constructor(parent: HTMLElement, private flow: SettingsFlow) {
73	    if (!document.getElementById('sw-set-style')) {
74	      const style = document.createElement('style');
75	      style.id = 'sw-set-style';
76	      style.textContent = CSS;
77	      document.head.appendChild(style);
78	    }
79	    this.panel = document.createElement('div');
80	    this.panel.className = 'sw-set-panel';
81	    this.panel.innerHTML = `
82	      <div class="sw-set-title">${Lang.text('LegacyMenu.14')}</div>
83	      <div class="sw-set-body">
84	        <div class="sw-set-cats"></div>
85	        <div class="sw-set-content"></div>
86	      </div>
87	      <div class="sw-set-footer"><button class="sw-set-back">${Lang.text('LegacyMenu.6')}</button></div>
88	    `;
89	    this.content = this.panel.querySelector('.sw-set-content')!;
90	    this.buildCats();
91	    this.panel.querySelector('.sw-set-back')!.addEventListener('click', () => this.close(flow.onBack));
92	    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
93	      if (e.code === 'Escape') this.close(flow.onBack);
94	    };
95	    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
96	    // 语言切换 → 整面板重建(标题/分栏/内容全部重取文案;保留当前分栏)
97	    this.unsubLang = Lang.onChange(() => this.rebuild());
98	    parent.appendChild(this.panel);
99	    this.build();
100	    UISfx.click();
101	  }
102	
103	  private catLabel(cat: string): string {
104	    switch (cat) {
105	      case 'sound': return Lang.text('LegacyMenu.65'); // 音量
106	      case 'ui': return Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.Interface');
107	      case 'game': return Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.Gameplay');
108	      case 'data': return Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.Data');
109	      default: return cat;
110	    }
111	  }
112	
113	  private buildCats() {
114	    const cats = document.createElement('div');
115	    cats.className = 'sw-set-cats';
116	    const list = ['sound', 'ui', 'game', ...(this.flow.onQuickLoad ? ['data'] : [])];
117	    for (const c of list) {
118	      const div = document.createElement('div');
119	      div.dataset.cat = c;
120	      div.textContent = this.catLabel(c);
121	      div.addEventListener('click', () => { this.cat = c; this.build(); });
122	      cats.appendChild(div);
123	    }
124	    this.panel.querySelector('.sw-set-cats')!.replaceWith(cats);
125	  }
126	
127	  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;
128	
129	  private close(fn: () => void) {
130	    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
131	    this.unsubLang?.();
132	    this.panel.remove();
133	    fn();
134	  }
135	
136	  private slider(label: string, min: number, max: number, step: number, get: () => number, set: (v: number) => void) {
137	    const row = document.createElement('div');
138	    row.className = 'sw-set-row';
139	    const span = document.createElement('span');
140	    span.textContent = label;
141	    const input = document.createElement('input');
142	    input.type = 'range';
143	    input.min = String(min); input.max = String(max); input.step = String(step);
144	    input.value = String(get());
145	    const val = document.createElement('span');
146	    val.className = 'val';
147	    val.textContent = String(Math.round(get() * 100));
148	    input.addEventListener('input', () => {
149	      const v = Number(input.value);
150	      set(v);
151	      val.textContent = String(Math.round(v * 100));
152	    });
153	    row.append(span, input, val);
154	    return row;
155	  }
156	
157	  private toggle(label: string, get: () => boolean, set: (v: boolean) => void) {
158	    const row = document.createElement('label');
159	    row.className = 'sw-set-row';
160	    const span = document.createElement('span');
161	    span.textContent = label;
162	    const input = document.createElement('input');
163	    input.type = 'checkbox';
164	    input.checked = get();
165	    input.addEventListener('change', () => set(input.checked));
166	    row.append(span, input);
167	    return row;
168	  }
169	
170	  /** 双态枚举行（点击在两态间切换；智能光标 Toggle/Hold 模式用） */
171	  private modeRow(label: string, _get: () => string, value: () => string, cycle: () => void) {
172	    const row = document.createElement('div');
173	    row.className = 'sw-set-row';
174	    const span = document.createElement('span');
175	    span.textContent = label;
176	    const btn = document.createElement('button');
177	    btn.className = 'sw-set-back';
178	    btn.style.padding = '4px 14px';
179	    btn.textContent = value();
180	    btn.addEventListener('click', () => { cycle(); btn.textContent = value(); });
181	    row.append(span, btn);
182	    return row;
183	  }
184	
185	  /** 语言选择网格:显示名取各语言包自带的 Language.* 键(原版语义) */
186	  private langGrid() {
187	    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
188	    const title = document.createElement('div');
189	    title.className = 'sw-set-lang-title';
190	    title.textContent = Lang.text('LegacyMenu.102'); // 选择语言
191	    const grid = document.createElement('div');
192	    grid.className = 'sw-set-lang-grid';
193	    const current = options.data.lang;
194	    for (const c of Lang.cultures) {
195	      const b = document.createElement('button');
196	      const name = Lang.text(c.displayNameKey);
197	      b.textContent = Lang.has(c.displayNameKey) ? name : c.name;
198	      if (c.name === current) b.classList.add('active');
199	      b.addEventListener('click', async () => {
200	        if (c.name === options.data.lang) return;
201	        const prev = options.data.lang;
202	        b.disabled = true;
203	        // 先写内存值:Lang.setLanguage 的 emit 会触发 rebuild 重建本网格,
204	        // 高亮读 options.data.lang——后写会拿到旧值,激活态留在上一语言
205	        options.data.lang = c.name;
206	        const ok = await Lang.setLanguage(c.name);
207	        b.disabled = false;
208	        if (ok) {
209	          await options.set('lang', c.name); // 持久化(值已同步,仅落盘)
210	        } else {
211	          options.data.lang = prev; // 失败回滚
212	          this.rebuild();
213	        }
214	      });
215	      b.dataset.lang = c.name;
216	      grid.appendChild(b);
217	    }
218	    wrap.append(title, grid);
219	    const hint = document.createElement('div');
220	    hint.className = 'sw-set-hint';
221	    hint.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.FontHint');
222	    wrap.append(hint);
223	    return wrap;
224	  }
225	
226	  private build() {
227	    this.panel.querySelectorAll('.sw-set-cats div').forEach((el) => {
228	      (el as HTMLElement).classList.toggle('active', (el as HTMLElement).dataset.cat === this.cat);
229	    });
230	    this.content.innerHTML = '';
231	    const d = options.data;
232	    if (this.cat === 'sound') {
233	      this.content.append(
234	        this.slider(Lang.text('LegacyMenu.99'), 0, 1, 0.05, () => d.musicVol, (v) => { d.musicVol = v; this.applySound(); void options.set('musicVol', v); }),
235	        this.slider(Lang.text('LegacyMenu.98'), 0, 1, 0.05, () => d.sfxVol, (v) => { d.sfxVol = v; this.applySound(); void options.set('sfxVol', v); }),
236	        // Ambient 环境音轨（Main.ambientVolume，config 键 VolumeAmbient；雷声/滴水/
237	        // 瀑布岩浆流/小动物鸣叫/暴风雪循环等 13+ 环境音独立音量，LegacySoundPlayer.cs:1443）
238	        // 标签 = LegacyMenu.119（Main.cs:50914 音量三杆同族 98/99/119,原版 12 语言官译;
239	        // 旧自造 Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.AmbientVol 仅 zh/en 两译,他语言落英文兜底）
240	        this.slider(Lang.text('LegacyMenu.119'), 0, 1, 0.05, () => d.ambientVol, (v) => { d.ambientVol = v; this.applySound(); void options.set('ambientVol', v); }),
241	      );
242	    } else if (this.cat === 'ui') {
243	      this.content.append(
244	        this.slider(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.UiScale'), 0.75, 1.5, 0.05, () => d.uiScale, (v) => void options.set('uiScale', v).then(() => this.applyUi())),
245	        // 资源条样式（原版 PlayerResourcesDisplaySet 两主样式：'New'=FancyClassic
246	        // 金框分格（1.4.4+ 默认）/ 'Default'=Classic 朴素心星）。样式名 = 原版键
247	        // UI.HealthManaStyle_*（Main.cs:49379 同源;旧硬编码中文 12 语言不可译）
248	        this.modeRow(
249	          Lang.text('UI.SelectHealthStyle').replace(/:.*/, ''),
250	          () => d.resourceBarStyle,
251	          () => Lang.text(d.resourceBarStyle === 'fancy' ? 'UI.HealthManaStyle_New' : 'UI.HealthManaStyle_Default'),
252	          () => void options.set('resourceBarStyle', d.resourceBarStyle === 'fancy' ? 'classic' : 'fancy'),
253	        ),
254	        // 小地图边框皮肤（MinimapFrameManager 9 款；config.json "MinimapFrame" 字符串键；
255	        // 循环方向 = SelectionHolder.CycleSelection 倒序回绕，cycleMinimapFrame 纯函数）
256	        this.modeRow(
257	          Lang.text('UI.SelectMapBorder').replace(/:.*/, ''),
258	          () => d.minimapFrame,
259	          () => Lang.text(`UI.MinimapFrame_${d.minimapFrame}`),
260	          () => { void options.set('minimapFrame', cycleMinimapFrame(d.minimapFrame)); },
261	        ),
262	        this.langGrid(),
263	      );
264	      const hint = document.createElement('div');
265	      hint.className = 'sw-set-hint';
266	      hint.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.UiScaleHint');
267	      this.content.append(hint);
268	    } else if (this.cat === 'game') {
269	      this.content.append(
270	        this.toggle(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.DevMode'), () => d.devMode, (v) => void options.set('devMode', v)),
271	        // 背景 开/关（Main.BackgroundEnabled Main.cs:790，原版视频设置项 Lang.menu[100/101]；
272	        // 天气阴天渐变 cloudBGAlpha 的门，Game.updateWeather 注入 WeatherCtx.backgroundEnabled）
273	        this.toggle(Lang.menu(100).replace(/[:：].*$/, ''), () => d.backgrounds, (v) => void options.set('backgrounds', v)),
274	        // 图块在风中摆动（原版视频设置 SettingsEnabled_TilesSwayInWind；WindSway 系统总门）。
275	        // 原版行文案 = UI.TilesSwayInWindOn/Off 融合串（IngameOptions.cs:1055），
276	        // 取 On 支去冒号做标签（同背景开关的 Lang.menu(100) 处理法）
277	        this.toggle(Lang.text('UI.TilesSwayInWindOn').replace(/[:：].*$/, ''), () => d.swayInWind, (v) => void options.set('swayInWind', v)),
278	        // 渲染帧率上限(2026-08-18 原版对齐:原版锁 60fps;60/120/不锁 三档循环。
279	        // 逻辑 tick 恒 60Hz 不变——仅 gate 渲染,高分屏省一半渲染/GPU 负载)
280	        this.modeRow(
281	          Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.FrameCap'),
282	          () => String(d.frameCap),
283	          () => d.frameCap === 0 ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.FrameCapOff')
284	            : d.frameCap === 120 ? '120 FPS' : '60 FPS',
285	          () => void options.set('frameCap', d.frameCap === 60 ? 120 : d.frameCap === 120 ? 0 : 60),
286	        ),
287	        // 跳帧三态(原版 FrameSkipMode:Off/On/Subtle,默认温和):渲染超预算只跑
288	        // 逻辑不画,保逻辑恒 60Hz——弱机卡顿时"画面慢但游戏不慢"
289	        this.modeRow(
290	          Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.FrameSkip'),
291	          () => d.frameSkipMode,
292	          () => d.frameSkipMode === 'off' ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.FrameSkipOff')
293	            : d.frameSkipMode === 'subtle' ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.FrameSkipSubtle')
294	            : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.FrameSkipOn'),
295	          () => void options.set('frameSkipMode', d.frameSkipMode === 'off' ? 'subtle' : d.frameSkipMode === 'subtle' ? 'on' : 'off'),
296	        ),
297	        // 智能光标（原版 IngameOptions :1418 Smart Cursor 段）：模式 Toggle/Hold + 三开关
298	        this.modeRow(
299	          Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartCursorMode'),
300	          () => d.smartCursorMode,
301	          () => Lang.text(d.smartCursorMode === 'toggle'
302	            ? 'Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartCursorToggle'
303	            : 'Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartCursorHold'),
304	          () => void options.set('smartCursorMode', d.smartCursorMode === 'toggle' ? 'hold' : 'toggle'),
305	        ),
306	        this.toggle(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartAxeAfterPickaxe'), () => d.smartAxeAfterPickaxe, (v) => void options.set('smartAxeAfterPickaxe', v)),
307	        this.toggle(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartBlocks'), () => d.smartBlocksEnabled, (v) => void options.set('smartBlocksEnabled', v)),
308	        this.toggle(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartHoldRelease'), () => d.smartHoldCanReleaseMidUse, (v) => void options.set('smartHoldCanReleaseMidUse', v)),
309	        // 玩家对决（Player.hostile;原版仅联机显示该 UI——Main.cs:2982 ShouldPVPDraw,
310	        // 单机常驻为调试便利,联机语义由协议同步压制）。显示态读【实时 player.hostile】
311	        // （原版会话语义:进服恒 false,与 options 上次设置无关——勿用 options 回显）
312	        this.toggle(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.Pvp'), () => {
313	          const p = (window as unknown as { __swGame?: { player: { hostile: boolean } } }).__swGame?.player;
314	          return p ? p.hostile : d.pvpEnabled;
315	        }, (v) => {
316	          void options.set('pvpEnabled', v);
317	          const p = (window as unknown as { __swGame?: { player: import('../entities/Player').Player } }).__swGame?.player;
318	          if (p) p.hostile = v;
319	        }),
320	        // 队伍 0-5（Player.team;点击循环切换,显示色块+序号）
321	        (() => {
322	          const row = document.createElement('div');
323	          row.className = 'sw-set-row';
324	          const span = document.createElement('span');
325	          span.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.PvpTeam');
326	          const btn = document.createElement('button');
327	          const render = () => {
328	            const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(5, (window as unknown as { __swGame?: { player: { team: number } } }).__swGame?.player.team ?? d.pvpTeam));
329	            btn.textContent = `${t} ▮`;
330	            btn.style.color = TEAM_COLORS[t] ? `rgb(${TEAM_COLORS[t][0]},${TEAM_COLORS[t][1]},${TEAM_COLORS[t][2]})` : '#fff';
331	          };
332	          btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
333	            const gp = (window as unknown as { __swGame?: { player: import('../entities/Player').Player } }).__swGame;
334	            const next = (((gp?.player.team ?? d.pvpTeam) + 1) % 6);
335	            void options.set('pvpTeam', next);
336	            if (gp) gp.player.team = next;
337	            render();
338	          });
339	          render();
340	          row.append(span, btn);
341	          return row;
342	        })(),
343	      );
344	      const hint = document.createElement('div');
345	      hint.className = 'sw-set-hint';
346	      hint.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.DevModeHint');
347	      this.content.append(hint);
348	      const scHint = document.createElement('div');
349	      scHint.className = 'sw-set-hint';
350	      scHint.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Settings.SmartCursorHint');
351	      this.content.append(scHint);
352	    } else if (this.cat === 'data' && this.flow.onQuickLoad) {
353	      const btns = document.createElement('div');
354	      btns.className = 'sw-set-btns';
355	      const mk = (label: string, fn: () => void) => {
356	        const b = document.createElement('button');
357	        b.textContent = label;
358	        b.addEventListener('click', () => this.close(fn));
359	        btns.appendChild(b);
360	      };
361	      mk(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Data.ContinueSave'), () => this.flow.onQuickLoad!());
362	      mk(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Data.LoadFile'), () => this.flow.onLoadFile?.());
363	      mk(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Data.ImportWld'), () => this.flow.onImportWld?.());
364	      this.content.append(btns);
365	    }
366	  }
367	
368	  /** 语言已切换:重建标题/分栏/内容(保留 this.cat) */
369	  private rebuild() {
370	    (this.panel.querySelector('.sw-set-title') as HTMLElement).textContent = Lang.text('LegacyMenu.14');
371	    (this.panel.querySelector('.sw-set-back') as HTMLElement).textContent = Lang.text('LegacyMenu.6');
372	    this.buildCats();
373	    this.build();
374	  }
375	
376	  /** 音量下发（Audio 实例由 main.ts 注入过 options.onChange，这里同步 UISfx） */
377	  private applySound() {
378	    // UISfx 共享实例的 master + ambient 双轨
379	    (UISfx as unknown as { sfx: { master: number; ambient: number } }).sfx.master = options.data.sfxVol;
380	    (UISfx as unknown as { sfx: { master: number; ambient: number } }).sfx.ambient = options.data.ambientVol;
381	  }
382	
383	  private applyUi() {
384	    UIScale.userScale = options.data.uiScale;
385	    UIScale.recompute();
386	  }
387	}
388	

```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-18T07:50:41.681Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
用户问:"Terarria1456 这里源码不全吗"——在问反编译源码是不是不全。我需要如实回答:

1. Terarria1456 是 C# 反编译源码,游戏逻辑(Main.cs/LiquidRenderer/WaterShaderData 等)是全的。
2. 但**着色器数学不在这套 C# 源码里**——WaterProcessor / FilterWaterDistortion / WaterDistortionObject 三个像素着色器是编译好的 HLSL 字节码,打包在 Content 的 Effects.fxb(XNB)里,C# 反编译只能看到 pass 名字符串注册(DyeInitializer.cs:427-428、ScreenEffectInitializer.cs:41)。所以"波动传播内核"与"合成扭曲"的逐指令数学在 1456 源码里拿不到。
3. C# 侧能拿到的全部:档位门、注入数学(NPC/玩家/弹幕/涟漪队列)、缓冲结构(0.25 缩放 ping-pong、中性 0.5、滚动偏移、每帧≤2 步)、强度链(SceneState 风雨深度公式)、进度累加(_progress += dt×intensity×0.75)、设置持久化与 UI。

还有一个可以抢救的:Effects.fxb 本身!游戏 Content 里如果有解包的 fxb,我可以反汇编/反编译 FX 字节码(Microsoft DXBC)。DXBC 反编译有现成工具(d3dcompiler_47 的 D3DDisassemble 或开源的 dxbc-disasm)。XNB 里 Effect 是 MGFX 格式(FNA/MonoGame 的 .mgfx,DXBC 内嵌)。我们可以:
- 找到游戏 Content 的 Effects.xnb(或 fxb)
- 提取 DXBC 字节码
- 用 spirv-cross/dxbc 反汇编读出指令

但等等——用户说的是"我们有1456源码"——这个 Terarria1456 目录是 C# decompile。真正的游戏 Content(含 Effects.fxb)可能也在某处(之前会话解包了贴图/音效,说明有原始 Content)。让我找找原始 Content 目录,看有没有 Effects.xnb / .fxb,然后尝试反汇编 DXBC。

XNA 的 .xnb Effect 是编译过的 HLSL(XSMV/DX9 字节码)。DX9 字节码反汇编比 DXBC 难一点但也有工具。FNA 转换的 .fnacx/mgfx 是 DXBC(DX11)。

先看看项目里有没有原始 Content:
```
