util.rs
//! `dxbc` — a `no_std` DXBC container parser, SM4/SM5 disassembler, and encoder.
//!
//! This crate can parse DXBC containers (the binary format produced by `fxc`
//! and consumed by Direct3D 10–12), decode individual chunk types (signatures,
//! resource definitions, statistics, root signatures, shader bytecode, etc.),
//! and round-trip shader bytecode through an intermediate representation.
//!
//! Metadata strings and raw blobs borrow from the input buffer via
//! `Cow<'a, T>` — zero allocation on read, transparent ownership on mutation.
//!
//! # Quick start
//!
//! ```rust,ignore
//! use dxbc::{DxbcContainer, ChunkData};
//!
//! let container = DxbcContainer::parse(dxbc_bytes).unwrap();
//! for chunk in &container.chunks {
//!     match chunk.parse() {
//!         ChunkData::Shader(program) => {
//!             // Program implements Display for fxc-compatible disassembly.
//!             println!("{program}");
//!         }
//!         _ => {}
//!     }
//! }
//! ```
//!
//! # Crate features
//!
//! * **`no_std`** — the crate depends only on `core` and `alloc`; no filesystem or I/O.
//! * **Zero-copy metadata** — chunk strings and blobs borrow from the input
//!   via `Cow<'a, T>`, supporting read-then-patch workflows.
//! * **Decode** — `shex::decode` turns raw SHEX/SHDR bytes into a [`Program`].
//! * **Display** — `Program` and all chunk types implement `Display` for
//!   `fxc.exe`-compatible disassembly output.
//! * **Encode** — `shex::encode` serialises a `Program` back to
//!   bytes, enabling shader modification and round-trip workflows.

#![no_std]
extern crate alloc;

/// DXBC chunk parsers for every known chunk FourCC.
pub mod chunks;
/// DXBC container (header + chunk table) parser and writer.
pub mod container;
/// SM4/SM5 shader bytecode decoder, encoder, and formatter.
pub mod shex;
/// Shared helpers (C-string reading, string table builder).
pub mod util;

// Re-export the most commonly used types at the crate root.
pub use chunks::ChunkData;
pub use container::{DxbcChunk, DxbcContainer, build_dxbc, scan_dxbc};
pub use shex::Program;
pub use shex::disassemble;

````

171	// 携带 CTAB/PRSI/CLIT/FXLC 四字符块。

174	  0x1070: 'rsq', 0x1080: 'sin', 0x1090: 'cos', 0x10A0: 'asin', 0x10B0: 'acos', 0x10C0: 'atan',
175	  0x2000: 'min', 0x2010: 'max', 0x2020: 'lt', 0x2030: 'ge', 0x2040: 'add', 0x2050: 'mul',
176	  0x2060: 'atan2', 0x2080: 'div', 0x3000: 'cmp', 0x3010: 'movc', 0x5000: 'dot', 0x5020: 'noise',
177	  0xA000: 'min_s', 0xA010: 'max_s', 0xA020: 'lt_s', 0xA030: 'ge_s', 0xA040: 'add_s',
178	  0xA050: 'mul_s', 0xA060: 'atan2_s', 0xA080: 'div_s', 0xD000: 'dot_s', 0xD020: 'noise_s',
179	};
180	function parsePreshader(blob, presAt, presSizeTok) {
181	  const b = new DataView(blob.buffer, blob.byteOffset, blob.byteLength);
182	  const tu = (o) => b.getUint32(presAt + o, true);
183	  const lines = [];
184	  let p = 8;                              // 跳过 PRES 四字符 + version token（0x46580201）
185	  const end = 4 + presSizeTok * 4 - 4;
186	  const blocks = {};
187	  while (p + 4 <= end) {
188	    const t = tu(p);
189	    if ((t & 0xFFFF) !== 0xFFFE) break;   // 0xFFFF 子 EOS
190	    const size = (t >>> 16) & 0x7FFF;
191	    const id = blob.toString('latin1', presAt + p + 4, presAt + p + 8);
192	    if (size > 0) blocks[id] = { at: presAt + p + 4, size: size - 1 };   // 四字符在 comment token+4；内容 token=size−1

197	  // CLIT：[fourcc][count][count 个 double]

200	  const lits = [];
201	  for (let i = 0; i < litCount; i++) lits.push(b.getFloat64(clit.at + 8 + i * 8, true));
202	  // preshader CTAB：符号→寄存器（INPUT item = regIndex*4+comp）
203	  let syms = [];
204	  if (blocks.CTAB) {

206	    const nConst = cbu(4 + 12), infoOff = cbu(4 + 16);
207	    for (let i = 0; i < nConst; i++) {
208	      const e = 4 + infoOff + i * 20;

210	      const regIndex = b.getUint16(cb.at + e + 6, true);
211	      const regCount = b.getUint16(cb.at + e + 8, true);
212	      syms.push({

214	        regIndex, regCount,
215	      });
216	    }
217	  }
218	  const symOf = (item) => {
219	    for (const s of syms) {
220	      const base = s.regIndex * 4, cnt = s.regCount * 4;
221	      if (base <= item && base + cnt > item) return `${s.name}${s.regCount > 1 ? `[${Math.floor((item - base) / 4)}]` : '.' + 'xyzw'[item % 4]}`;
222	    }
223	    return `in${item}`;
224	  };
225	  // PRSI：tokens[7]=output_map_count，之后 (base,count) 对
226	  let outMap = [];

228	    const pb = blocks.PRSI, pu = bu(pb);

231	  }
232	  const outOf = (item) => {
233	    for (const [base, cnt] of outMap) {
234	      if (base * 4 <= item && (base + cnt) * 4 > item) return `c${base + Math.floor((item - base * 4) / 4)}`;
235	    }
236	    return `out${item}`;
237	  };
238	  // FXLC：[fourcc][opcode_count] + 每指令（opcodetok, operandcount-1, 每 operand 3 token）
239	  const fx = blocks.FXLC, fu = bu(fx);
240	  let nInst = fu(4);
241	  let q = 8;

243	    const opTok = fu(q);

309	  const major = (verTok >> 8) & 0xFF, minor = verTok & 0xFF;
310	  lines.push(`    // ${((verTok >> 16) & 0xFFFF) === 0xFFFF ? 'ps' : 'vs'}_${major}_${minor}`);
311	  let maxP = blob.length;
312	  let presAt = -1, presSize = 0;
313	  while (p + 4 <= maxP) {
314	    const t = b.getUint32(p, true); p += 4;
315	    const op = t & 0xFFFF;
316	    if (op === 0xFFFE) {          // 注释段：size 字段=数据 DWORD 数（不含自身）
317	      if (p + 4 <= maxP && blob.toString('latin1', p, p + 4) === 'PRES') { presAt = p; presSize = (t >>> 16) & 0x7FFF; }
318	      p += ((t >>> 16) & 0x7FFF) * 4;
319	      continue;
320	    }
321	    if (op === 0xFFFF) { lines.push('    end'); break; }
322	    const e = OP[op];
323	    if (!e) { lines.push(`    ??? op=${op} raw=0x${t.toString(16)} @${p - 4}`); break; }
324	    const [name, nOps] = e;
325	    if (name === 'comment') { continue; }
326	    const ops = [];
327	    for (let i = 0; i < nOps; i++) { ops.push(b.getUint32(p, true)); p += 4; }
328	    if (name === 'dcl') {
329	      const info = ops[0];
330	      const itype = (info >> 28) & 7 | ((info >> 8) & 0x18);
331	      const texType = info & 0xFFFF;
332	      const tt = texType === 2 ? '2d' : texType === 3 ? 'cube' : texType === 4 ? 'volume' : texType === 1 ? '1d' : '';
333	      if (itype === 9) lines.push(`    dcl_${tt} ${regName(ops[1])}`);
334	      else if (itype === 1) lines.push(`    dcl v${ops[1] & 0x7FF}`);
335	      else if (itype === 3) lines.push(`    dcl t${ops[1] & 0x7FF}`);
336	      else lines.push(`    dcl_${itype} ${ops[1].toString(16)}`);
337	    } else if (name === 'def') {
338	      const f = [];
339	      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) f.push(b.getFloat32(p - 16 + i * 4, true));
340	      lines.push(`    def ${regName(ops[0])}, ${f.map((x) => x.toFixed(6)).join(', ')}`);
341	    } else if (name === 'defb') {
342	      lines.push(`    defb ${regName(ops[0])}, ${ops[1]}`);
343	    } else if (name === 'texld') {
344	      lines.push(`    texld ${dstStr(ops[0])}, ${srcStr(ops[1])}, ${regName(ops[2])}`);
345	    } else {
346	      const ctrl = op === 0x40 ? '' : '';
347	      const parts = ops.map((o, i) => (i === 0 && name !== 'texkill' ? dstStr(o) : srcStr(o)));
348	      lines.push(`    ${name}${ctrl} ${parts.join(', ')}`.trimEnd());
349	    }
350	  }
351	  // cN → 参数名注解
352	  const named = [...regSym.entries()].sort((a, z) => parseInt(a[0].slice(1)) - parseInt(z[0].slice(1)));
353	  if (named.length) lines.push(`    // symbols: ${named.map(([r, n]) => `${r}=${n}`).join(' ')}`);
354	  if (presAt >= 0) lines.push(...parsePreshader(blob, presAt, presSize));
355	  return lines.join('\n');
356	}
357	
358	// ---------- JSON 导出（游戏侧 SM2 解释器消费：字节码级 1:1）----------
359	const jsonIdx = process.argv.indexOf('--json');
360	if (jsonIdx >= 0) {
361	  const passesJson = [];
362	  const seenPass = new Set();
363	  for (const t of techs) {
364	    for (const pass of t.passes) {
365	      if (!pass.name || seenPass.has(pass.name)) continue;   // 重名 pass（ArmorPlaid×2）取首
366	      seenPass.add(pass.name);
367	      for (const s of pass.states) {
368	        const oi = s.value.objects?.[0];
369	        if (oi === undefined || (objType[oi] !== 15 && objType[oi] !== 16)) continue;
370	        const blob = blobs.get(oi);
371	        if (!blob) continue;
372	        // 抽取：指令 token 序列（到 end）+ CTAB 符号 + preshader（块原始 token）
373	        const b = new DataView(blob.buffer, blob.byteOffset, blob.byteLength);
374	        let p = 0;
375	        const verTok = b.getUint32(p, true); p += 4;
376	        if (((verTok >> 16) & 0xFFFF) !== 0xFFFF) continue;   // 仅像素着色器
377	        const tokens = [];
378	        let presTokens = null;
379	        while (p + 4 <= blob.length) {
380	          const tok = b.getUint32(p, true); p += 4;
381	          const op = tok & 0xFFFF;
382	          if (op === 0xFFFE) {
383	            const size = (tok >>> 16) & 0x7FFF;
384	            if (p + 4 <= blob.length && blob.toString('latin1', p, p + 4) === 'PRES') {
385	              presTokens = [];
386	              for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) { presTokens.push(b.getUint32(p + i * 4, true)); }
387	            }
388	            p += size * 4;
389	            continue;
390	          }
391	          tokens.push(tok);
392	          if (op === 0xFFFF) break;
393	        }
394	        // preshader 预解码：presTokens 重建字节缓冲后按字节解析（块布局同 parsePreshader）
395	        let pres = null;
396	        if (presTokens) {
397	          pres = { lits: [], syms: [], outMap: [], insts: [] };
398	          const pb = Buffer.alloc(presTokens.length * 4);
399	          presTokens.forEach((tk, i) => pb.writeUInt32LE(tk >>> 0, i * 4));
400	          const pu32 = (o) => pb.readUInt32LE(o);
401	          let q = 8;                       // PRES 四字符 + version token
402	          while (q + 8 <= pb.length) {
403	            const ct = pu32(q);
404	            if ((ct & 0xFFFF) !== 0xFFFE) break;
405	            const csize = (ct >>> 16) & 0x7FFF;
406	            const id = pb.toString('latin1', q + 4, q + 8);
407	            const ds = q + 8;              // 块内容起点（fourcc 后）
408	            if (id === 'CLIT') {          // 内容=[count][doubles]
409	              const n = pu32(ds);
410	              for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) pres.lits.push(pb.readDoubleLE(ds + 4 + i * 8));
411	            } else if (id === 'PRSI') {   // mojo tokens[7] 以 fourcc 为 [0] → 内容+24
412	              const n = pu32(ds + 24);
413	              for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) pres.outMap.push([pu32(ds + 28 + i * 8), pu32(ds + 32 + i * 8)]);
414	            } else if (id === 'CTAB') {   // 内容起点即 Size 字段（偏移基准）
415	              const st = ds;
416	              const nConst = pu32(st + 12), infoOff = pu32(st + 16);
417	              for (let i = 0; i < nConst; i++) {
418	                const e = st + infoOff + i * 20;          // CINFO 20B
419	                const nameOff = pu32(e);
420	                const regIndex = pb.readUInt16LE(e + 6);
421	                const regCount = pb.readUInt16LE(e + 8);
422	                let nm = '';
423	                for (let b = st + nameOff; b < pb.length && pb[b] !== 0; b++) nm += String.fromCharCode(pb[b]);
424	                pres.syms.push({ name: nm, regIndex, regCount });
425	              }
426	            } else if (id === 'FXLC') {   // 内容=[opcode_count][instrs]
427	              const nInst = pu32(ds);
428	              let r = ds + 4;
429	              for (let i = 0; i < nInst; i++) {
430	                const opTok = pu32(r);
431	                const nOps = pu32(r + 4) + 1;
432	                r += 8;
433	                const ops = [];
434	                for (let j = 0; j < nOps; j++) {
435	                  ops.push([pu32(r), pu32(r + 4), pu32(r + 8)]);
436	                  r += 12 + pu32(r) * 8;
437	                }
438	                pres.insts.push([opTok, ops]);
439	              }
440	            }
441	            q = ds + (csize - 1) * 4;      // 下一子注释（size 含 fourcc token）
442	          }
443	        }
444	        // def 常量内嵌为 token 序列即可；CTAB 符号给寄存器映射
445	        const syms = parseCtab(blob).map((s) => ({
446	          name: s.name, kind: s.regSet === 3 ? 'sampler' : 'const',
447	          reg: s.regSet === 3 ? `s${s.regIndex}` : `c${s.regIndex}`, count: s.regCount,
448	        }));
449	        passesJson.push({ pass: pass.name, obj: oi, tokens, presTokens, preshader: pres, symbols: syms });
450	      }
451	    }
452	  }
453	  writeFileSync(process.argv[jsonIdx + 1], JSON.stringify({ source: file.replace(/^.*\//, ''), passes: passesJson }));
454	  console.log(`json: ${passesJson.length} passes → ${process.argv[jsonIdx + 1]}`);
455	}
456	const out = [];
457	out.push(`== ${file}  params=${numParams} techs=${numTechs} objects=${numObjects} small=${numSmall} large=${numLarge}`);
458	out.push('-- effect params (uniforms):');
459	for (const p of params) {
460	  const v = p.values ? `[${p.values.map((x) => +x.toFixed(4)).join(', ')}]` : p.objects ? `→obj${p.objects}` : p.samplerStates ? 'sampler' : '';
461	  out.push(`  ${p.name ?? '?'} type=${p.type} class=${p.class}${v ? ' ' + v : ''}`);
462	}
463	for (const t of techs) {
464	  out.push(`\n== technique ${t.name} (${t.passes.length} passes)`);
465	  for (const pass of t.passes) {
466	    if (only && !only.has(pass.name)) continue;
467	    out.push(`  -- pass ${pass.name ?? ''}`);
468	    for (const s of pass.states) {
469	      const oi = s.value.objects?.[0];
470	      if (oi === undefined) continue;
471	      if (objType[oi] !== 15 && objType[oi] !== 16) continue;   // D3DXPT PIXELSHADER=15/VERTEXSHADER=16
472	      const blob = blobs.get(oi);
473	      if (!blob) { out.push(`    <missing blob obj${oi}>`); continue; }
474	      out.push(`    <${objType[oi] === 15 ? 'ps' : 'vs'} obj${oi} ${blob.length}B>`);
475	      const dumpIdx = process.argv.indexOf('--dump-obj');
476	      if (dumpIdx >= 0 && process.argv[dumpIdx + 1].split(',').includes(String(oi))) {
477	        writeFileSync(`/tmp/fx-obj${oi}.bin`, blob);
478	        out.push(`    (dumped /tmp/fx-obj${oi}.bin)`);
479	      }
480	      try {
481	        const syms = parseCtab(blob);
482	        out.push(disasm(blob, syms));
483	      } catch (e) { out.push(`    !! ${e.message}`); }
484	    }
485	  }
486	}
487	const text = out.join('\n');
488	writeFileSync(outFile, text);
489	console.log(`wrote ${outFile} (${text.length}B)`);
490	console.log(text.split('\n').slice(0, 40).join('\n'));
491	

```

{src(ops[1])}, s{ops[2]&0x7FF}\")\n    elif name in ('dp2add',):\n        print(f\"dp2add {dst(ops[0])}, {src(ops[1])}, {src(ops[2])}, {src(ops[3])}\")\n    elif name == 'cmp':\n        print(f\"cmp {dst(ops[0])}, {src(ops[1])}, {src(ops[2])}, {src(ops[3])}\")\n    else:\n        parts = [dst(ops[0])] + [src(o) for o in ops[1:]]\n        print(f\"{name} {', '.join(parts)}\")\n    p += 1 + n\nEOF",

i += 1\n    return s.decode('latin1', 'replace')\n# CLIT: blk = [fourcc][count][doubles...]\nlits = []\nif 'CLIT' in blocks:\n    b = blocks['CLIT']\n    n = b[2]\n    for i in range(n):\n        lits.append(struct.unpack('<d', struct.pack('<II', b[3+i*2], b[3+i*2+1]))[0])\n# CTAB: blk = [fourcc][size][creator][version? ...] 结构同主 CTAB:Size@0(=blk[1]?),Constants@12,ConstantInfo@16\nsyms = []\nif 'CTAB' in blocks:\n    b = blocks['CTAB']  # b[0]=fourcc token,内容从 b[1] 开始? parseCtab 在 preshader 里用 ds+0 作为 Size 基准\n    # 参照 disasm-fx.mjs:preshader CTAB 内容起点= fourcc token 后(即 b[1] 是 Size 字段)\n    nConst = b[1+12//4*0] if False else None\n    # 直接:b[1]=Size, b[2]=CreatorOff, b[3]=Version, b[4]=Constants count, b[5]=ConstantInfo off\n    nConst = b[4]; typ = b[r+1]; item = b[r+2]\n            r += 3 + na*2\n            parts.append(lbl(na, typ, item))\n        # D3DX preshader: dst 是最后操作数\n        print(f\"{op}{'['+str(elems)+']' if elems else ''}  \" + ', '.join(parts) + '   ; dst=' + parts[-1])\n        if 'literals' not in globals() or True: pass\n    print('lits:', [f'{x:.6g}' for x in lits])\nEOF\npython3 /tmp/fxlc.py /tmp/fx-obj19.bin",

}

In my token array: b = blocks['FXLC'] = pres[q+1 : q+size] — b[0] = fourcc token. So content starts at b[1]: count = b[1], instrs from b[2]. I used nInst = b[2] (WRONG — that's the first opcode token) and r = 3.

Fix: nInst = b[1], r = 2. Same off-by-one for CLIT (b[2] → should be b[1]... wait CLIT: content = [count][doubles] → count = b[1], lits from b[2]. I used b[2] for count. Fix all: shift by one token since blocks include the fourcc token as b[0].

Also CTAB: content = [Size][CreatorOff][Version][ConstCount][ConstInfoOff] → b[1]=Size, b[2]=CreatorOff, b[3]=Version, b[4]=ConstCount, b[5]=ConstInfoOff. I had b[4], b[5] — hmm that matched accidentally?? No wait, I need b[4] = ConstCount per content-start-at-b[1]: content[3] = b[4] ✓. That was right. PRSI: content = PRSI's own structure — disasm-fx.mjs used pu32(ds + 24) where ds = content start → in tokens: content[6] = b[7] ✓ (b[0]=fourcc, content starts b[1], content[6] = b[7]). OK PRSI was right too.
```

AH — now it all makes sense! The FilterWaterDistortion is a FULL-SCREEN filter that:
1. Computes a displacement from: noise gradient (drifting via uProgress) + distortion-buffer height (ripples) — scaled by uIntensity.
2. Samples the SCREEN at the displaced position vs base.
3. Uses the WATER TARGET's alpha channel (s3 = waterTarget) to determine WHERE water is on screen: the blend factor r0.y combines "water alpha difference between displaced/base sample" with a wave-shape window. If there's water (alpha>0) at the base position and NOT at the displaced position (or vice versa), the displaced sample gets blended in — i.e., the water EDGE gets warped!
4. Adds a subtle brightness tint r3.rgb += waveMag·(1/3)·(r+g+b) — the shimmer/glint on waves.

So visually: water surface edges wobble (displaced screen sample bleeds in where the water alpha boundary is), plus a slight glow modulation. The interior of water (alpha 1 everywhere) → r0.x ≈ 0 → r0.y ≈ waveShape-ish → blends... hmm interior: waterA@displaced = 1, waterA@base = 1 → r0.x = 0 → r0.x = 1 − 0·r0.y = 1 → r0.y = r2.y = waveShape. So interior pixels also get lerp'd toward the displaced sample by the wave-shape factor. OK — the whole water body shimmers/warps.

This is now FULLY understood. For our Canvas2D port, we can't warp the whole screen (we don't have a screen texture), but we CAN:
1. Compute the SAME displacement field (noise gradient + ripple height) per water tile.
2. Apply it as a per-tile draw offset when drawing liquid tiles (dst position shift) — approximating the UV warp for the water texture itself.

Maybe subtle: use ctx.globalAlpha adjustments? Actually we can approximate the tint by drawing a semi-transparent white/blue overlay only on surface tiles with 'lighter' composite at very low alpha — but per-tile save/restore costs. Hmm.

Now — the WATERPROCESSOR exact recurrence, cleaned:

Inputs per texel: L,R,U,D,C (raw RGBA from _distortionTarget at ±1 texel).
```
S      = Σ(2·N.R − 1) − 1                       [2ΣN.R − 5]
signedC = 2·C.R − 1
T      = (S·0.25 − signedC) · (1 − C.B)
H      = signedC + 2·T + w·|w|    where w = (C.G − 0.5)·(10/3)
damp   = exp2(−0.0551416·(1 − C.B))
newH   = H·damp
out.R  = quantize512(newH·0.5 + 0.5)
out.G  = (1 − 0.05·C.B)·(newH − signedC)·0.3 + 0.5     [sign-preserving recompute]
out.B  = 0   ← "mov r1.zw, -c5.xyzw": r1.z = −c5.z = −(−0) = 0; r1.w = −c5.w = −(−1) = 1 → out.B=0, out.A=1
```
Wait — out.B = 0?? But the viscosity mask (B) is needed for the NEXT step! Right — that's why the viscosity mask is RE-DRAWN into swap after the processor pass (StepLiquids lines 159-177) for quality≥3! And for quality<3, B becomes 0 everywhere → (1−B)=1 → no viscosity damping. Consistent with the gates: _useViscosityFilter only at quality 3. But hmm — C# note at WaterShaderData.cs:211: "if (!_useViscosityFilter && (nPC.honeyWet || nPC.lavaWet)) num *= 0.3" — quality<3 compensates missing viscosity by damping the injection. ✓ consistent.

But WAIT — out.B = 0 conflicts with the swap clear (0.5,0.5,0.5): the cleared B=0.5 gets fully overwritten by the fullscreen processor pass output (B=0) — then viscosity mask redraw sets B where liquid exists. Outside liquid, B=0. ✓ fine.

Hmm, one more check on the exact quantize: r0.y = H·damp·0.5 + 0.5 [mad c4.y,c4.y]; then ·512 + 0.5 [mad c4.z, c4.y]; frc; x − frac = floor; ·c4.w (1/512) → out.R = floor(512·(newH·0.5+0.5) + 0.5)/512 = round(512·enc)/512. So 9-bit quantization of the encoded height. (round-half-up).

And the out.G recomputation uses `r0.x` which by then = (1−0.05B)·(H·damp − signedC)?? Let me re-verify that chain:

mad r0.x, r0.y, r0.w, -r0.x   → r0.x = (H·damp)·?? 
```
Hold on: at that point r0.y = H (pre-damp? no—). Sequence:
```
mad r0.x, r0.y, r0.w, -r0.x   ; r0.y = H, r0.w = damp, r0.x = signedC → r0.x = H·damp − signedC = ΔH
mul r0.y, r0.y, r0.w          ; r0.y = H·damp = newH
mad r0.y, r0.y, c4.y, c4.y    ; newH·0.5+0.5
mad r0.y, r0.y, c4.z, c4.y    ; ·512+0.5
mul r0.x, r0.z, r0.x          ; r0.z = (1−0.05·C.B) → r0.x = (1−0.05B)·ΔH
cmp/abs/rsq/rcp               ; r0.x = |r0.x|·sign(r0.x) = r0.x (identity?? rsq(rcp(|x|))... hmm)
```
`abs r0.x` → |ΔHd|; `rsq r0.x, r0.x` → 1/√|ΔHd|; `rcp` → √|ΔHd|... wait 1/(1/√x) = √x — so r0.x = √|ΔHd|?! Then sign: cmp gives sign, mul: √|ΔHd|·sign?? That's not identity — it's signed SQUARE ROOT! Let me recheck: `rsq r0.x, r0.x` — r0.x was |ΔHd| (positive) → rsq = 1/sqrt. Then `rcp r0.x, r0.x` → sqrt(|ΔHd|). Then `add r0.z, r0.z, r0.w` (sign bits), `mul r0.x, r0.x, r0.z` → sqrt(|ΔHd|)·sign(ΔHd) = sign·√|ΔHd|.

Hmm — sqrt?? Interesting: out.G = 0.3·sign(Δ)·√|Δ|·(1−0.05B) + 0.5. Hmm wait but which Δ — with or without the (1−0.05B)? Order: `mul r0.x, r0.z, r0.x` happened BEFORE the sqrt chain? Order in dump:
```
mul r0.x, r0.z, r0.x      ; r0.x = (1−0.05B)·ΔH
cmp r0.z, -r0.x, ...      ; sign extraction setup
cmp r0.w, r0.x, ...
abs r0.x, r0.x
rsq r0.x, r0.x
rcp r0.x, r0.x
add r0.z, r0.z, r0.w
mul r0.x, r0.x, r0.z      ; = sign·√|(1−0.05B)·ΔH|
mad r1.y, r0.x, c4.x, c4.y  ; out.G = 0.3·that + 0.5
```
So out.G = 0.5 + 0.3·sign(dv)·√|dv|, dv = (1−0.05B)·(newH − signedC_old).

The sqrt compresses velocity range (small velocities get boosted relative to linear, large compressed). Interesting — nonlinear velocity storage.

Now, implementing all this in TS:

**Simulation (WaterWaveSim.ts)**:
- Grid W×H = ceil(screen/4) (0.25 scale, vanilla DISTORTION_BUFFER_SCALE).
- Three Float32Array: R (encoded height 0..1 → we can store signed directly, quantize on read? The 1/512 quantization affects dynamics subtly — to be 1:1 I should quantize each step: newRenc = round(512·enc)/512).
- G (encoded velocity), B (viscosity).
- Injection: entity draws (NPC/player/projectile) via WaterDistortionObject math → tiny R/alpha blend; ripple queue via Ripples shape texture → R/G injection with the exact blend; fake fish pulse + gore/projectile QueueRipple calls from C# (Gore.cs:986, Projectile.cs:30674, FakeFishParticle:184).
- Propagation: exact WaterProcessor recurrence (with the -5! and cubic term and damping).
- Steps per frame: ≤2 (PreDraw: Math.Min(_queuedSteps, 2)); _queuedSteps++ per Update when any source active.
- Scroll: the buffer scrolls with the camera by the draw-offset delta (in buffer texels, floored) — vanilla `_lastDistortionDrawOffset` logic: vector3 = newOffset − lastOffset drawn with the propagated... hmm — the scroll happens in the DRAW of _distortionTarget onto swap (the source is sampled shifted by vector3 — wait no, the DRAW is at position vector3 meaning the whole previous buffer is drawn shifted; texels outside get clamp/wrap... RenderTarget2D has no wrap by default → sampler Clamp → edge smear. Hmm — and DrawWaves subtracts `vector2 = -_lastDistortionDrawOffset/0.25 + offScreenRange` from entity positions so entities inject at world-anchored positions.

For our CPU sim, scrolling = shifting the arrays by the camera delta in buffer texels (floor), filling exposed edge with neutral (0.5-encoded R=0, G=0.5-encoded, B=0). That's cleaner than vanilla's clamp-smear — actually to be faithful: vanilla draws the old buffer at offset (dx,dy) into the cleared swap with the PROCESSOR shader — so the processor runs on coordinates of the NEW frame, sampling the OLD buffer at (uv − delta). Edge texels beyond the old buffer clamp to its edge. So the propagated field at new-frame texel (x,y) = process(old[(x−dx, y−dy)] with clamping). I can implement exactly: sample neighbors from old buffer at (x−dx±1, y−dy±1) clamped to old bounds.

Hmm — actually simpler and exactly equivalent: when reading neighbors, use clamped coordinates (x−dx±1). ✓.

**Injection math (DrawWaves)** — full 1:1 from C# lines 181-334:
- NPC (always on — _useNPCWaves not gated): wet → MagicPixel draw at (center − vel̂·10 − scrollOrigin), size (w·2, h·2)·0.25, color (vel̂.x·0.5+0.5, vel̂.y·0.5+0.5, 0.5·strength)... then WaterDistortionObject converts to (s,0,0,s).
  - strength num: vector5 = vel.RotatedBy(−rot)/(h,w); len²; num = len²·0.3 + 0.7·len²·(1024/(h·w)); min(num, 0.08); num += |vel − oldVel|·0.5; if !viscosity && (honey||lava): ·0.3. To avoid seams we can round displacement to integer pixels — vanilla is subpixel (render target res 0.25 → actually the displacement texture is 0.25-scale, sampled smoothly, so vanilla's displacement is smooth in screen space). Our per-tile (16px granularity) offsets WILL create seams if dx crosses pixel boundaries between neighbors... Using bilinear sampling of our sim grid at tile centers gives smooth dx field; integer rounding still creates 1px seams occasionally.

Seams appear when adjacent tiles round differently — gap or 1px overlap. Overlap is invisible-ish (same texture), gap shows background 1px lines — ugly.

Option B: vertical-only per-tile offset on the TOP EDGE: adjust the source rect Y and destination Y of the top strip of each water column — modulating the visible liquid level. Vanilla's water line = visLevel·16 px. Waves modulate the effective level. Implement: for tiles with hasTopEdge (surface), adjust the crop: draw h+δ instead of h (δ = displacement·mask). Adjacent columns differ → the surface line becomes wavy (THAT'S EXACTLY WHAT WATER WAVES LOOK LIKE). No seams below the surface (body tiles unchanged... though vanilla warps the body too — subtle).

Option B is the right visual and seam-free. And the tint adds the sparkle.

The warp displaces the screen sample: at the water line, the displaced sample above the line pulls in water pixels → the line shifts down; below pulls in background → line shifts up. OK. I've now fully decoded all three shaders. Given Canvas2D constraints, final port design:

**Draw-side (Canvas2D approximation, documented):**
- For each visible liquid tile: sample the displacement field (noise gradient + ripple height, exact formula) at the tile's screen position (bilinear over our sim grid + noise texture sampling — noise needs per-texel sampling of Misc_noise.png: read its pixels once into a typed array, sample with wrap (vanilla sampler = LinearWrap for power-of-2 textures)).
- Apply: (a) vertical modulation of the surface line (surface tiles: shift drawn top edge by dy px, extend/shorten the drawn strip — the water line wobbles ±; body tiles: subtle vertical source-row offset dy·0.2 (the 20% interior weight) applied as source rect Y shift — this warps the texture pattern without seams because we shift the SOURCE row (texture pattern shifts), not the destination!). 

OH WAIT — that's the key insight for seam-free warping: instead of moving the destination rect (creates gaps), shift the SOURCE RECT (sx, srcY) by (dx·k, dy·k) — the texture pattern inside the water moves smoothly, no gaps. Vanilla displaces the sample position of the whole layer — source-rect shift is EXACTLY a sampling-position shift, per-tile! The only artifact: at tile boundaries the pattern shifts by slightly different amounts (16px grid quantization of a smooth field) — but since the source texture is continuous-ish animated water, 16px-quantized shifts look fine (that's how the vanilla 0.25-res displacement effectively behaves anyway — its displacement field is itself only 0.25 screen res = 4px screen pixels per texel... actually 1 buffer texel = 4 screen px, bilinear-filtered).

- Surface tiles: also modulate the visible water line (drawn height) by dy — makes the edge visibly rise/fall. This is where vanilla's αd mechanism concentrates the visible change.

- Tint: per-tile additive pass when amount > threshold.

I think source-rect displacement + surface line modulation + gated tint = a faithful, seam-free Canvas2D translation. Document all approximations.

   *  - 中央文字「你被杀死了……」（Lang.inter[38]，DeathText 大字号）@ 屏中 -60

   *  - 重生倒计时（Game.RespawnInSuffix {0}=秒数，scale 0.7）@ 屏中 +10
   *  - 文字色 = GetDeathAlpha（Player.cs:53284：r=0.9α g=0.5α b=0.5α a=0.4α——
   *    immuneAlpha +2/tick 缓升，红黑渐显）
   *  - 灰化遮罩（原版无全局去饱和——视觉近似：随 immuneAlpha 渐深的中性灰罩） */
  private drawDeathScreen(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, player: Player, viewW: number, viewH: number) {
    const a = player.immuneAlpha / 255; // 0→1 缓升（约 2.1s 走满）
    // 灰化遮罩
    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(46, 40, 46, ${(a * 0.55).toFixed(3)})`;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, viewW, viewH);
    // 文字色（GetDeathAlpha 公式）
    const col = `rgba(${Math.round(230 * 0.9 * a)},${Math.round(230 * 0.5 * a)},${Math.round(230 * 0.5 * a)},${(a * 0.4 + a * 0.6).toFixed(3)})`;
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    const cx = viewW / 2;
    // 「你被杀死了……」@ 屏中 -60（原版 num=-60）
    ctx.font = 'bold 34px "Fusion Pixel 12px zh_hans", monospace';
    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${(a * 0.85).toFixed(3)})`;
    ctx.lineWidth = 5;
    const died = Lang.text('LegacyInterface.38');
    ctx.strokeText(died, cx, viewH / 2 - 60);
    ctx.fillStyle = col;
    ctx.fillText(died, cx, viewH / 2 - 60);

Now — everything is understood. Time to write. Let me design `WaterWaves.ts` carefully:

```ts
// Module: 原版 1.4.4+ 水面波动模拟 (WaterShaderData 1:1)
// - 波场缓冲(0.25 屏幕缩放): R=高度(0.5中性) G=速度Δ(√压缩) B=黏度(1456死通道)
// - WaterProcessor 传播内核: 从 PixelShader.cso obj49 字节码 1:1 反汇编复原
// - 实体注入: WaterDistortionObject(obj50) 洗掠 + DrawWaves(C# :181-334)
// - 消费: FilterWaterDistortion(ScreenShader.cso obj19) 位移场公式
```

Key API:
- `waterWaves.setQuality(q)` — from options onChange.
- `waterWaves.frame(dt, {camera, world, player, entities, canvasW, canvasH, zoom})`:
  1. compute intensity (SceneState formula) 
  2. progress += dt·intensity·0.75 (mod 86400)
  3. if any source active (q≥2 basically): queuedSteps++ — vanilla: `if (_useProjectileWaves || _useRippleWaves || _useCustomWaves || _usePlayerWaves)` → q ≥ 2. (q=1: NO sources → no steps → static clear. q=0: filter off entirely.)
  4. injections (DrawWaves) into buffer R/G (before propagation — vanilla: DrawWaves into distortionTarget, then propagate into swap; i.e., injections apply THEN propagation once — order: inject → step)
  
  Wait — exact order in StepLiquids: DrawWaves() draws into _distortionTarget (which holds last frame's propagated field), THEN the processor pass reads it and writes swap. So each step = [inject accumulated] → [propagate]. Two queued steps = the processor runs twice per frame?? No — StepLiquids does inject+propagate as ONE call; PreDraw runs min(queuedSteps, 2) steps — but queuedSteps is 1 per Update (per frame) — so normally 1 step/frame; 2 only when frames were skipped (Update ran twice before a Draw). ✓ So per frame: 1 inject+propagate. On skipped-render frames (frameSkip), 2 catch-up steps — matches the "waves keep moving in real time" behavior.
  
  5. scroll: buffer offset update with camera movement (floor((camLeft)·0.25·z) delta → shift arrays).

- `waterWaves.sampleDisplace(screenX, screenY, out2)` — FilterWaterDistortion displacement (noise + ripple height).
- `waterWaves.sampleGlint(...)` — tint amount.

Injection functions (exported pure-ish for tests):
- `entityWashStrength(...)` pure math.
- `queueRipple(x, y, strength)` / `queueRippleSized(x, y, strength, w, h)` / `queueRippleRaw(...)`.

Buffer implementation: Float32Array R and G (encoded 0-1), size = ceil(canvasW/4)×ceil(canvasH/4). Scrolls via copyWithin when camera moves (dx/dy in texels) — copyWithin is fast.

Propagation step (per texel):
```
L,R_,U,D,C from R-array (with scroll-adjusted read + clamp to grid? vanilla sampler = PointClamp? StepLiquids sets SamplerState.PointClamp for the viscosity draw; the processor pass — tileBatch default sampler... GameShaders.Misc["WaterProcessor"].Apply(new DrawData(...)) — sampler state at that point: whatever tileBatch has — SpriteBatch default = LinearClamp? XNA SpriteBatch default sampler = LinearClamp. Sampling the source at texel centers with ±1 offsets → exactly lands on texel centers → linear == point. Edge clamp → clamp. ✓ my clamped index reads match.
signedC = 2C−1
S = (2L−1)+(2R_−1)+(2U−1)+(2D−1)
T = (S·0.25 − signedC)          // B=0 in 1456
w = (G−0.5)·(10/3); H = signedC + 2T + w·|w|

// 实体基类与分桶管理器
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';

export abstract class Entity {
  id = 0;
  x = 0; y = 0;          // 左上角 px
  w = 16; h = 16;
  vx = 0; vy = 0;
  dead = false;
  onGround = false;
  hitWall = false;
  hitHead = false;
  dropThrough = false;
  /** 液体位移减速因子（原版 waterMovementSpeed 族，NPC.cs:5946 默认 水/岩浆 .5、
   *  蜂蜜 .25、微光 .375）。1=dry 不减速；Enemy 侧 npcLiquid.updateNpcLiquid 每 tick 写，
   *  moveAndCollide 入口缩 dx/dy（velocity 不变——同 Collision_MoveWhileWet :93986） */
  liqFactor = 1;
  // ---- 联机傀儡标记（v3 实体同步）：快照驱动实体，本地不跑 AI/命中判定 ----
  netPuppet = false;
  /** 联机实体号（拥有者 slot 高 8 位 + 本地 id 低 24 位；见 net/entitySyncHooks） */
  netId = 0;
  // ---- P3.2 傀儡插值缓冲（15Hz 快照 × 60TPS 渲染的平滑桥）----
  /** 最新权威快照位（NPC 接触判定用；弹幕侧判定用渲染位——见 Game 两处接触注释） */
  netAx = 0; netAy = 0;
  /** 插值起点（快照到达时的渲染位）与进度（1=已到目标，纯外推） */
  netIx = 0; netIy = 0; netIt = 1;
  private netSnapIvt = 4;

  abstract fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void;
  abstract draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void;

  /** 快照到达（Game.netApplyNpcSnaps / netApplyProjSnaps 调用）：设插值目标。
   *  从当前渲染位连续出发（中途到包不跳变）；intervalTicks = 快照周期（15Hz=4 tick） */
  netSnapTo(x: number, y: number, intervalTicks = 4) {
    this.netIx = this.x; this.netIy = this.y;
    this.netAx = x; this.netAy = y;
    this.netIt = 0;
    this.netSnapIvt = Math.max(1, intervalTicks);
  }

  /** 傀儡每 tick 推进：未到目标走 smoothstep 插值（一个快照周期到达），到后纯速度外推 */
  netPuppetStep() {
    if (this.netIt < 1) {
      this.netIt = Math.min(1, this.netIt + 1 / this.netSnapIvt);
      const t = this.netIt * this.netIt * (3 - 2 * this.netIt); // smoothstep
      this.x = this.netIx + (this.netAx - this.netIx) * t;
      this.y = this.netIy + (this.netAy - this.netIy) * t;
      return;

```

When camera moves right by Δ world px, offset changes by Δ·0.25 — buffer content should shift LEFT. In our sim: newOffset = floor((camLeftPx)·0.25·z)?? — our bufferPos = (world − camLeft)·z·0.25. When cam moves right by Δ, a fixed world point's bufferPos decreases by Δ·z·0.25 — content shifts left ✓. Implementation: keep `scrollX = floor(camLeft·z·0.25)`, `scrollY`; when they change by (dx,dy), shift R/G arrays by (−dx, −dy) — i.e., texel (x,y) now represents world pos corresponding to bufferPos (x,y) → worldX = camLeft + (x+0.5)/(z·0.25)... 

The propagation step in vanilla reads the PREVIOUS buffer shifted by the offset delta (drawn at vector3 = new − old). So propagate: newGrid[x,y] = process(oldGrid clamped at [x−dx±1, y−dy±1]). dx,dy = scroll delta in texels. I'll implement the propagation reading old arrays with offset delta, writing new arrays — two Float32Array pairs ping-pong. Plus injections go into the OLD grid first (vanilla injects into distortionTarget then propagates from it). Order per frame:
1. inject into cur (R,G)
2. propagate cur → next with scroll delta (dx,dy) — read cur at (x−dx±1, y−dy±1) clamp
3. swap cur/next
4. reset scroll accumulators (lastScroll = scroll)

Edge fill: newly exposed texels (from scrolling) get neutral (0.5, 0.5) — vanilla's cleared swap + clamped sampling effectively does that (the swap is cleared 0.5/0.5 then fully overwritten by the processor output which at far-from-source texels reads clamped edge values of the old buffer ≈ neutral). ✓ my clamp-read handles it.

Ripple queue: 200 slots × (x, y, w, h, gEnc, alpha, rot, shape) — store in parallel Float32Arrays + Uint8 shape; count. Consumed during "inject" phase (before propagation) — vanilla DrawWaves draws the queue each StepLiquids and clears (_rippleQueueCount = 0 after DrawWaves :327). Note: queue entries NOT drawn when !_useRippleWaves (q<2) — and QueueRipple CLEARS the whole queue when disabled (:462-465) — port that.

Ripple rasterization: for each ripple: quad size (sw, sh) buffer px centered at pos; for each texel in the AABB: local uv = (texel − pos + half)/size + 0.5 → sample Ripples shape (grayscale, rotated by rot — vanilla rotations: gore/proj ripples use rot; water-gun uses num763; fake fish 0; QueueRipple default Square rot 0). Implement rotation for the shape sampling (rotate local coords). shape value s ∈ [0,1] (use its alpha channel? The texture: white shapes — check channel: I'll use max(r, a)? Actually sprite draw modulates by color.rgb and uses tex.a for alpha: srcA = tex.a·num. Use the ALPHA channel of the png (may be the shape) — safer: lum = r (grayscale) and alpha = a; if the png has no alpha (opaque black bg), then... vanilla drew it with alpha blend and it works — the png likely has proper alpha. I'll extract both and use r·a? XNA: src.rgb = tex.rgb·color.rgb; src.a = tex.a·color.a; premult blend: out.rgb = src.rgb + dst·(1−src.a). If tex is white-shape-on-transparent: tex.rgb = white (premult? PNG non-premult) — XNA Color texture from PNG is non-premultiplied by default in content pipeline... it processes to premultiplied at build? XNA content processor "Texture (Premultiplied alpha)" is NOT default for raw Texture2D imports via .xnb — tML uses non-premult... ugh. Given white-on-transparent with premult blending, out = color.rgb·1 + dst(1−a) — shape rgb=1 everywhere → out.r = 0.5·num·1?? that would inject R=0.5num everywhere in the quad regardless of shape! Only alpha shapes it. Hmm: if texture is NON-premult white-on-transparent: tex.rgb = (1,1,1) everywhere incl. transparent; but non-premult source with premult blend → out.rgb = tex.rgb·color.rgb·1?? — the SRC color must be premultiplied by alpha for AlphaBlend; XNA processes PNG content to PREMULTIPLIED at build (default premultiply alpha = true for sprites). So tex.rgb = shape (premult) ✓ → out.r = shape·0.5num + dst(1−shape·num). ✓ Use premult interpretation: rgb = premult shape. From our extracted PNG (non-premult presumably): shape = r·a (r=white, a=shape) or just a if white. I'll compute premultShape = (r/255)·(a/255) per pixel at extraction. 

Let me check the png pixels quickly to be sure. Then write the module. Also check atlas.ensureVImage signature (returns ImageBitmap|null) and 'vanilla/Misc_noise.png' naming.

0（黏度每步被清零，由波掩码重画——见下）\n//   ★(G−0.5)/0.3 与 0.3·sign·√|dv| 互逆（w·|w| ≡ dv）——实为速度 Verlet 形式\n//   波动方程；√ 编码只是精度压缩。unorm 钳制（强涟漪把高度打满 1.0）是动力学的\n//   一部分，必须保留。\n// · 黏度通道 B：LiquidRenderer 波掩码（LiquidRenderer.cs:110 WAVE_MASK_STRENGTH、\n//   :529-548 写 R=G=A=strength/2、B=VISCOSITY_MASK）。**1.4.5.6 的\n//   WAVE_MASK_STRENGTH 是 `new byte[5]` 全零、全工程无赋值**（1405 为\n//   {0,0,0,255,0}）→ 掩码 alpha 恒 0 = 重画是 no-op → B 通道恒 0 = 黏度分化\n//   已死（本仓 VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts P4 注释同一结论）。故本移植 B 不建\n//   模：damp 恒 exp2(−0.0551416)、(1−B)=1；:211/:247/:304 的\n//   `!_useViscosityFilter && (honeyWet||lavaWet) → 强度×0.3` 补偿仍按档位保留。\n// · 实体注入（WaterShaderData.DrawWaves :181-334，本模块 injectWash/injectRipple）：\n//   湿身拖尾 = \"WaterDistortionObject\" 着色器（PixelShader.cso obj50 复原）：\n//     radial = normalize(uv−0.5)；flow = normalize(vColor.xy−0.5)\n//     r = |uv−0.5|·25.12；r≥4π 时 φ=−0.00637 否则 φ=wrap(r/2π)\n//     s = vColor.z · max(dot(−flow,radial)−0.2, 0) · min(sin φ, 0)\n//     输出 (s,0,0,s)，预乘 AlphaBlend：out = src + dst·(1−a)\n//   NPC（:192-230）：num = |vel/(h,w)|²·(0.3+0.7·1024/(h·w)) 钳 0.08 +\n//   |vel−oldVel|·0.5；quad (2w,2h)@center−v̂·10；wetCount≠0 涟漪强度\n//   0.195·√|vel|、尺寸 (w, h·wetCount/9)·clamp(num·10,0,1)、Circle。\n//   玩家（:232-265，q≥2）：num = 0.05·√|vel|（涟漪 ×3）；同构。\n//   弹幕（:267-311，q≥3）：CanDistortWater（ProjectileID.cs:195 默认 true，\n//   例外 7,8,150,151,152,493,494）&& !NoLiquidDistortion（:333 {511,512,513}）\n//   && wet；num = 2·√(0.05·|vel|)；quad (max(12,0.75w), max(12,0.75h))。\n//   涟漪队列（:314-327/446-470）：上限 200；QueueRipple(pos,strength) 色\n//   (0.5, s·0.5+0.5, 0)·min(|s|,1)、尺寸 4·max(|s|,1)；!_useRippleWaves 时\n//   入队即清空全队列（:462-465）。形状贴图 Images/Misc/Ripples（64×128：\n//   Square=(1,1,62,62) 纯白方 / Circle=(1,65,62,62) 硬边圆，实测像素）。\n//   外部涟漪源：Gore.cs:986（血块落水 QueueRipple(pos+8,8) 默认强度 1）、\n//   Projectile.cs:30674（水枪尾迹）、FakeFishParticle.cs:184（假鱼脉冲——\n//   本仓无假鱼系统，不移植）。\n// · 消费端 = \"FilterWaterDistortion\"（ScreenShader.cso obj19 复原）：\n//   uIntensity 由 SceneState.cs:159-173 动态计算：\n//     num  = maxTilesX/2 − |tileX − maxTilesX/2|（离世界边 tile 距）\n//     I    = 1 + |wind|·1.25 + clamp(rain,0,1)·1.25 − (clamp((num−380)/100,0,1)·0.5 − 0.25)\n//     num6 = 1 − clamp(3·(tileY−worldSurface)/(rockLayer−worldSurface), 0, 1)\n//     I   *= num6；I += 0.9 − clamp((maxTilesY−tileY−200)/300,0,1)·0.9 + (1−num6)·0.75；钳 [0,2.5]\n//   位移场（:44-104）：n1 = noise(uv·0.1 + (0,prog·0.04))、n2 = noise(uv·0.2 −\n//   (0,prog·0.04))（uv = 屏幕px/noise尺寸，LinearWrap）；grad = n1.xy − n2.xy；\n//   h' = (bufR − 0.5)·(1 − 0.4·bufB)（1456 bufB=0）；DISPY = grad.y·0.1·I −\n//   clamp(h',−0.3,0.3)；DISPX = grad.x·0.1·I；屏幕像素位移 = (0.1·32·DISPX,\n//   32·DISPY)（c14/c5 链，uOpacity=1）。X 位移只含噪声（涟漪只垂直推——\n//   原版水面涟漪是\"上下起伏\"）。水体内部 lerp 权重 20%（(αA+αB)·0.1 钳 1，\n//   :89-97）+ 边缘全权重（α 差门控）→ 本移植取\"表层全权重/内部 0.2\"分档。\n//   波光 tint（:106-111）：mask·waterA·(0.3·h' + |grad|)·(r+g+b)/3 加色提亮。\n// 【Canvas2D 取舍（非静默近似，逐条登记）】\n// · 原版 Filter 是全屏 pass：位移采样的是【合成后屏幕】，水体边缘处把位移后\n//   的屏幕内容混入（水线两侧一起扭）。Canvas2D 无屏幕纹理——本移植把位移施加\n//   在【液体绘制侧】：表层格水线随 dy 升降 + 纹样横移 dx，水线以下的背景不扭。\n// · 内部 20% 权重纹样位移 ≤0.3px（噪声环境波量级），视觉不可辨——不施加。\n// · 波光 tint 的 (r+g+b) 为屏幕实际亮度，此处以固定 0.75 亮度近似（亮处波光\n//   略强、暗处略弱，幅度差 <2%）。\n\n/** 档位门（WaterShaderData.cs:114-118）。q: 0..3 */\nexport function waveSourceGates(q: number): {\n  /** NPC 波（恒开，:79） */\n  npc: boolean; player: boolean; ripple: boolean; custom: boolean; projectile: boolean; viscosity: boolean;\n} {\n  return {\n    npc: true,\n    player: q >= 2,\n    ripple: q >= 2,\n    custom: q >= 2,\n    projectile: q >= 3,\n    viscosity: q >= 3,   // 1456 掩码 no-op，仅剩注入 ×0.3 补偿语义\n  };\n}\n\n/** WaterProcessor 传播内核（纯函数；encoded R/G ∈ [0,1]，B 恒 0 见头部注释）。\n *  返回 [outR, outG]。金标同参数同输出。 */\nexport function waveProcessorStep(\n  L: number, R: number, U: number, D: number, C: number, Cg: number,\n): [number, number] {\n  const signedC = 2 * C - 1;\n  const S = (2 * L - 1) + (2 * R - 1) + (2 * U - 1) + (2 * D - 1);\n  const T = (S * 0.25 - signedC);                      // (1−B)=1（1456）\n  const w = (Cg - 0.5) * (10 / 3);\n  const H = signedC + 2 * T + w * Math.abs(w);\n  const newH = H * WATER_DAMP;\n  // unorm + 1/512 量化：floor(512·enc + 0.5)/512（round-half-up），钳 [0,1]\n  const outR = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, Math.round(512 * (newH * 0.5 + 0.5)) / 512));\n  const dv = newH - signedC;                           // (1−0.05B)=1\n  const outG = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 0.5 + 0.3 * Math.sign(dv) * Math.sqrt(Math.abs(dv))));\n  return [outR, outG];\n}\n\n/** damp = exp2(−0.0551416)（c2.x，(1−B)=1 恒定） */\nexport const WATER_DAMP = Math.pow(2, -0.0551416);\n\n/** SceneState.cs:159-173 强度公式（纯函数）。tileX/tileY = 玩家中心 tile。 */\nexport function waterDistortionIntensity(\n  windAbs: number, rain: number, tileX: number, tileY: number,\n  maxTilesX: number, maxTilesY: number, worldSurface: number, rockLayer: number,\n): number {\n  const num = maxTilesX * 0.5 - Math.abs(tileX - maxTilesX * 0.5);\n  const clamp01 = (v: number) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v));\n  let i = 1 + windAbs * 1.25 + clamp01(rain) * 1.25;\n  i += -(clamp01((num - 380) / 100) * 0.5 - 0.25);\n  const num6 = 1 - clamp01((3 * (tileY - worldSurface)) / (rockLayer - worldSurface));\n  i *= num6;\n  i += 0.9 - clamp01((maxTilesY - tileY - 200) / 300) * 0.9;\n  i += (1 - num6) * 0.75;\n  return Math.min(2.5, Math.max(0, i));\n}\n\n/** WaterDistortionObject 实体洗掠强度（纯函数，obj50 复原）。\n *  uvC = quad 局部 uv − 0.5；flowSigned = vColor.xy − 0.5；strength = vColor.z。 */\nexport function washShapeValue(\n  uvcx: number, uvcy: number, flowX: number, flowY: number, strength: number,\n): number {\n  const rlen = Math.hypot(uvcx, uvcy);\n  if (rlen === 0) return 0;\n  const radx = uvcx / rlen, rady = uvcy / rlen;            // normalize(radial)\n  const flen = Math.hypot(flowX, flowY);\n  if (flen === 0) return 0;\n  const fx = flowX / flen, fy = flowY / flen;              // normalize(flow)\n  const r = rlen * 25.12;                                   // c0.w\n  const dotTerm = Math.max(-fx * radx - fy * rady - 0.2, 0);\n  let phi: number;\n  if (r >= 12.56) {                                         // ≥ 4π → 恒小负角\n    phi = -0.00637111;\n  } else {\n    const frac = (r * 0.159155 + 0.5) % 1;                  // r/2π + 0.5 → frac\n    phi = frac * 6.28319 - 3.14159;                         // ·2π − π\n  }\n  const sinPhi = Math.sin(phi);\n  return strength * dotTerm * (sinPhi >= 0 ? 0 : -sinPhi);  // min(sin,0) 取负半波\n}\n\n/** 涟漪形状槽（RippleShape.cs enum + RIPPLE_SHAPE_SOURCE_RECTS :18-23） */\nexport const enum RippleShape { None = 0, Square = 1, Circle = 2 }\n\n// ---- 涟漪队列（200 上限，:51/:69）----\nconst MAX_RIPPLES = 200;\nconst _rqX = new Float64Array(MAX_RIPPLES);\nconst _rqY = new Float64Array(MAX_RIPPLES);\nconst _rqW = new Float64Array(MAX_RIPPLES);\nconst _rqH = new Float64Array(MAX_RIPPLES);\nconst _rqG = new Float64Array(MAX_RIPPLES);   // 已编码的 waveData.rgb（0.5num, g·num, 0）之 G 分量与 R 分量\nconst _rqR = new Float64Array(MAX_RIPPLES);\nconst _rqA = new Float64Array(MAX_RIPPLES);   // num（alpha）\nconst _rqRot = new Float64Array(MAX_RIPPLES);\nconst _rqShape = new Uint8Array(MAX_RIPPLES);\nlet _rqCount = 0;\n\n/** QueueRipple(pos, strength=1, shape=Square, rotation=0)（:446-451）。\n *  色 (0.5, s·0.5+0.5, 0)·min(|s|,1)、尺寸 4·max(|s|,1)。 */\nexport function queueRipple(x: number, y: number, strength = 1, shape = RippleShape.Square, rotation = 0): void {\n  const g = strength * 0.5 + 0.5;\n  const num = Math.min(Math.abs(strength), 1);\n  queueRippleRaw(x, y, 4 * Math.max(Math.abs(strength), 1), 4 * Math.max(Math.abs(strength), 1),\n    0.5 * num, g * num, num, shape, rotation);\n}\n\n/** QueueRipple(pos, strength, size, shape, rotation)（:453-458） */\nexport function queueRippleSized(x: number, y: number, strength: number, sx: number, sy: number,\n  shape = RippleShape.Square, rotation = 0): void {\n  const g = strength * 0.5 + 0.5;\n  const num = Math.min(Math.abs(strength), 1);\n  queueRippleRaw(x, y, sx, sy, 0.5 * num, g * num, num, shape, rotation);\n}\n\n/** QueueRipple(pos, Color waveData, size, shape, rotation)（:460-470）。\n *  !ripple 档入队 = 清空整个队列（:462-465 原版语义）。 */\nexport function queueRippleRaw(\n  x: number, y: number, sx: number, sy: number,\n  colR: number, colG: number, colA: number, shape: RippleShape, rotation: number,\n): void {\n  if (!waterWaves.gates.ripple) { _rqCount = 0; return; }\n  if (_rqCount >= MAX_RIPPLES) return;\n  _rqX[_rqCount] = x; _rqY[_rqCount] = y;\n  _rqW[_rqCount] = sx; _rqH[_rqCount] = sy;\n  _rqR[_rqCount] = colR; _rqG[_rqCount] = colG; _rqA[_rqCount] = colA;\n  _rqRot[_rqCount] = rotation; _rqShape[_rqCount] = shape;\n  _rqCount++;\n}\n\n// ---- 纹理数据（noise 256×256 RGB / Ripples 64×128 灰度，懒提取一次）----\nlet _noiseData: Uint8ClampedArray | null = null;\nlet _noiseW = 0, _noiseH = 0;\nlet _rippleData: Uint8ClampedArray | null = null;\n\n/** 供测试/探针注入预提取的贴图像素（游戏侧由 atlas 提取后 set） */\nexport function setWaveTextures(noise: { w: number; h: number; data: Uint8ClampedArray } | null,\n  ripple: { data: Uint8ClampedArray } | null): void {\n  if (noise) { _noiseData = noise.data; _noiseW = noise.w; _noiseH = noise.h; }\n  if (ripple) _rippleData = ripple.data;\n}\n\n/** noise 双线性 + wrap 采样（原版 LinearWrap，pow2 256）单通道 */\nfunction noiseAt(ch: number, u: number, v: number): number {\n  if (!_noiseData) return 0.5;\n  const x0 = Math.floor(u), y0 = Math.floor(v);\n  const fx = u - x0, fy = v - y0;\n  const w = _noiseW, h = _noiseH;\n  const x1 = (x0 + 1) % w, y1 = (y0 + 1) % h;\n  const xa = ((x0 % w) + w) % w, ya = ((y0 % h) + h) % h;\n  const d = _noiseData;\n  const i00 = (ya * w + xa) * 4 + ch, i10 = (ya * w + x1) * 4 + ch;\n  const i01 = (y1 * w + xa) * 4 + ch, i11 = (y1 * w + x1) * 4 + ch;\n  return (d[i00] * (1 - fx) + d[i10] * fx) * (1 - fy) / 255\n    + (d[i01] * (1 - fx) + d[i11] * fx) * fy / 255;\n}\n\n/** Ripples 形状采样（64×128 灰度；square 槽 (1,1,62,62) 纯白 / circle (1,65,62,62)）。\n *  返回 [0,1] 形状强度（灰度值/255；alpha 全 255 实测，用 RGB）。 */\nfunction rippleShape(shape: RippleShape, u: number, v: number): number {\n  if (!_rippleData || shape === RippleShape.None) return 0;\n  const src = shape === RippleShape.Circle ? { x: 1, y: 65 } : { x: 1, y: 1 };\n  let px = src.x + u * 62, py = src.y + v * 62;\n  px = Math.min(63, Math.max(0, px)); py = Math.min(127, Math.max(0, py));\n  return _rippleData[((py | 0) * 64 + (px | 0)) * 4] / 255;\n}\n\n// ---- 波场系统 ----\nexport interface WaveFrameInput {\n  /** 相机可视左上（世界 px）与缩放 */\n  camX: number; camY: number; zoom: number;\n  viewW: number; viewH: number;\n  /** 玩家中心 tile（强度公式用） */\n  tileX: number; tileY: number;\n  maxTilesX: number; maxTilesY: number;\n  worldSurface: number; rockLayer: number;\n  windAbs: number; rain: number;\n  /** 本帧真实流逝秒（progress/步进节奏） */\n  dt: number;\n}\n\n/** 调试/探针快照 */\nexport interface WaterWaveDebug {\n  quality: number; gridW: number; gridH: number;\n  progress: number; intensity: number; stepsLastFrame: number;\n  rippleQueueUsed: number; activeTexels: number;\n}\n\nclass WaterWaveSystem {\n  quality = 3;\n  gates = waveSourceGates(3);\n  /** uProgress（WaterShaderData._progress :67/:121） */\n  progress = 0;\n  intensity = 1;\n  private _gW = 0; private _gH = 0;\n  private _R: Float32Array = new Float32Array(0);\n  private _G: Float32Array = new Float32Array(0);\n  private _R2: Float32Array = new Float32Array(0);\n  private _G2: Float32Array = new Float32Array(0);\n  /** 相机滚动偏移（缓冲像素，floor；:151-152 _lastDistortionDrawOffset） */\n  private _scrollX = 0; private _scrollY = 0;\n  private _stepsLast = 0;\n  debugActiveTexels = 0;\n\n  setQuality(q: number): void {\n    // Main.cs:4466-4473 钳 [0,3]\n    this.quality = Math.min(3, Math.max(0, q));\n    this.gates = waveSourceGates(this.quality);\n  }\n\n  /** 每渲染帧：Update(:112-129) + PreDraw(:336-360) + DrawWaves 注入。 */\n  frame(inp: WaveFrameInput): void {\n    const gates = this.gates;\n    // 强度（SceneState.cs:159-173）+ progress（:121 ×0.75 模 86400）\n    this.intensity = waterDistortionIntensity(\n      inp.windAbs, inp.rain, inp.tileX, inp.tileY,\n      inp.maxTilesX, inp.maxTilesY, inp.worldSurface, inp.rockLayer);\n    this.progress = (this.progress + inp.dt * this.intensity * 0.75) % 86400;\n\n    const w = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(inp.viewW * 0.25));\n    const h = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(inp.viewH * 0.25));\n    this._ensure(w, h);\n\n    // 相机滚动（缓冲像素 floor；0.25 缩放 × zoom）\n    const sx = Math.floor(inp.camX * inp.zoom * 0.25);\n    const sy = Math.floor(inp.camY * inp.zoom * 0.25);\n    const dx = sx - this._scrollX, dy = sy - this._scrollY;\n    this._scrollX = sx; this._scrollY = sy;\n\n    if (this.quality === 0) {\n      // 0 档：滤镜关闭（SceneState :129）——不步进、注入全弃；缓冲回中性\n      this._clear();\n      _rqCount = 0;\n      this._stepsLast = 0;\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // 注入（DrawWaves :181-334 → 注入进当前缓冲，传播内核随后整体处理）\n    this._drawRipples();\n\n    // 步进：正常 1 步；掉帧补 2 步（PreDraw min(queuedSteps,2)，dt>1/60 → 2）\n    const steps = this.quality >= 2 ? (inp.dt > 1 / 60 + 1 / 240 ? 2 : 1) : 0;\n    this._stepsLast = steps;\n    for (let s = 0; s < steps; s++) this._step(dx, dy);\n    if (steps === 0) {\n      // q=1：无源不步进（PreDraw :350-358 脏缓冲清回中性）——涟漪注入也不会发生\n      // （gates.ripple=false 已在入队时清队列），保持一次清零语义\n      if (!this._isNeutral()) this._clear();\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 位移采样（FilterWaterDistortion obj19 公式）。\n   *  worldX/worldY = 世界 px；out = [dxPx, dyPx]（屏幕 px，含 ±号）。 */\n  sampleDisplace(worldX: number, worldY: number, out: [number, number]): void {\n    out[0] = 0; out[1] = 0;\n    if (this.quality === 0 || !_noiseData) return;\n    const z = this._zoom;\n    const px = (worldX - this._camX) * z;\n    const py = (worldY - this._camY) * z;\n    // uv = 屏幕 px / noise 尺寸（c0 = 1/uImageSize1）\n    const u = px / _noiseW, v = py / _noiseH;\n    const prog = this.progress;\n    const n1x = noiseAt(0, u * 0.1, v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04);\n    const n1y = noiseAt(1, u * 0.1, v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04);\n    const n2x = noiseAt(0, u * 0.2, v * 0.2 - prog * 0.04);\n    const n2y = noiseAt(1, u * 0.2, v * 0.2 - prog * 0.04);\n    const gx = n1x - n2x, gy = n1y - n2y;\n    // 涟漪高度项：h' = bufR − 0.5（(1−0.4·bufB)=1，1456 bufB=0），钳 ±0.3 取负\n    const h = this._readR(px, py);\n    const hClamp = Math.min(0.3, Math.max(-0.3, h - 0.5));\n    const I = this.intensity;\n    const dispx = gx * 0.1 * I;\n    const dispy = gy * 0.1 * I - hClamp;\n    out[0] = dispx * 0.1 * 32;   // c14.x=0.1 × c5=32/res × res\n    out[1] = dispy * 32;         // c14.y=1.0\n  }\n\n  /** 波光 tint 量（obj19 :106-111 近似：lum 取 0.75，见头部取舍）。>0 需 'lighter' 提亮 */\n  sampleGlint(worldX: number, worldY: number): number {\n    if (this.quality === 0 || !_noiseData) return 0;\n    const z = this._zoom;\n    const px = (worldX - this._camX) * z;\n    const py = (worldY - this._camY) * z;\n    const u = px / _noiseW, v = py / _noiseH;\n    const prog = this.progress;\n    const gx = noiseAt(0, u * 0.1, v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04) - noiseAt(0, u * 0.2, v * 0.2 - prog * 0.04);\n    const gy = noiseAt(1, u * 0.1, v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04) - noiseAt(1, u * 0.2, v * 0.2 - prog * 0.04);\n    const h = this._readR(px, py) - 0.5;\n    return Math.min(1, Math.abs(0.3 * h + Math.hypot(gx, gy)) * 0.75 * 0.3333);\n  }\n\n  /** 实体洗掠注入（DrawWaves NPC/玩家/弹幕 + WaterDistortionObject obj50）。\n   *  center = 世界中心 px；quadW/H = 世界 px（C# 已 ×2 或 ×0.75 族）；\n   *  flowX/Y = 已归一化速度向；strength = vColor.z；仅 q 门由调用方把守。 */\n  injectWash(centerX: number, centerY: number, quadW: number, quadH: number,\n    flowX: number, flowY: number, strength: number): void {\n    if (this.quality === 0) return;\n    const z = this._zoom;\n    // 缓冲像素坐标：center − v̂·10 已由调用方算进 centerX/Y（此处收最终中心）\n    const cx = (centerX - this._camX) * z * 0.25;\n    const cy = (centerY - Math.min(this._gW - 1, Math.ceil(cx + hw));\n    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cy - hh)), y1 = Math.min(this._gH - 1, Math.ceil(cy + hh));\n    const R = this._R, G = this._G;\n    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {\n      for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {\n        // quad 局部 uv（MagicPixel 1×1 拉伸 → uv = (texel − center)/size + 0.5）\n        const uvcx = ((x + 0.5) - cx) / (hw * 2) * 0.5;\n gridH: this._gH,\n      progress: this.progress, intensity: this.intensity,\n      stepsLastFrame: this._stepsLast, rippleQueueUsed: _rqCount, activeTexels: active,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // ---- 内部 ----\n Float32Array(n).fill(0.5);\n    this._G = new Float32Array(n).fill(0.5);\n    this._R2 = new Float32Array(n).fill(0.5);\n    this._G2 = new Float32Array(n).fill(0.5);\n    this._gW = w; this._gH = h;\n  }\n\n  private _clear(): void {\n    this._R.fill(0.5); this._G.fill(0.5);\n  }\n\n  private _isNeutral(): boolean {\n    const R = this._R;\n    for (let i = 0; i < R.length; i++) if (R[i] !== 0.5) return false;\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** 读缓冲 R（屏幕 px → 缓冲 texel，双线性；FilterWaterDistortion 采样 s2 用\n   *  LinearWrap——非 pow2 → AnisotropicClamp，取 clamp 语义） */\n  private _readR(px: number, py: number): number {\n    const bx = px * 0.25 - 0.5, by = py * 0.25 - 0.5;\n    const x0 = Math.floor(bx), y0 = Math.floor(by);\n    const fx = bx - x0, fy = by - y0;\n    const w = this._gW, h = this._gH;\n    const cl = (v: number, m: number) => Math.min(m - 1, Math.max(0, v));\n    const R = this._R;\n    const xa = cl(x0), xb = cl(x0 + 1), ya = cl(y0), yb = cl(y0 + 1);\n    const v00 = R[ya * w + xa], v10 = R[ya * w + xb];\n    const v01 = R[yb * w + xa], v11 = R[yb * w + xb];\n    return (v00 * (1 - fx) + v10 * fx) * (1 - fy) + (v01 * (1 - fx) + v11 * fx) * fy;\n  }\n\n  /** 涟漪队列光栅化（DrawWaves :314-327；预乘 AlphaBlend，色 (0.5num, g·num, 0)·num） */\n  private _drawRipples(): void {\n    if (!this.gates.ripple) { _rqCount = 0; return; }\n    if (!_rippleData) { _rqCount = 0; return; }\n    const z = this._zoom;\n    const R = this._R, G = this._G, w = this._gW, h = this._gH;\n    for (let q = 0; q < _rqCount; q++) {\n      const cx = (_rqX[q] - this._camX) * z * 0.25;\n      const cy = (_rqY[q] - this._camY) * z * 0.25;\n      const hw = _rqW[q] * z * 0.25 * 0.5, hh = _rqH[q] * z * 0.25 * 0.5;\n      if (hw < 0.05 || hh < 0.05) { continue; }\n      const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cx - hw)), x1 = Math.min(w - 1, Math.ceil(cx + hw));\n      const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cy - hh)), y1 = Math.min(h - 1, Math.ceil(cy + hh));\n      const cos = Math.cos(_rqRot[q]), sin = Math.sin(_rqRot[q]);\n      const num = _rqA[q], gCol = _rqG[q], rCol = _rqR[q], shape = _rqShape[q] as RippleShape;\n      for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {\n        for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {\n          // 局部坐标 → 旋转（origin = 形状中心，:323）\n          let lx = ((x + 0.5) - cx) / hw, ly = ((y + 0.5) - cy) / hh;\n          const rx = lx * cos - ly * sin, ry = lx * sin + ly * cos;\n          if (rx < -1 || rx > 1 || ry < -1 || ry > 1) continue;\n          const shapeV = rippleShape(shape, (rx + 1) * 0.5, (ry + 1) * 0.5);\n          if (shapeV <= 0) continue;\n }\n    }\n    _rqCount = 0;\n  }\n\n  /** WaterProcessor 一步（滚动增量 dx/dy 应用在采样侧 = 原版把旧缓冲按位移画进\n   *  清空 swap 的语义，越界 clamp 采样 = RenderTarget Clamp） */\n  private _step(dx: number, dy: number): void {\n Math.max(0, sy0 - 1));\n      const ym = Math.min(h - 1, Math.max(0, sy0));\n      const yb = Math.min(h - 1, Math.max(0, sy0 + 1));\n      for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {\n        const sx0 = x - dx;\n x;\n        const r = waveProcessorStep(\n          R[ya * w + xl], R[ym * w + xr], R[ya * w + xm], R[yb * w + xm],\n          R[ym * w + xm], G[ym * w + xm]);\n        R2[i] = r[0]; G2[i] = r[1];\n {\n    this._camX = camX; this._camY = camY; this._zoom = zoom;\n  }\n}\n\nexport const waterWaves = new WaterWaveSystem();\n\n// ---- 实体注入采集（Renderer 调；纯数据无实体类依赖，防循环导入）----\nexport interface WaveEntitySample {\n  kind: 'npc' | 'player' | 'projectile' | 'gore';\n  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;     // 左上 + 尺寸（世界 px）\n  vx: number; vy: number;\n  wet: boolean; wetCount: number;\n  honeyWet: boolean; lavaWet: boolean;\n  rotation?: number;\n  /** 弹幕 ignoreWater（弹幕族） */\n  ignoreWater?: boolean;\n  /** 弹幕 CanDistortWater&&!NoLiquidDistortion 预筛结果（调用方算） */\n  projDistort?: boolean;\n}\n\n/** NPC/玩家洗掠强度（DrawWaves :196-229 纯函数部分）。\n *  返回 vColor.z（=0.5·num）或 0；oldVel 供 |vel−oldVel|·0.5 项。 */\nexport function npcWashStrength(\n  vx: number, vy: number, ovx: Math.cos(-rotation), sin = Math.sin(-rotation);\n  const rx = (vx * cos - vy * sin) / h;\n  const ry = (vx * sin + vy * cos) / w;\n  let num = rx * rx + ry * ry;\n  num = num * 0.3 + 0.7 * num * (1024 / (h * w));\n  num = Math.min(num, 0.08);\n  num += Math.hypot(vx - ovx, vy - ovy) * 0.5;\n  if (!viscous && honeyOrLava) num *= 0.3;\n  return 0.5 * num;   // vColor.z = 0.5·num\n}\n\n/** 涟漪强度（NPC velLen : 0;\n  const vny = velLen > 0 ? e.vy / velLen : 0;\n  const isNpc = e.kind === 'npc';\n  const isPlayer = e.kind === 'player';\n  const viscous = gates.viscosity;\n\n  if (isNpc || (isPlayer vny * 10;\n    if (isPlayer) {\n      // :242-253 num3 = 0.05·√|vel|；quad (2w, 2h)\n      let num3 = 0.05 * Math.sqrt(velLen);\n      if (!viscous && (e.honeyWet || e.lavaWet)) num3 *= 0.3;\n      if wet? num3 : −num3, size)\n        const s3 = num3 * 3;\n        const px = e.x + e.w / 2 + vnx * (e.wet ? 5 : -20);\n        const py = e.y + e.h / 2 + vny * (e.wet ? 5 : -20);\n        const k = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, s3 * 10));\n        queueRippleSized(px, py, e.wet ? s3 : -s3, e.w, e.h * (e.wetCount / 9) * k, RippleShape.Circle, 0);\n      }\n    } else if (isNpc) {\n      // :200-217\n      let num = 0;\n      const key = e as unknown as object;\n      const ov = _oldVel.get(key);\n      const vz = npcWashStrength(e.vx, e.vy, ov ? ov[0] : e.vx, ov ? ov[1] : e.vy,\n        e.w, e.h, h·wetCount/9)·clamp(num·10)\n        const n2 = npcRippleStrength(velLen);\n        const px = e.x + e.w / 2 + vnx * (e.wet ? 5 : -20);\n        const py = e.y + e.h / 2 + vny * (e.wet ? 5 : -20);\n        const k = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, n2 * 10));\n        queueRippleSized(px, py, e.wet ? n2 : -n2, e.w, e.h * (e.wetCount / 9) * k, RippleShape.Circle, 0);\n      }\n    } else {\n      // 弹幕 :299-310：num6 = 2·√(0.05·|vel|)；quad (max(12, 0.75w), max(12, 0.75h))\n      let num6 = 2 * Math.sqrt(0.05 * velLen);\n      if (!viscous && (e.honeyWet || e.lavaWet)) num6 *= 0.3;\n      const qw = Math.max(12, e.w * 0.75), qh = Math.max(12, e.h * 0.75);\n      waterWaves.injectWash(cx, cy, qw, qh, vnx, vny, 0.5 * num6);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Gore 涟漪（Gore.cs:986：落水首帧 QueueRipple(pos+8,8) 默认强度 1）\n My call passed `e.w, e.h * (e.wetCount / 9) * k` — MISSING k on w! Fix: sx = e.w·k, sy = e.h·(wetCount/9)·k. Same for NPC: size = (nPC.width, nPC.height·(wetCount/9))·clamp → sx = w·k.

18. Projectile wet condition — my injectEntity gates on `e.projDistort` precomputed; the wet check: C# requires `flag3` (wet or ignoreWater) — my sample has `wet` — caller must set wet=true when in any liquid (or ignoreWater). I'll require e.wet in the condition: `(e.kind === 'projectile' && gates.projectile && e.projDistort && e.wet)`.

19. `injectEntity` — `const isNpc...` block structure: the projectile branch uses cx/cy which include −v̂·10 — fix per #15.

Let me rewrite the file fixing all these. Also remove tuple allocation in _step by inlining (keep pure exported fn for tests).

本帧真实流逝秒（progress/步进节奏；>1/60+容差 → 补 2 步） */\n  dt: number;\n}\n\n/** 调试/探针快照（F5 / _waveprobe） */\nexport interface WaterWaveDebug {\n  quality: number; gridW: number; gridH: number;\n  progress: number; intensity: number; stepsLastFrame: number;\n  rippleQueueUsed: number; activeTexels: number;\n}\n\nclass WaterWaveSystem {\n  quality = 3;\n  gates = waveSourceGates(3);\n  /** private _R2 = new Float32Array(0);\n  private _G2 = new Float32Array(0);\n  private _camX = 0; private _camY = 0; private _zoom = 1;\n  /** 相机滚动偏移（缓冲像素，floor；:151-152 _lastDistortionDrawOffset） */\n this._zoom = zoom;\n  }\n\n  /** 每渲染帧：Update(:112-129) + PreDraw(:336-360) + 涟漪队列注入。 */\n  frame(inp: WaveFrameInput): void {\n    this.setCamera(inp.camX, inp.camY, inp.zoom);\n    this.intensity = waterDistortionIntensity(\n      inp.windAbs, inp.rain, inp.tileX, inp.tiltYProxy ?? inp.tileY,\n      inp.maxTilesX, inp.maxTilesY, inp.worldSurface, inp.rockLayer);\n    this.progress = (this.progress + inp.dt * this.intensity * 0.75) % 86400;\n\n    const w = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(inp.viewW * 0.25));\n    const h = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(inp.viewH * 0.25));\n    this._ensure(w, h);\n\n    // 相机滚动（缓冲像素 floor；:148-152）——只在第一步施加（lastOffset 在\n    // StepLiquids 内更新，第二步增量 0）\n    const sx = Math.floor(inp.camX * inp.zoom * 0.25);\n    const sy = Math.floor(inp.camY * inp.zoom * 0.25);\n    const dx = sx - this._scrollX, dy = sy - this._scrollY;\n    this._scrollX = sx; this._scrollY = sy;\n\n    if (this.quality === 0) {\n      // 0 档：滤镜整体关闭（SceneState :129）——不注入不步进，缓冲回中性\n 步进（PreDraw :343-349 min(queuedSteps,2)；正常 1 步、掉帧补 2）\n    const steps = this.quality >= 2 ? (inp.dt > 1 / 60 + 1 / 240 ? 2 : 1) : 0;\n    this._stepsLast = steps;\n    if (steps > 0) {\n      this._step(dx, dy);\n      if (steps > 1) this._step(0, 0);\n    } else if (!this._isNeutral()) {\n      // q=1：无源不步进（PreDraw :350-358）——脏缓冲清回中性\n      this._clear();\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 位移采样（FilterWaterDistortion obj19 公式）。\n   *  worldX/worldY = 世界 px；out = [dxPx, dyPx]（canvas px，z=1 时=原版屏幕 px）。 */\n  sampleDisplace(worldX: number, worldY: number, out: [number, number]): void {\n    out[0] = 0; out[1] = 0;\n    if (this.quality === 0 || !_noiseData) return;\n    const px = (worldX - this._camX) * this._zoom;\n    const py = (worldY - this._camY) * this._zoom;\n    const u = px / _noiseW, v = py / _noiseH;   // uv = 屏幕px/noise 尺寸（c0）\n    const prog = this.progress;\n    const d1x = v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04, d1y = v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04;\n    const gx = noiseAt(0, u * 0.1, d1x) - noiseAt(0, u * 0.2, v * 0.2 - prog * 0.04);\n    const gy = noiseAt(1, u * 0.1, d1y) - noiseAt(1, u * 0.2, v * 0.2 - prog * 0.04);\n    // 涟漪高度项：h' = bufR − 0.5（(1−0.4·bufB)=1，1456 bufB=0），钳 ±0.3 取负入 DISPY\n    const h = this._readR(px, py);\n    const hClamp = Math.min(0.3, Math.max(-0.3, h - 0.5));\n    const I = this.intensity;\n    const dispx = gx * 0.1 * I;\n    const dispy = gy * 0.1 * I - hClamp;\n    out[0] = dispx * 0.1 * 32;   // c14.x=0.1 × c5=32/res × res\n    out[1] = dispy * 32;         // c14.y=1.0\n  }\n\n  /** 波光 tint 量（obj19 :106-111 近似：lum 取 0.75，见头部取舍）。>0 → 'lighter' 提亮 */\n  sampleGlint(worldX: number, worldY: number): number {\n    if (this.quality === 0 || !_noiseData) return 0;\n    const px = (worldX - this._camX) * this._zoom;\n    const py = (worldY - this._camY) * this._zoom;\n    const u = px / _noiseW, v = py / _noiseH;\n    const prog = this.progress;\n    const gx = noiseAt(0, u * 0.1, v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04) - noiseAt(0, u * 0.2, v * 0.2 - prog * 0.04);\n    const gy = noiseAt(1, u * 0.1, v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04) - noiseAt(1, u * 0.2, v * 0.2 - prog * 0.04);\n    const h = this._readR(px, py) - 0.5;\n    return Math.min(1, Math.abs(0.3 * h + Math.hypot(gx, gy)) * 0.75 * 0.3333);\n  }\n\n  /** 实体洗掠注入（WaterDistortionObject obj50 + DrawWaves 调用参数）。\n   *  center = 世界中心 px（NPC/玩家已含 −v̂·10 偏移；弹幕为裸 Center）；\n   *  quadW/H = 世界 px；flowX/Y = 已归一化流方向；strength = vColor.z。 */\n  injectWash(centerX: number, centerY: number, quadW: number, quadH: number,\n    flowX: number, flowY: number, strength: number): void {\n    if (this.quality === 0) return;\n    const z = this._zoom;\n    const cx = (centerX - this._camX) * z * 0.25;\n    const cy = (centerY - this._camY) * z * 0.25;\n    const qw = quadW * z * 0.25, qh = quadH * z * 0.25;\n    if (qw < 0.1 || qh < 0.1) return;\n    const hw = qw * 0.5, hh = qh * 0.5;\n    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cx - hw)), x1 = Math.min(this._gW - 1, Math.ceil(cx + hw));\n    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cy - hh)), y1 = Math.min(this._gH - 1, Math.ceil(cy + hh));\n    const R = this._R, G = this._G, w = this._gW;\n    const flowSignedX = flowX - 0.5, flowSignedY = flowY - 0.5;   // vColor.xy − 0.5\n    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {\n      for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {\n        // quad 局部 uv（MagicPixel 1×1 拉伸 → uv = (texel−center)/size + 0.5），再 −0.5\n        const uvcX = ((x + 0.5) - cx) / qw - 0.5;\n        const uvcY = ((y + 0.5) - cy) / qh - 0.5;\n        const s = washShapeValue(uvcX, uvcY, flowSignedX, flowSignedY, strength);\n        if (s <= 0) continue;\n        const i = y * w + x;\n        const inv = 1 - s;              // 预乘 AlphaBlend：out = src + dst·(1−a)，src=(s,0,0,s)\n        R[i] = s + R[i] * inv;\n        G[i] = G[i] * inv;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 调试快照（F5 / 探针；activeTexels 全格扫描，勿每帧调） */\n  debug(): WaterWaveDebug {\n    let active = 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < this._R.length; i++) if (this._R[i] !== 0.5) active++;\n    return {\n      quality: this.quality, gridW: this._gW, gridH: this._gH,\n      progress: this.progress, intensity: this.intensity,\n      stepsLastFrame: this._stepsLast, rippleQueueUsed: _rqCount, activeTexels: active,\n    };\n  }\n\n  private _ensure(w: number, h: number): void {\n    if (w === this._gW && h === this._gH) return;\n    const n = w * h;\n    this._R = new Float32Array(n).fill(0.5);\n    this._G = new Float32Array(n).fill(0.5);\n    this._R2 = new Float32Array(n).fill(0.5);\n    this._G2 = new Float32Array(n).fill(0.5);\n    this._gW = w; this._gH = h;\n  }\n\n  private _clear(): void {\n    this._R.fill(0.5); this._G.fill(0.5);\n  }\n\n  private _isNeutral(): boolean {\n    const R = this._R;\n    for (let i = 0; i < R.length; i++) if (R[i] !== 0.5) return false;\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** 读缓冲 R（屏幕 px → 缓冲 texel 双线性；非 pow2 缓冲 → clamp 采样语义） */\n  private _readR(px: number, py: number): number {\n    const bx = px * 0.25 - 0.5, by = py * 0.25 - 0.5;\n    const x0 = Math.floor(bx), y0 = Math.floor(by);\n    const fx = bx - x0, fy = by - y0;\n    const w = this._gW, h = this._gH;\n    const cl = (v: number, m: number) => Math.min(m - 1, Math.max(0, v));\n    const R = this._R;\n    const xa = cl(x0, w), xb = cl(x0 + 1, w), ya = cl(y0, h), yb = cl(y0 + 1, h);\n    return (R[ya * w + xa] * (1 - fx) + R[ya * w + xb] * fx) * (1 - fy)\n      + (R[yb * w + xa] * (1 - fx) + R[yb * w + xb] * fx) * fy;\n  }\n\n  /** 涟漪队列光栅化（DrawWaves :314-327；预乘 AlphaBlend，形状旋转于中心） */\n  private _drawRipples(): void {\n    if (!this.gates.ripple || !_rippleData) { _rqCount = 0; return; }\n    const z = this._zoom;\n    const R = this._R, G = this._G, w = this._gW, h = this._gH;\n    for (let q = 0; q < _rqCount; q++) {\n      const cx = (_rqX[q] - this._camX) * z * 0.25;\n      const cy = (_rqY[q] - this._camY) * z * 0.25;\n      const qw = _rqW[q] * z * 0.25, qh = _rqH[q] * z * 0.25;\n      if (qw < 0.1 || qh < 0.1) continue;\n      const hw = qw * 0.5, hh = qh * 0.5;\n      const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cx - hw)), x1 = Math.min(w - 1, Math.ceil(cx + hw));\n      const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cy - hh)), y1 = Math.min(h - 1, Math.ceil(cy + hh));\n      const cos = Math.cos(_rqRot[q]), sin = Math.sin(_rqRot[q]);\n      const num = _rqA[q], gCol = _rqColG[q], rCol = _rqColR[q];\n      const shape = _rqShape[q] as RippleShape;\n      for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {\n        for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {\n          const lx = ((x + 0.5) - cx) / hw, ly = ((y + 0.5) - cy) / hh;   // ∈ [−1,1]\n          const rx = lx * cos - ly * sin, ry = lx * sin + ly * cos;\n          if (rx < -1 || rx > 1 || ry < -1 || ry > 1) continue;\n          const shapeV = rippleShape(shape, (rx + 1) * 0.5, (ry + 1) * 0.5);\n          if (shapeV <= 0) continue;\n          const a = shapeV * num;\n          const inv = 1 - a;\n          const i = y * w + x;\n          R[i] = shapeV * rCol + R[i] * inv;\n          G[i] = shapeV * gCol + G[i] * inv;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    _rqCount = 0;\n  }\n\n  /** WaterProcessor 一步（滚动增量 dx/dy 施加在采样侧 = 原版把旧缓冲按位移增量\n   *  画进清空 swap 的语义；越界 clamp = RenderTarget Clamp 采样） */\n  private _step(dx: number, dy: number): void {\n    const w = this._gW, h = this._gH;\n    const R = this._R, G = this._G, R2 = this._R2, G2 = this._G2;\n    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {\n      const sy0 = y - dy;\n      const yU = Math.min(h - 1, Math.max(0, sy0 - 1));   // 上邻（uv y 向下）\n      const yM = Math.min(h - 1, Math.max(0, sy0));\n      const yD = Math.min(h - 1, Math.max(0, sy0 + 1));\n      const rowM = yM * w, rowU = yU * w, rowD = yD * w;\n      for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {\n        const sx0 = x - dx;\n        const xL = Math.min(w - 1, Math.max(0, sx0 - 1));\n        const xM = Math.min(w - 1, Math.max(0, sx0));\n        const xR = Math.min(w - 1, Math.max(0, sx0 + 1));\n        // 内联 waveProcessorStep（热循环零分配；公式见纯函数版）\n        const c = R[rowM + xM], cg = G[rowM + xM];\n        const signedC = 2 * c - 1;\n        const S = (2 * R[rowM + xL] - 1) + (2 * R[rowM + xR] - 1)\n          + (2 * R[rowU + xM] - 1) + (2 * R[rowD + xM] - 1);\n        const wv = (cg - 0.5) * (10 / 3);\n        const newH = (signedC + 2 * (S * 0.25 - signedC) + wv * Math.abs(wv)) * WATER_DAMP;\n        const i = y * w + x;\n        const q = Math.round(512 * (newH * 0.5 + 0.5));\n        R2[i] = q < 0 ? 0 : q > 512 ? 1 : q / 512;\n        const dv = newH - signedC;\n        const gEnc = 0.5 + 0.3 * Math.sign(dv) * Math.sqrt(Math.abs(dv));\n        G2[i] = gEnc < 0 ? 0 : gEnc > 1 ? 1 : gEnc;\n      }\n    }\n    this._R = R2; this._G = G2; this._R2 = R; this._G2 = G;\n  }\n\n  // 探针钩子：强度公式 tileY 覆盖位（正常恒 undefined → 用 inp.tileY）\n  private tiltYProxy: number | undefined;\n  /** 测试/探针用：直接读内部格（encoded R） */\n  readGridR(): Float32Array { return this._R; }\n  readGridG(): Float32Array { return this._G; }\n  gridW(): number { return this._gW; }\n  gridH(): number { return this._gH; }\n  /** 测试用：重置到干净态 */\n  resetForTest(): void {\n    this._gW = 0; this._gH = 0;\n    this.progress = 0; this._scrollX = 0; this._scrollY = 0;\n    _rqCount = 0;\n  }\n}\n\nexport const waterWaves = new WaterWaveSystem();\n\n// ---- 实体注入采集（Renderer 调；纯数据防实体类循环导入）----\nexport interface WaveEntitySample {\n  kind: 'npc' | 'player' | 'projectile' | 'gore';\n  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;     // 左上 + 尺寸（世界 px）\n  vx: number; vy: number;\n  wet: boolean; wetCount: number;\n  honeyWet: boolean; lavaWet: boolean;\n  /** NPC 视觉旋转（洗掠流方向 RotatedBy(−rotation) 用；默认 0） */\n  rotation?: number;\n  /** 弹幕侧预筛：CanDistortWater[type] && !NoLiquidDistortion[type] &&（wet ||\n   *  ignoreWater 现场复查）——调用方算好放 projDistort && wet */\n  projDistort?: boolean;\n}\n\n/** NPC 洗掠参数（DrawWaves :200-217 纯函数部分）。\n *  out[0] = vColor.z（=0.5·num），out[1]/out[2] = 流方向 xy（normalize(vel/(h,w))，\n *  再 +0.5 编码进色）。ovx/ovy = 上一 tick 速度（|vel−oldVel|·0.5 项）。 */\nconst _npcOut = [0, 0, 0];\nexport function npcWashParams(\n  vx: number, vy: number, ovx: number, ovy: number,\n  w: number, h: number, rotation: number, viscous: boolean, honeyOrLava: boolean,\n): [number, number, number] {\n  // vector5 = velocity.RotatedBy(−rotation) / (h, w)（:202——注意除的是 (height,width)）\n  const cos = Math.cos(-rotation), sin = Math.sin(-rotation);\n  const rx = (vx * cos - vy * sin) / h;\n  const ry = (vx * sin + vy * cos) / w;\n  let num = rx * rx + ry * ry;\n  num = num * 0.3 + 0.7 * num * (1024 / (h * w));\n  num = Math.min(num, 0.08);\n  num += Math.hypot(vx - ovx, vy - ovy) * 0.5;\n  if (!viscous && honeyOrLava) num *= 0.3;\n  const len = Math.hypot(rx, ry);\n  const fl = len > 0 ? 1 / len : 0;\n  _npcOut[0] = 0.5 * num;\n  _npcOut[1] = (len > 0 ? rx * fl : 0) * 0.5 + 0.5;   // vColor.x = vector5.X·0.5+0.5\n  _npcOut[2] = (len > 0 ? ry * fl : 0) * 0.5 + 0.5;   // vColor.y\n  return _npcOut as [number, number, number];\n}\n\n/** NPC 涟漪强度（:219-222）：num = 0.195·√|vel| */\nexport function npcRippleStrength(velLen: number): number {\n  return 0.195 * Math.sqrt(velLen);\n}\n\n/** 上一 tick 速度缓存（|vel−oldVel| 项；WeakMap 每实体一槽） */\nconst _oldVel = new WeakMap<object, [number, number]>();\n\n/** 采集一个实体并注入（Renderer 逐实体调用；档位门在此把守） */\nexport function injectEntity(e: WaveEntitySample, camX: number, camY: number,\n  viewW: number, viewH: number, zoom: number): void {\n  const gates = waterWaves.gates;\n  if (waterWaves.quality === 0) return;\n  // 屏幕盒剔除（:196 Collision.CheckAABBvAABBCollision：屏矩形 vs 实体盒 ±16px）\n  const sx = (e.x - camX) * zoom, sy = (e.y - camY) * zoom;\n  const sw = e.w * zoom, sh = e.h * zoom;\n  if (sx + sw < -16 || sy + sh < -16 || sx > viewW + 16 || sy > viewH + 16) return;\n\n  const velLen = Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy);\n  const vnx = velLen > 0 ? e.vx / velLen : 0;\n  const vny = velLen > 0 ? e.vy / velLen : 0;\n  const viscous = gates.viscosity;\n  const cx0 = e.x + e.w / 2, cy0 = e.y + e.h / 2;\n\n  if (e.kind === 'npc' || e.kind === 'townnpc') {\n    // NPC（恒开，:192-230）\n    const key = e as unknown as object;\n    const ov = _oldVel.get(key);\n    const [b, fx, fy] = npcWashParams(e.vx, e.vy, ov ? ov[0] : e.vx, ov ? ov[1] : e.vy,\n      e.w, e.h, e.rotation ?? 0, viscous, e.honeyWet || e.lavaWet);\n    if (ov) { ov[0] = e.vx; ov[1] = e.vy; } else _oldVel.set(key, [e.vx, e.vy]);\n    if (e.wet) {\n      // quad @ Center − v̂·10（:201/:210），流方向 = vColor.xy（含 +0.5 编码）\n      waterWaves.injectWash(cx0 - vnx * 10, cy0 - vny * 10, e.w * 2, e.h * 2, fx, fy, b);\n    }\n    if (e.wetCount !== 0) {\n      // raw Color 重载（:228）：Color(0.5, (wet?num:−num)·0.5+0.5, 0,1)×0.5\n      const n2 = npcRippleStrength(velLen);\n      const g = ((e.wet ? n2 : -n2) * 0.5 + 0.5) * 0.5;\n      const k = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, n2 * 10));\n      queueRippleRaw(cx0 + vnx * (e.wet ? 5 : -20), cy0 + vny * (e.wet ? 5 : -20),\n        e.w * k, e.h * (e.wetCount / 9) * k, 0.25, g, 0.5, RippleShape.Circle, 0);\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (e.kind === 'player') {\n    if (!gates.player) return;\n    // :242-263：num3 = 0.05·√|vel|；quad @ Center − v̂·10；流方向 = v̂（+0.5 编码）\n    let num3 = 0.05 * Math.sqrt(velLen);\n    if (!viscous && (e.honeyWet || e.lavaWet)) num3 *= 0.3;\n    if (e.wet) {\n      waterWaves.injectWash(cx0 - vnx * 10, cy0 - vny * 10, e.w * 2, e.h * 2,\n        vnx * 0.5 + 0.5, vny * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.5 * num3);\n    }\n    if (e.wetCount !== 0) {\n      // (strength,size) 重载（:263）：num3 ×= 3 → s3；尺寸 (w, h·wetCount/9)·clamp(s3·10)\n      const s3 = num3 * 3;\n      const k = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, s3 * 10));\n      queueRippleSized(cx0 + vnx * (e.wet ? 5 : -20), cy0 + vny * (e.wet ? 5 : -20),\n        e.wet ? s3 : -s3, e.w * k, e.h * (e.wetCount / 9) * k, RippleShape.Circle, 0);\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (e.kind === 'projectile') {\n    if (!gates.projectile || !e.projDistort || !e.wet) return;\n    // :299-310：quad @ Center（无 −v̂·10）；num6 = 2·√(0.05·|vel|)\n    let num6 = 2 * Math.sqrt(0.05 * velLen);\n    if (!viscous && (e.honeyWet || e.lavaWet)) num6 *= 0.3;\n    waterWaves.injectWash(cx0, cy0, Math.max(12, e.w * 0.75), Math.max(12, e.h * 0.75),\n      vnx * 0.5 + 0.5, vny * 0.5 + 0.5, num6 * 0.5);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (e.kind === 'gore' && gates.ripple) {\n    // Gore.cs:986：落水帧 QueueRipple(pos+8, 8)（默认强度 1；落水沿由调用方把守）\n    queueRipple(e.x + 8, e.y + 8, 1, RippleShape.Square, 0);\n  }\n}\n"
}

1. `frame()` references `inp.tiltYProxy` — typo leftover, `tiltYProxy` doesn't exist on WaveFrameInput. Fix to just `inp.tileY`.

2. In `sampleDisplace` I have a silly `const d1x = v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04, d1y = v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04;` — d1x should be `v * 0.1 + prog * 0.04` for X sample too? The X coordinate for n1 is u·0.1 (no drift), Y is v·0.1 + prog·0.04. So d1x is wrong-named but the value used for noiseAt(0, u*0.1, d1x) — the v-coordinate of sample 1 = v·0.1 + prog·0.04 ✓ both same expression. It's fine but confusing naming; fix naming: d1v.

3. injectEntity: `e.kind === 'npc' || e.kind === 'townnpc'` — WaveEntitySample.kind type doesn't include 'townnpc'. Add it to the union.

4. The gore path: fires every frame while gore on screen — vanilla queues ONCE on water-entry frame. Caller must gate. Note in comment ✓ (says 落水沿由调用方把守).

Fix these.
```

</details>
