 "new_string": "// ---- 纹理数据（noise 256×256 RGB 彩噪 / Ripples 64×128 灰度形状，提取一次）----\nlet _noiseData: Uint8ClampedArray | null = null;\nlet _noiseW = 256, _noiseH = 256;\nlet _rippleData: Uint8ClampedArray | null = null;\nlet _texReady = false;\n\n/** 游戏侧由 atlas 提取像素后注入（探针/测试也可直接喂数组） */\nexport function setWaveTextures(\n  noise: { w: number; h: number; data: Uint8ClampedArray } | null,\n  ripple: { data: Uint8ClampedArray } | null,\n): void {\n  if (noise) { _noiseData = noise.data; _noiseW = noise.w; _noiseH = noise.h; }\n  if (ripple) _rippleData = ripple.data;\n  _texReady = !!(noise && ripple);\n}\n\n/** 从 atlas 提取两张原版贴图像素（Images/Misc/noise 256×256、Images/Misc/Ripples\n *  64×128；各一次，离屏 canvas getImageData）。Renderer 每帧幂等调用。 */\nexport function ensureWaveTextures(\n  atlas: { ensureVImage(f: string): ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null } | null,\n): void {\n  if (_texReady || !atlas || typeof document === 'undefined') return;\n  const noise = atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/Misc_noise.png');\n  const ripple = atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/Misc_Ripples.png');\n  if (!noise || !ripple) return;   // 未就绪下帧再试\n  const grab = (img: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => {\n    const c = document.createElement('canvas');\n    c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;\n    const cc = c.getContext('2d');\n    if (!cc) return null;\n    cc.drawImage(img, 0, 0);\n    return cc.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data;\n  };\n  const nd = grab(noise), rd = grab(ripple);\n  if (!nd || !rd) return;\n  setWaveTextures({ w: noise.width, h: noise.height, data: nd }, { data: rd });\n}"
}

  //   仅上方有水 → 格顶 16×4 横条(:4081-4087)
  //   仅下方有水 → 格底 16×4 横条(:4089-4093)
  //   仅左侧有水 → 格左 4px 竖条(:4113-4116);仅右侧 → 格右 4px(:4118-4121)
  //   左右都有   → 整格 16 宽(:4108-4111);深度按液体量 num6=(256-max)/32*2 从底收
  if (isBackground) drawLiquidBehindTilesOnly(ctx, atlas, st, groundLevel, tx0, ty0, tx1, ty1, waterStyle);

  // ---- 调试快照落盘（DebugReport render.subsystems.liquids）----

```

{
 "command": "cd ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game && node --input-type=module - <<'EOF'\n// 与 WaterWaves.ts 相同公式,生成测试金标\nconst DAMP = Math.pow(2, -0.0551416);\nconst step = (L, R, U, D, C, Cg) => {\n  const signedC = 2 * C - 1;\n  const S = (2*L-1)+(2*R-1)+(2*U-1)+(2*D-1);\n  const T = S * 0.25 - signedC;\n  const w = (Cg - 0.5) * (10/3);\n  const H = signedC + 2*T + w*Math.abs(w);\n  const newH = H * DAMP;\n  const outR = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, Math.round(512*(newH*0.5+0.5))/512));\n  const dv = newH - signedC;\n  const outG = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 0.5 + 0.3*Math.sign(dv)*Math.sqrt(Math.abs(dv))));\n  return [outR, outG];\n};\nconsole.log('neutral', step(0.5,0.5,0.5,0.5,0.5,0.5));\nconsole.log('bump  ', step(0.5,0.5,0.5,0.5,0.50390625,0.5));\nconsole.log('spread', step(0.50390625,0.5,0.5,0.5,0.5,0.5));\nconsole.log('allhigh', step(1,1,1,1,1,1));\nconsole.log('alllow ', step(0,0,0,0,0,0));\nconsole.log('vel    ', step(0.5,0.5,0.5,0.5,0.5,0.75));\nconsole.log('damp const', DAMP);\nconst clamp01 = v => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v));\nconst inten = (windAbs, rain, tileX, tileY, mx, my, ws, rl) => {\n  const num = mx*0.5 - Math.abs(tileX - mx*0.5);\n  let i = 1 + windAbs*1.25 + clamp01(rain)*1.25;\n  i += -(clamp01((num-380)/100)*0.5 - 0.25);\n  const num6 = 1 - clamp01(3*(tileY-ws)/(rl-ws));\n  i *= num6;\n  i += 0.9 - clamp01((my-tileY-200)/300)*0.9;\n  i += (1-num6)*0.75;\n  return Math.min(2.5, Math.max(0, i));\n};\nconsole.log('I surface calm', inten(0,0,1050,100,2100,700,110,190));\nconsole.log('I surface wind ', inten(0.8,1,1050,100,2100,700,110,190));\nconsole.log('I underworld   ', inten(0,0,1050,650,2100,700,110,190));\nconsole.log('I underground  ', inten(0,0,1050,300,2100,700,110,190));\nconsole.log('I edge         ', inten(0,0,30,100,2100,700,110,190));\nEOF",
 "description": "Compute golden values for tests"
}

waveProcessorStep(0.50390625, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5);\n    expect(r[0]).toBeCloseTo(0.501953125, 10);\n    expect(r[1]).toBeCloseTo(0.5183950780239174, 9);\n  });\n  it('unorm 钳制：满场 1 → outR 钳 1（打满轨道是动力学一部分）', () => {\n    const r = waveProcessorStep(1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1);\n    expect(r[0]).toBe(1);\n    expect(r[1]).toBeCloseTo(0.9870831444521206, 9);\n  });\n  it('速度 √ 编码与 10/3 解码互逆（w·|w| ≡ dv）', () => {\n    // 速度 G 编码后一步解码应回到同量级：直接验证 (G−0.5)/0.3 与 0.3·√|dv| 互逆\n    const g0 = 0.75;                            // 编码速度\n    const dv = (g0 - 0.5) / 0.3;                // w\n    const reEnc = 0.5 + 0.3 * Math.sign(dv) * Math.sqrt(Math.abs(dv));\n    // w·|w| = dv；再编码 = 0.5+0.3·√|dv| —— 不是恒等（√ 压缩），验证双向映射单调\n    expect(dv).toBeCloseTo(0.8333333333333334, 10);\n    expect(reEnc).toBeCloseTo(0.7742677069855648, 9);\n  });\n  it('damp 常量 = exp2(−0.0551416)', () => {\n    expect(WATER_DAMP).toBeCloseTo(0.9624999694393103, 12);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('waterDistortionIntensity（SceneState.cs:159-173）', () => {\n  const base = (wind: number, rain: number, tx: number, ty: number) =>\n    waterDistortionIntensity(wind, rain, tx, ty, 2100, 700, 110, 190);\n  it('地表中心无风雨：num6=1 → 1 − 0.25 + 0 + 0.9 = 1.65？——世界边项取 −0.25', () => {\n    // num=1050-|1050-1050|=1050 → clamp((1050-380)/100)=1 → −(0.5−0.25)=−0.25\n    // 地表 num6=1 → ·1；+0.9（地狱项 clamp((700-100-200)/300)=1 → 0）；+(1−1)·0.75=0\n    expect(base(0, 0, 1050, 100)).toBeCloseTo(1.65, 10);\n  });\n  it('狂风暴雨钳 2.5', () => {\n    expect(base(0.8, 1, 1050, 100)).toBeCloseTo(2.5, 10);\n  });\n  it('地下（worldSurface 与 rockLayer 之间）', () => {\n    // tileY=300: num6 = 1−clamp(3·190/80)=0 → I·0 + 0 + 0.75 → 0.75? no:\n    // I(=1.65)·0 = 0 → +0.9−clamp((700−300−200)/300)·0.9 = 0.9−0.667·0.9 = 0.3 → +0.75 → 1.05\n    expect(base(0, 0, 1050, 300)).toBeCloseTo(1.05, 10);\n  });\n  it('地狱层（近底）', () => {\n    // tileY=650: num6=0 → 0 + 0.9−0·0.9 + 0.75 = 1.65? 计算:clamp((700-650-200)/300)=0 → 0.9 → 1.65\n    expect(base(0, 0, 1050, 650)).toBeCloseTo(1.65, 10);\n  });\n  it('世界边缘（num<380 → 边缘项 +0.25）', () => {\n    // num=30: clamp((30-380)/100)=0 → −(0−0.25)=+0.25 → 1+0.25+0.9 = 2.15? 金标 1.25?\n    expect(base(0, 0, 30, 100)).toBeCloseTo(1.25, 10);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('washShapeValue（WaterDistortionObject obj50 复原）', () => {\n  it('quad 中心 rlen=0 → 0', () => {\n    expect(washShapeValue(0, 0, 0.4, 0, 0.5)).toBe(0);\n  });\n  it('flow 零向量 → 0', () => {\n    expect(washShapeValue(0.2, 0, 0, 0, 0.5)).toBe(0);\n  });\n  it('顺流方向且 sinφ<0 的点产生正强度', () => {\n    // radial=(1,0) 方向、flow=(−1,0)+0.5 → flowSigned=(−0.5,0) → dot(−f,rad)=1 → 1−0.2=0.8\n    // r=0.2·25.12=5.024 < 12.56 → φ=frac(5.024/2π+0.5)·2π−π\n    const v = washShapeValue(0.2, 0, -0.5, 0, 1);\n    expect(v).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    const frac = (5.024 * 0.159155 + 0.5) % 1;\n    const phi = frac * 6.28319 - 3.14159;\n    expect(v).toBeCloseTo(Math.max(-Math.sin(phi), 0) * 0.8, 9);\n  });\n  it('远处（r≥4π）恒小负角 → 微弱尾迹', () => {\n    // uv 对角 (0.5·√2? r=|uvc|·25.12 ≥ 12.56 需 |uvc| ≥ 0.5 → (0.6,0)\n    const v = washShapeValue(0.6, 0, -0.5, 0, 1);\n    const frac = ((0.6 * 25.12 * 0.159155 + 0.5) % 1);\n    expect(v).toBeCloseTo(0.0063711 * 0.8, 3);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('涟漪队列（WaterShaderData.cs:446-470）', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    waterWaves.resetForTest();\n    waterWaves.setQuality(2);\n  });\n  it('queueRipple 默认重载：尺寸 4·max(|s|,1)、色 (0.5,g,0)·num', () => {\n    queueRipple(100, 100, 1, RippleShape.Square, 0);\n    const d = waterWaves.debug();\n    expect(d.rippleQueueUsed).toBe(1);\n  });\n  it('q<2 时入队 = 清空全队列（:462-465）', () => {\n    queueRipple(100, 100);          // 先入一个（q=2 有效）\n    expect(waterWaves.debug().rippleQueueUsed).toBe(1);\n    waterWaves.setQuality(1);\n    queueRipple(200, 200);          // 触发清空\n    expect(waterWaves.debug().rippleQueueUsed).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('波场系统 frame（WaterShaderData StepLiquids/PreDraw）', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    waterWaves.resetForTest();\n    waterWaves.setQuality(3);\n    // 喂假纹理：noise 全 0.5 灰（环境波为零 → 只测涟漪链路）、Ripples 圆形=白\n    const noise = new Uint8ClampedArray(256 * 256 * 4).fill(128);\n    const ripple = new Uint8ClampedArray(64 * 128 * 4).fill(255);\n    setWaveTextures({ w: 256, h: 256, data: noise }, { data: ripple });\n  });\n  const frameInp = {\n    camX: 0, camY: 0, zoom: 1, viewW: 400, viewH: 400,\n    tileX: 1050, tileY: 100, maxTilesX: 2100, maxTilesY: 700,\n    worldSurface: 110, rockLayer: 190, windAbs: 0, rain: 0, dt: 1 / 60,\n  };\n  it('涟漪注入 → 传播 → activeTexels > 0 且扩散', () => {\n    queueRippleRaw(200, 200, 64, 64, 0.5, 1, 1, RippleShape.Square, 0);   // 满强度\n    waterWaves.frame(frameInp);\n    const d1 = waterWaves.debug();\n    expect(d1.stepsLastFrame).toBe(1);\n    expect(d1.activeTexels).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    const n1 = d1.activeTexels;\n    // 无人搅动再跑 30 帧：能量衰减（波纹量化后死亡 → activeTexels 不增）\n    for (let f = 0; f < 30; f++) waterWaves.frame(frameInp);\n    const d2 = waterWaves.debug();\n    expect(d2.activeTexels).toBeLessThanOrEqual(n1 * 3);   // 扩散有界\n  });\n  it('q=0：不步进、缓冲中性', () => {\n    waterWaves.setQuality(0);\n    queueRippleRaw(200, 200, 64, 64, 0.5, 1, 1, RippleShape.Square, 0);\n    waterWaves.frame(frameInp);\n    const d = waterWaves.debug();\n    expect(d.stepsLastFrame).toBe(0);\n    expect(d.activeTexels).toBe(0);\n  });\n  it('q=1：涟漪入队即清 → 缓冲中性（只有噪声环境波，无涟漪源）', () => {\n    waterWaves.setQuality(1);\n    queueRippleRaw(200, 200, 64, 64, 0.5, 1, 1, RippleShape.Square, 0);\n    waterWaves.frame(frameInp);\n    expect(waterWaves.debug().activeTexels).toBe(0);\n  });\n  it('progress 按 dt·intensity·0.75 累加（:121）', () => {\n    waterWaves.frame(frameInp);\n    const d = waterWaves.debug();\n    expect(d.progress).toBeCloseTo((1 / 60) * d.intensity * 0.75, 10);\n  });\n  it('掉帧（dt 大）→ 补 2 步（PreDraw min(queuedSteps,2)）', () => {\n    queueRippleRaw(200, 200, 64, 64, 0.5, 1, 1, RippleShape.Square, 0);\n    waterWaves.frame({ ...frameInp, dt: 1 / 20 });\n    expect(waterWaves.debug().stepsLastFrame).toBe(2);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('injectEntity 档位门', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    waterWaves.resetForTest();\n    setWaveTextures(null, null);\n  });\n + wetCount 涟漪入队', () => {\n    waterWaves.setQuality(3);\n    injectEntity({ kind: 'player', x: 100, y: 100, w: 20, h: 42, vx: 3, vy: 0, wet: true, wetCount: 10, honeyWet: false, lavaWet: false }, cam.camX, cam.camY, cam.viewW, cam.viewH, cam.zoom);\n    expect(waterWaves.debug().rippleQueueUsed).toBe(1);\n  });\n  it('q=2：弹幕不注入（>=3 门）', () => {\n    waterWaves.setQuality(2);\n    injectEntity({ kind: 'projectile', x: 300, y: 300, w: 10, h: 10, vx: 8, vy: 0, wet: true, wetCount: 0, honeyWet: false, lavaWet: false, projDistort: true }, cam.camX, cam.camY, cam.viewW, cam.viewH, cam.zoom);\n    expect(waterWaves.debug().rippleQueueUsed).toBe(0);\n  });\n  it('屏幕外实体剔除（:196 ±16px 盒）', () => {\n    waterWaves.setQuality(3);\n    injectEntity({ kind: 'player', x: 5000, y: 100, w: 20, h: 42, vx: 3, vy: 0, wet: true, wetCount: 10, honeyWet: false, lavaWet: false }, cam.camX, cam.camY, cam.viewW, cam.viewH, cam.zoom);\n    expect(waterWaves.debug().rippleQueueUsed).toBe(0);\n  "old_string": "/** WaterProcessor 传播内核（纯函数，供单测金标；encoded R/G ∈ [0,1]）。\n *  L/R/U/D = 四邻 encoded 高度；C/Cg = 本格 encoded 高度/速度。返回 [outR, outG]。 */\nexport function waveProcessorStep(\n  L: number, R: number, U: number, D: number, C: number, Cg: number,\n): [number, number] {\n  const signedC = 2 * C - 1;\n  const S = (2 * L - 1) + (2 * R - 1) + (2 * U - 1) + (2 * D - 1);\n  const T = S * 0.25 - signedC;                        // (1−B)=1（1456）\n  const w = (Cg - 0.5) * (10 / 3);\n  const H = signedC + 2 * T + w * Math.abs(w);\n  const newH = H * WATER_DAMP;\n  // unorm + 1/512 量化：floor(512·enc + 0.5)/512（round-half-up），钳 [0,1]\n  const outR = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, Math.round(512 * (newH * 0.5 + 0.5)) / 512));\n  const dv = newH - signedC;                           // (1−0.05B)=1\n  const outG = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 0.5 + 0.3 * Math.sign(dv) * Math.sqrt(Math.abs(dv))));\n  return [outR, outG];\n}",
 "new_string": "/** unorm8 通道量化（渲染目标 R8G8B8A8 存储 = 1/256 步；★动力学一部分——\n *  小速度被 8bit 量化截断是涟漪能量汇，浮点缓冲会出\"永生驻波\"） */\nconst q256 = (v: number): number => {\n  const q = Math.round(v * 256);\n  return q < 0 ? 0 : q > 256 ? 1 : q / 256;\n};\n\n/** WaterProcessor 传播内核（纯函数，供单测金标；encoded R/G ∈ [0,1]）。\n *  L/R/U/D = 四邻 encoded 高度；C/Cg = 本格 encoded 高度/速度。返回 [outR, outG]。 */\nexport function waveProcessorStep(\n  L: number, R: number, U: number, D: number, C: number, Cg: number,\n): [number, number] {\n  const signedC = 2 * C - 1;\n  const S = (2 * L - 1) + (2 * R - 1) + (2 * U - 1) + (2 * D - 1);\n  const T = S * 0.25 - signedC;                        // (1−B)=1（1456）\n  const w = (Cg - 0.5) * (10 / 3);\n  const H = signedC + 2 * T + w * Math.abs(w);\n  const newH = H * WATER_DAMP;\n  // 着色器内 1/512 量化（floor(512·enc+0.5)/512）→ 写入 unorm8 目标再 1/256 量化\n  const q512 = Math.round(512 * (newH * 0.5 + 0.5));\n  const outR = q256(q512 < 0 ? 0 : q512 > 512 ? 1 : q512 / 512);\n  const dv = newH - signedC;                           // (1−0.05B)=1\n  const outG = q256(0.5 + 0.3 * Math.sign(dv) * Math.sqrt(Math.abs(dv)));\n  return [outR, outG];\n}"

scripts/_glpar-probe.mjs
// _glpar-probe:WebGL2 迁移(#1 背景/#2 全屏地图)像素对拍
// 同一大世界存档,GL(?bggl 默认开) vs 2D(?bggl=0&mapgl=0) 各跑一遍:
// 钉死时钟(黄昏=满 tint 压力)+玩家落点 → 网格采样主画布 → 节点侧逐点比对。
// 采样避开 HUD/实体动画噪声:地表取上半屏(天空+背景层),地图取全屏。
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
const ORIGIN = process.env.SW_ORIGIN || 'http://localhost:5199';
const b = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: 'new', defaultViewport: { width: 1280, height: 800 } });

async function runSession(query) {
  const p = await b.newPage();
  p.on('pageerror', e => console.log(`[pageerr ${query}]`, String(e).slice(0, 140)));
  p.on('console', m => { const t = m.text(); if (t.includes('GLSpriteLayer') || t.includes('[bitmap失败')) console.log(`[${query}]`, t.slice(0, 120)); });
  await p.goto(`${ORIGIN}/${query}`, { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 90000 });
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 5000));
  const ok = await p.evaluate(async () => {
    const r = await fetch('/tmp-glpar-world.json');
    if (!r.ok) return `fetch ${r.status}`;
    await window.__swFlow.loadJson(await r.text());
    return 'ok';
  }).catch(e => String(e));
  if (ok !== 'ok') { console.log(`[${query}] loadJson 失败:`, ok); await p.close(); return null; }
  for (let i = 0; i < 150; i++) {
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000));
    if (await p.evaluate(() => !!window.__swGame).catch(() => false)) break;
  }
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 6000));
  // 钉死状态:时钟=黄昏 0.75(tint 满压)+玩家=出生点(地表森林)
  await p.evaluate(() => {
    const g = window.__swGame;
    g.world.clock.timeOfDay = 0.75;
    const p2 = g.player;
    p2.x = (g.world.spawnX - 2) * 16; p2.y = (g.world.spawnY - 3) * 16;
    p2.vx = 0; p2.vy = 0;
  });
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2500));
  const sample = await p.evaluate((region) => {
    const c = window.__swGame.renderer.canvas;
    const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
    const GX = 96, GY = region === 'surface' ? 30 : 54;   // 地表只取上半屏(避开实体/尘)
    const y0 = 0, y1 = region === 'surface' ? Math.floor(c.height * 0.55) : c.height;
    const out = [];
    for (let j = 0; j < GY; j++) {
      for (let i = 0; i < GX; i++) {
        const x = Math.floor((i + 0.5) * c.width / GX);
        const y = Math.floor(y0 + (j + 0.5) * (y1 - y0) / GY);
        const d = ctx.getImageData(x, y, 1, 1).data;
        out.push(d[0], d[1], d[2], d[3]);
      }
    }
    return { w: c.width, h: c.height, data: out };
  }, 'surface');
  // 开全屏地图(直接置态,跳过缩放缓动)
  await p.evaluate(() => {
    const g = window.__swGame;
    const fm = g.renderer.fullMap;
    fm.open = true; fm.zoom = 1.25; fm.zoomTarget = 1.25;
    fm.panX = g.world.w * 1.25 / 2 - (g.player.cx / 16) * 1.25 - g.renderer.canvas.width / 2 + g.world.w * 1.25 / 2;
    fm.panX = 0; fm.panY = 0;
  });
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1200));
  const mapSample = await p.evaluate(() => {
    const c = window.__swGame.renderer.canvas;
    const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
    const GX = 96, GY = 54;
    const out = [];
    for (let j = 0; j < GY; j++) for (let i = 0; i < GX; i++) {
      const x = Math.floor((i + 0.5) * c.width / GX);
      const y = Math.floor((j + 0.5) * c.height / GY);
      const d = ctx.getImageData(x, y, 1, 1).data;
      out.push(d[0], d[1], d[2], d[3]);
    }
    return { w: c.width, h: c.height, data: out };
  });
  await p.close();
  return { sample, mapSample };
}

function compare(name, A, B) {
  if (!A || !B) { console.log(`${name}: 采样缺失`); return; }
  const n = Math.min(A.data.length, B.data.length);

 "content": "// _waveprobe:水面波动系统(Water Waves)+ 浪质量四档 浏览器探针。\n// 流程:?play=small 生成世界 → 找地表水潭 → 钉时钟/清风 → 逐档位(q0-3)验证:\n//   B/C/D) waterWaves 模块态:q0 步进 0+中性、q≥2 步进 1、玩家入水涟漪入队+传播\n//   E) 视觉:风=0 时水贴图动画帧率恒 6fps → 等待同 animFrame 抓两帧,水线带像素差\n//      q3 > 0(波动在动)、q0 == 0(同帧唯一差异源只剩波动) —— 波动存在的铁证\n// 页面零 pageerror 为硬门。\nimport puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';\nconst ORIGIN = process.env.SW_ORIGIN || 'http://localhost:5199';\nconst errors = [];\nconst b = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: 'new', defaultViewport: { width: 1024, height: 640 } });\nconst p = await b.newPage();\np.on('pageerror', e => errors.push(String(e).slice(0, 200)));\nawait p.goto(`${ORIGIN}/?play=small`, { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 90000 });\nawait new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 4000));\n// 生成小世界(seed 固定)\nconst gen = await p.evaluate(async () => {\n  await window.__swGame.newWorld('12345', 4200, 1200, () => {});\n  return 'ok';\n}).catch(e => String(e));\nconsole.log('worldgen:', gen);\nfor (let i = 0; i < 240; i++) {\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1000));\n  if (await p.evaluate(() => window.__swGame?.world?.store?.liquid?.length > 0).catch(() => false)) break;\n}\nawait new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 3000));\n\n// 找水潭 + 钉态(时钟正午/风 0/雨 0) + 玩家压入水中\nconst pool = await p.evaluate(() => {\n  const g = window.__swGame, w = g.world, st = w.store;\n  g.world.clock.timeOfDay = 0.5;\n  if (w.weather) { w.weather.windSpeedCurrent = 0; w.weather.windSpeedTarget = 0; w.weather.maxRaining = 0; }\n  const sx = w.spawnX, sy = w.spawnY;\n  for (let dx = -220; dx <= 220; dx++) {\n    const x = sx + dx;\n    if (x < 20 || x >= st.w - 20) continue;\n    for (let y = Math.max(10, sy - 60); y < Math.min(st.h - 40, sy + 120); y++) {\n      const i = st.idx(x, y);\n      // 顶格空气 + 3 格连水(≥160 液位) = 水潭表面\n      if (st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - 1)] === 0 && st.liquid[i] > 200 && !st.isSolid(x, y)\n        && st.liquid[st.idx(x + 1, y)] > 200 && st.liquid[st.idx(x - 1, y)] > 200\n        && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y + 1)] > 200 && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y + 2)] > 200) {\n        const pl = g.player;\n        pl.x = x * 16 + 2; pl.y = (y + 1) * 16 - 6; pl.vx = 0; pl.vy = 0;\n        return { x, y, w: st.w, h: st.h, ground: w.groundLevel, rock: w.rockLevel };\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n});\nconsole.log('pool:', JSON.stringify(pool));\nif (!pool) { console.log('FAIL: 未找到水潭'); await b.close(); process.exit(1); }\nawait new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2500));\n\n// 玩家持续搅水(踢 vy → wet 洗掠+涟漪)\nawait p.evaluate(() => {\n  window.__waveKick = setInterval(() => {\n    const pl = window.__swGame.player;\n    pl.vy = -2.5; pl.vx = 1.2;\n  }, 250);\n});\n\nconst mod = await p.evaluate(async () => {\n  const m = await import('/src/render/WaterWaves.ts');\n  const liq = await import('/src/render/VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts');\n  window.__ww = m; window.__liq = liq;\n  return 'ok';\n});\nconsole.log('modules:', mod);\n\nasync function setQ(q) {\n  await p.evaluate(async (qq) => {\n    const o = await import('/src/core/Options.ts');\n    o.options.data.waveQuality = qq;   // Renderer 每帧同步(不走持久化,探针专用)\n    return window.__ww.waterWaves.quality;\n  }, q);\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1200));\n}\n\n// 水线带采样:pool.y 格 ±8px 横带(世界→画布由相机换算,取画布中央 512px 宽)\nasync function grabStrip() {\n  return p.evaluate(() => {\n    const g = window.__swGame, c = g.renderer.canvas;\n    const ctx = c.getContext('2d');\n    // 相机中心=玩家 → 水线世界 y = (poolY+1)*16 附近;直接取玩家附近横带\n    const py = (g.player.y + g.player.h / 2);\n    const cy = Math.round((py - (g.renderer.ctx.getTransform ? 0 : 0)) * 1);  // 世界系\n    // 画布中心对应玩家 → 水线画布 y ≈ canvas.height/2 ± (水线−玩家中心)·zoom\n    const cam = g.camera;\n    const zoom = cam.zoom;\n    const screenY = (py - cam.y) * zoom + c.height / 2;\n    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.round(screenY) - 20);\n    const y1 = Math.min(c.height, Math.round(screenY) + 20);\n    const out = [];\n    for (let y = y0; y < y1; y += 2) {\n      for (let x = 0; x < c.width; x += 4) {\n        const d = ctx.getImageData(x, y, 1, 1).data;\n        out.push(d[0], d[1], d[2]);\n      }\n    }\n    return out;\n  });\n}\n// 等待与上次相同的动画帧(wind=0 → 6fps,周期 ~2.67s)\nasync function grabAtSameFrame() {\n  const f0 = await p.evaluate(() => window.__liq.liquidDebugState().animFrame);\n  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {\n    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 120));\n    const f1 = await p.evaluate(() => window.__liq.liquidDebugState().animFrame);\n    if (f1 === f0) {\n      return await grabStrip();\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nconst results = {};\n// ---- B) q0:不步进、中性、位移 0 ----\nawait setQ(0);\nresults.q0 = await p.evaluate(() => {\n  const ww = window.__ww.waterWaves;\n  const d = ww.debug();\n  const out = [0, 0];\n  ww.sampleDisplace(window.__swGame.player.cx, window.__swGame.player.cy, out);\n  return { steps: d.stepsLastFrame, active: d.activeTexels, disp: out[1] !== 0 };\n});\n// ---- C) q1:无源步进 0 但噪声环境波位移非 0 ----\nawait setQ(1);\nresults.q1 = await p.evaluate(() => {\n  const ww = window.__ww.waterWaves;\n  const d = ww.debug();\n  const out = [0, 0];\n  ww.sampleDisplace(window.__swGame.player.cx, window.__swGame.player.cy, out);\n  return { steps: d.stepsLastFrame, active: d.activeTexels, disp: Math.abs(out[1]) };\n});\n// ---- D) q3:玩家湿身涟漪入队 → 传播 activeTexels>0 ----\nawait setQ(3);\nresults.q3 = await p.evaluate(() => {\n  const ww = window.__ww.waterWaves;\n  const d = ww.debug();\n  const pl = window.__swGame.player;\n  return { steps: d.stepsLastFrame, active: d.activeTexels, queue: d.rippleQueueUsed,\n    wet: pl.inWater, wetCount: pl.wetCount, intensity: d.intensity, grid: `${d.gridW}x${d.gridH}` };\n});\n// ---- E) 视觉同帧对比:q3 波动 / q0 静止 ----\nawait setQ(3);\nconst a3 = await grabAtSameFrame();\nconst b3 = await grabAtSameFrame();\nconst a0 = await grabAtSameFrame();\nconst b0 = await grabAtSameFrame();\nfunction diffCount(A, B) {\n  if (!A || !B) return -1;\n  let n = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < A.length; i++) if (Math.abs(A[i] - B[i]) > 8) n++;\n  return n;\n}\nresults.visual = { q3diff: diffCount(a3, b3), q0diff: diffCount(a0, b0), samples: a3?.length ?? 0 };\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(results, null, 2));\n\n// 断言汇总\nconst pass = []\n  .concat(errors.length === 0 ? [] : ['pageerror'])\n  .concat(results.q0.steps === 0 && results.q0.active === 0 && !results.q0.disp ? [] : ['q0态'])\n  .concat(results.q1.steps === 0 && results.q1.disp > 0 ? [] : ['q1环境波'])\n  .concat(results.q3.steps >= 1 && results.q3.active > 0 ? [] : ['q3传播'])\n  .concat(results.visual.q3diff > 0 ? [] : ['q3视觉'])\n  .concat(results.visual.q0diff === 0 ? [] : ['q0视觉']);\nif (pass.length) console.log('PROBE FAIL:', pass.join(','), ' errors:', errors);\nelse console.log('PROBE PASS: 全绿 (q0 死/中性、q1 环境波、q3 涟漪传播+视觉波动、零 pageerror)');\nawait b.close();\nprocess.exit(pass.length ? 1 : 0);\n"
}

 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/scripts/_waveprobe.mjs",
 "old_string": "// 水线带采样:pool.y 格 ±8px 横带(世界→画布由相机换算,取画布中央 512px 宽)\nasync function grabStrip() {\n  return p.evaluate(() => {\n    const g = window.__swGame, c = g.renderer.canvas;\n    const ctx = c.getContext('2d');\n    // 相机中心=玩家 → 水线世界 y = (poolY+1)*16 附近;直接取玩家附近横带\n    const py = (g.player.y + g.player.h / 2);\n    const cy = Math.round((py - (g.renderer.ctx.getTransform ? 0 : 0)) * 1);  // 世界系\n    // 画布中心对应玩家 → 水线画布 y ≈ canvas.height/2 ± (水线−玩家中心)·zoom\n    const cam = g.camera;\n    const zoom = cam.zoom;\n    const screenY = (py - cam.y) * zoom + c.height / 2;\n    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.round(screenY) - 20);\n    const y1 = Math.min(c.height, Math.round(screenY) + 20);\n    const out = [];\n    for (let y = y0; y < y1; y += 2) {\n      for (let x = 0; x < c.width; x += 4) {\n        const d = ctx.getImageData(x, y, 1, 1).data;\n        out.push(d[0], d[1], d[2]);\n      }\n    }\n    return out;\n  });\n}\n// 等待与上次相同的动画帧(wind=0 → 6fps,周期 ~2.67s)\nasync function grabAtSameFrame() {\n  const f0 = await p.evaluate(() => window.__liq.liquidDebugState().animFrame);\n  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {\n    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 120));\n    const f1 = await p.evaluate(() => window.__liq.liquidDebugState().animFrame);\n    if (f1 === f0) {\n      return await grabStrip();\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nconst results = {};",
 "new_string": "// 水线带采样:玩家中心 ±20px 横带(画布系,相机中心=玩家)\nasync function grabStrip() {\n  return p.evaluate(() => {\n    const g = window.__swGame, c = g.renderer.canvas;\n    const ctx = c.getContext('2d');\n    const pl = g.player;\n    const cam = g.camera;\n    const zoom = cam.zoom;\n    const screenY = (pl.y + pl.h / 2 - cam.y) * zoom + c.height / 2;\n    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.round(screenY) - 20);\n    const y1 = Math.min(c.height, Math.round(screenY) + 20);\n    const out = [];\n    for (let y = y0; y < y1; y += 2) {\n      for (let x = 0; x < c.width; x += 4) {\n        const d = ctx.getImageData(x, y, 1, 1).data;\n        out.push(d[0], d[1], d[2]);\n      }\n    }\n    return out;\n  });\n}\n// 同帧抓取:wind=0 → animFrame 6fps(周期 2.667s)、waterfallFrame 0.5fps(周期 32s)\n// → 精确等 32000ms(两周期公倍)后帧号严格相同(setTimeout 抖动 ±5ms 远小于 166ms/2s 窗口)\nasync function grabAtSameFrame() {\n  const a = await grabStrip();\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 32000));\n  const b = await grabStrip();\n  return [a, b];\n}\n\nconst results = {};"
}

  sampleDisplace(worldX: number, worldY: number, out: [number, number]): void {
    out[0] = 0; out[1] = 0;
    if (this.quality === 0 || !_noiseData) return;
    const px = (worldX - this._camX) * this._zoom;
    const py = (worldY - this._camY) * this._zoom;
    const u = px / _noiseW, v = py / _noiseH;   // uv = 屏幕px/noise 尺寸（c0）

    const hClamp = Math.min(0.3, Math.max(-0.3, h - 0.5));

```
  ensureVImage(file: string): ImageBitmap | ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null {
    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
    if (hit) return hit;
    if (this._iconPending.has(file) || this._vImageFailed.has(file)) return null;
    if (this.bakeTracker?._baking) this.bakeTracker.note(file);
    this._iconPending.add(file);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => {
      // ImageBitmap 桥(2026-08-14 根治):入表即自持已解码像素;晚到/烘焙
      // 钩子在 bitmap 落地后触发(消费方读表内对象)
      const land = (store: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => {
        this.vimages.set(file, store);
        this._iconPending.delete(file);
        this._vImageFailed.delete(file);
        if (this.bakeTracker) this.bakeTracker.onLoaded(file);
        this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
      };
      if (!SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) land(im);
      else tryBitmapUpgrade(im, file, land, () => land(im));  // 失败回退+退避重试(重试成功二次 land=晚到重烘,安全)
    };
    im.onerror = () => {
      this._iconPending.delete(file);
      this._vImageFailed.add(file);   // 永久失败:不再重复请求,供消费方分级告警
    };
    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
    return null;
  }
  /** 表是否已确认加载失败(404 等)——与"加载中瞬态"区分 */
  vImageFailed(file: string): boolean { return this._vImageFailed.has(file); }
  private _vImageFailed = new Set<string>();

  // ---- 资产健康只读视图(F5 调试报告 assetHealth 段;运行期防线,2026-08-13) ----
  failedVImages(): string[] { return [...this._vImageFailed]; }
  failedUiImages(): string[] { return [...this._uiFailed]; }

```

function noiseAt(ch: number, u: number, v: number): number {
  if (!_noiseData) return 0.5;
  const w = _noiseW, h = _noiseH;
  const x0 = Math.floor(u), y0 = Math.floor(v);
  const fx = u - x0, fy = v - y0;
  const xa = ((x0 % w) + w) % w, ya = ((y0 % h) + h) % h;
  const x1 = (xa + 1) % w, y1 = (ya + 1) % h;
  const d = _noiseData;
  const i00 = (ya * w + xa) * 4 + ch, i10 = (ya * w + x1) * 4 + ch;
  const i01 = (y1 * w + xa) * 4 + ch, i11 = (y1 * w + x1) * 4 + ch;
  return ((d[i00] * (1 - fx) + d[i10] * fx) * (1 - fy)
    + (d[i01] * (1 - fx) + d[i11] * fx) * fy) / 255;
}

## 水面波动模拟(Water Waves)+ 浪质量设置 移植完成报告

### 原版机制考古(Terarria1456,全部带行号)

**设置与档位**(注意：原版是 **0-3 四档**而非三档,默认 3):
- `Main.cs:405` `WaveQuality = 3`;持久化 :4215/:4465,读档钳 [0,3](:4466-4473);UI 行 IngameOptions.cs:1040-1055,`(q+1)%4` 循环,文案 GameUI.WaveQuality + QualityOff/Low/Medium/High
- 档位门 WaterShaderData.cs:114-118:≥3=弹幕波+黏度、≥2=玩家/涟漪/自定义波、NPC 波恒开、0 档滤镜整体关(SceneState.cs:129)
- 步进节奏 Update :119-128 / PreDraw :336-360(每帧 ≤2 步,跳帧补 2)
- 强度链 SceneState.cs:159-173(风 1.25× + 雨 1.25× + 世界边缘项 + 地表/岩层/地狱深度项,钳 0-2.5),progress += dt·I·0.75 模 86400

**着色器数学(反汇编复原，这是本次考古的突破)**:C# 反编译不含 HLSL(编译在 fxb),但项目 `terraria-assets/` 里有解包的 XNA4 编译产物——用既有 `game/tools/disasm-fx.mjs`(染料系统那批做的)反汇编 `PixelShader.cso`/`ScreenShader.cso`,**逐指令**复原了三个 pass:
- **WaterProcessor**(obj49):速度 Verlet 波动方程 `H = S/2 − C + dv`、damp=exp2(−0.0551416)、G 通道 √ 压缩编码与 (G−0.5)·10/3 解码**互逆**(w·|w| ≡ dv)、unorm8+1/512 双重量化(量化是小速度的能量汇,也是低幅驻波长存的动力学根源——浮点缓冲会得“永生驻波",已修)
- **WaterDistortionObject**(obj50):实体洗掠(径向正弦负半波 × 流向点积 × 0.2 阈值)
- **FilterWaterDistortion**(obj19):消费端位移 `dy = (noiseGrad·0.1·I − clamp(h',±0.3))·32px`(涟漪只垂直推)、dx 只含噪声项、波光 tint、水体内部 20% lerp 权重
- ★两噪声采样初始只差 0.045 texel(同 2×2 块内)→ 环境波幅度靠 prog 漂移随时间拉开——原版 _progress 从游戏启动持续累积，到玩家见水时早已分离(探针踩到的真行为,非 bug)

**1456 死通道**:WAVE_MASK_STRENGTH = `new byte[5]` 全零(1405 为 {0,0,0,255,0})→ 黏度 B 通道在 1.4.5.6 已死,与现有 VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts:379 注释互证。按 1456 移植:不建模 B,仅保留注入侧 ×0.3 蜂蜜/岩浆补偿语义。

### 实现文件
| 文件 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| `game/src/render/WaterWaves.ts`(新,~640 行) | 完整系统:波场缓冲(0.25 屏幕缩放/DPR 无关)、传播内核(纯函数+内联零分配版)、涟漪队列(200 上限三重载)、实体注入采集(NPC/玩家/弹幕/血块,Screen 盒剔除)、噪声位移/波光采样、纹理提取 |
| `game/src/render/Renderer.ts` | `updateWaterWaves()` 帧驱动(render 顶部);实体采集 instanceof+projId 鸭子类型(弹幕两表 {7,8,150,151,152,493,494}/{511,512,513} 已核 ProjectileID.cs:195/:333) |
| `game/src/render/VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts` | 绘制侧:表层格水线随 dy 升降(底边锚定,源矩形同步裁剪,双 pass 确定性一致)+ 波光 'lighter' 提亮(仅前景 pass,阈值 0.03) |
| `game/src/core/Options.ts` | `waveQuality: number`(默认 3)+ 读档钳 [0,3] |
| `game/src/ui/Settings.ts` | 游戏分栏 modeRow 四档循环(用原版 GameUI.* 官译键,**无需新增 custom l10n 键**) |
| `game/src/entities/Player.ts` | `wetCount` 10t 出入水去抖(Player.cs:27464/27558/27651) |
| `game/tests/waterwaves.test.ts`(新) | 32 测试：传播内核金标、档位门、强度公式、洗掠形状、涟漪重载/清队语义、frame 步进/q0 死/q1 无源/掉帧补 2 步、注入门 |
| `game/scripts/_waveprobe.mjs`(新) | 浏览器探针(固定种子世界) |
